# CLAUDE.md — Polymarket Up/Down Trading Bot (Rust)

Single source of truth for this project. Read it fully before every task. If the official docs contradict this file, the docs win — update this file and note the change in the Decisions Log (§15) in the same task.

---

## 1. What we are building

A high-performance Rust trading bot for Polymarket short-duration crypto Up/Down markets:

- **Universe (initial):** BTC and ETH × 5-minute, 15-minute, 1-hour Up/Down series (6 series). The dashboard's per-series comparison later narrows to the best 2–3.
- **Two modes, one engine:** Paper and Live. Paper consumes **real live Polymarket data** — real books, trades, resolutions, fee math — only the money is simulated (operator-adjustable starting capital). The strategy engine is 100% identical between modes; only the execution venue adapter differs.
- **24/7 autonomy:** when a window resolves, the bot rolls to the next window of that series automatically, forever.
- **Strategy (full spec §8):** model-based two-sided quoting (market making) around a fair value from the underlying price feeds, with cancel-first repricing, inventory-skewed quotes, strict pair-cost discipline, and a fee-aware taker module for momentum/late-window opportunities. The core failure mode we engineer against is adverse selection: resting quotes picked off by faster traders when the underlying moves.
- **Dashboard:** served by the bot, showing both paper and live sessions, per-series comparison, live window view, fills, risk state, and controls (incl. paper capital adjustment).
- **Deployment:** Linux VPS close to Polymarket infra (region chosen via our latency benchmark). Development on the operator's PC — §3 build rules are mandatory.

The operator writes no code; you (Claude Code) implement everything, one focused prompt at a time from BUILD_PLAN.md.

---

## 2. Hard rules for every session

1. **Read before you write.** Before touching a Polymarket API, fetch and read the relevant docs page (map §13; index https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt). Never invent endpoint paths, field names, message shapes, or auth flows. If a field isn't confirmed by docs or a live API response you inspected, don't use it.
2. **One task, fully done.** Each prompt covers one module/function. Finish it to Definition of Done (§12) before touching anything else. Don't "improve" unrelated code in passing.
3. **Never break the workspace.** Every task ends with the whole workspace compiling, lints clean, all existing tests passing — not just the crate you touched.
4. **Paper-first, live-gated.** The bot must be incapable of sending a live order unless live mode is explicitly armed (config flag AND env confirmation AND dashboard arm action, §11). Default everything to paper. A safety invariant, not a feature.
5. **Identical engine paths.** Engine code must never know which venue it talks to. If you write "if paper / if live" inside engine logic, stop and refactor — mode differences live only in the venue adapters.
6. **Money is decimal, never float.** Prices, sizes, fees, balances use rust_decimal. Float is allowed only inside the math model (vol, normal CDF, fair value), converted at the boundary with explicit rounding.
7. **Verify against live reality.** Where docs are ambiguous (slugs, strike/price-to-beat source, field presence), write a small read-only probe against the live public API, inspect actual responses, implement, and record in the Decisions Log.
8. **Update this file.** Architectural decision, API fact learned, or parameter default changed → append to the Decisions Log (§15) in the same task.

---

## 3. Build performance (mandatory — a prior Rust project made the operator's PC unusable)

Configure so iteration is fast and the machine stays responsive; implement at scaffold time, never regress:

- **Many small workspace crates** (§4) — Rust recompiles at crate granularity. Engine logic must not live in one giant crate.
- **Dev profile:** workspace members unoptimized; **all external deps at opt-level 2 via a profile package override** (compiled once, cached, fast at runtime even in dev). Debug info line-tables-only in dev to shrink link times.
- **Fast linker:** mold on Linux (fallback lld) via cargo config; document the one-time install in README.
- **Cap parallelism:** default build jobs = (logical cores − 2) via cargo config so builds never starve the desktop; document VPS override.
- **Iteration:** iterate with cargo check / clippy via a watcher (bacon), not full builds; full release builds only for deployment. Encode as just recipes.
- **Dependency discipline:** TLS is rustls everywhere — never openssl. Keep the tree minimal. Allowlist (add others only with written justification in the Decisions Log): tokio, tokio-tungstenite, serde + serde_json, reqwest (rustls), rust_decimal, thiserror, anyhow, tracing + tracing-subscriber, axum + tower, rusqlite or sqlx-sqlite, figment or config, chrono or time, the official Polymarket Rust SDK and only its transitive deps, flate2 (pure-Rust `rust_backend`, journal segment gzip — operator-mandated, 2026-06-14). Forbidden: polars, full ethers, openssl-sys, any ORM, any heavyweight GUI toolkit.
- **Feature-gate heavy optional parts** (e.g., replay analytics) so the core bot builds lean.
- A fresh contributor following the README gets a working check loop in under 10 minutes.

---

## 4. Workspace architecture

Cargo workspace, one binary, small library crates. Names are suggestions — keep them short and consistent.

| Crate | Responsibility | Depends on |
|---|---|---|
| core-types | Domain types, internal event enums, ids, decimal newtypes, rounding helpers | — |
| config | Layered config (file defaults + env overrides), typed per-subsystem structs, secret handling | core-types |
| timeutil | Monotonic/wall-clock discipline, NTP offset check, τ-remaining helpers, latency harness | core-types |
| discovery | Gamma/CLOB REST market discovery for the 6 series; market metadata cache | core-types, config |
| scheduler | Per-series 24/7 window lifecycle state machine and rollover | core-types, discovery, config |
| feed-util | Shared feed plumbing: WS transport seam, generic reconnect/staleness driver, backoff, scripted fake transport (feature `fake`) | core-types |
| feed-rtds | Polymarket RTDS WebSocket (Chainlink + Binance price topics) | core-types, feed-util |
| feed-binance | Direct Binance WebSocket (lowest-latency signal) + feed-comparison diagnostics | core-types, feed-util |
| feed-clob | Polymarket market-channel WebSocket + local L2 book maintenance | core-types, feed-util |
| model | Vol estimator, fair-value engine, basis tracker, health states | core-types |
| venue-api | The execution port (trait): place/cancel/cancel-all/balances/event-stream | core-types |
| venue-live | Live adapter over the official Polymarket Rust SDK; user-channel WebSocket; order store reconcile | venue-api |
| venue-paper | Paper matching engine, paper ledger/wallet, fee+rebate simulation | venue-api, feed-clob types |
| engine | Inventory/pair accounting, quoting calculator, quote manager, taker modules, risk manager | model, venue-api |
| journal | Append-only event log (sqlite + raw tick files), replay reader | core-types |
| analytics | Markouts, PnL attribution, per-series aggregates | journal |
| dashboard | Axum REST + WebSocket push + static UI | analytics, engine state |
| bot (binary) | Orchestrator: wires everything, supervision, control plane, CLI subcommands | all |

Rules: dependency arrows point inward only (no crate depends on `bot`; `engine` depends only on `venue-api`, never `venue-live`/`venue-paper`). Long-running tasks communicate through typed channels carrying core-types events — no shared mutable state across async tasks except small lock-protected snapshots for the dashboard. In hot paths (tick → model → quote): no avoidable heap alloc, no locks held across await, no synchronous I/O.

---

## 5. Runtime topology (one process)

Long-running tasks: rtds feed, binance feed, one clob feed per active window pair, scheduler, model (per asset), engine (per series), venue adapter, journal writer, analytics ticker, dashboard server, control plane. The scheduler announces window lifecycle events; engines attach to windows; the risk manager sits between every engine and the venue — **no order reaches a venue without passing risk checks**. Graceful shutdown: stop strategies → cancel all open orders → flush journal → exit.

---

## 6. Trading universe & 24/7 rollover

- Series: BTC-5m, BTC-15m, BTC-1h, ETH-5m, ETH-15m, ETH-1h. Each independently enable/disable-able at runtime (dashboard + config). All six run concurrently in paper for the evaluation phase.
- Windows are pre-created by Polymarket on a fixed schedule. Discovery must always hold the current active window + ≥1 next window per series, fetched ≥60 s before the current one ends.
- The market WebSocket (custom feature flag, §7) emits `new_market` and `market_resolved` — primary rollover triggers, with wall clock + discovery refresh as fallback so a missed event can never stall a series.
- Per-series lifecycle: Discovering → Pending (subscribed, waiting for open) → Active (trading) → Closing (final-seconds §8) → Resolved (settle, merge/redeem, attribute PnL) → roll. A series with no discoverable next window logs loudly, retries with backoff, never crashes the process.
- Resolution: outcome is Up if the resolution-source end price is **≥** the start price ("price to beat"). Ties resolve Up. Source for 5m/15m = **Chainlink BTC/USD (resp. ETH/USD) data stream**; confirm the 1h series' rules via the API at discovery and store the resolution source per market rather than assuming.

---

## 7. Polymarket API facts (verified June 2026 — re-verify via docs when implementing)

**Hosts**
- CLOB REST: https://clob.polymarket.com
- Discovery (Gamma): https://gamma-api.polymarket.com (docs "Fetching Markets")
- Market WebSocket: wss://ws-subscriptions-clob.polymarket.com/ws/market
- User WebSocket (auth): wss://ws-subscriptions-clob.polymarket.com/ws/user
- Real-Time Data Socket (RTDS): wss://ws-live-data.polymarket.com
- Docs index: https://docs.polymarket.com/llms.txt

**Official Rust SDK — use it.** Docs show first-class Rust examples (polymarket-client-sdk-v2): order builders (limit + market), separate sign and post, batch posting, automatic fetch of tick size / neg-risk / fee rate inside the builder. Locate via docs Client Reference + crates.io; pin the version. Don't hand-roll EIP-712 signing or auth headers unless the SDK is missing something — if so, document why.

**Auth & wallets.** New API users → **deposit wallets** (docs "Deposit Wallets"; signature types: EOA=0, proxy=1, Gnosis Safe=2, deposit wallet=3). Live trading needs one-time token approvals for the exchange contracts (docs "Trading Quickstart" + "Deposit Wallets"). Collateral/balance denomination: read docs "Polymarket USD (PUSD)" during wallet/ledger work.

**Order types & behaviors**
- GTC, GTD (limit, rest on book) and FOK, FAK (marketable, execute immediately).
- **Post-only** (GTC/GTD): order rejected instead of matching if it would cross. Maker quotes use post-only so we never pay taker fees.
- GTD has a 60-second security threshold: for an effective lifetime of N s, set expiration = now + 60 + N.
- Marketable: BUY = dollar amount, SELL = share count; the price field is a worst-price slippage limit, not a target.
- Batch endpoint: up to 15 orders/request.
- Cancels: by order id, by market (cancel-market), and cancel-all (docs "Cancel Orders"). Wire cancel-all into every safety path.

**Market channel (public)** — subscribe with JSON: token ids under `assets_ids`, `type`="market", `custom_feature_enabled`=true (unlocks best_bid_ask, new_market, market_resolved). Client sends text `PING` ≥ every 10 s (we send 5 s); server replies `PONG`. A text frame is ONE event object OR a JSON ARRAY of them (arrays seen on multi-token subscribe). Events tagged by `event_type`:
- book — full snapshot on subscribe and whenever a trade affects the book; arrays of {price, size} for bids/asks; has asset_id, market (condition id), timestamp, hash (opaque). **Trades reach books ONLY through these snapshots** (~1.2/s per token on an active BTC-5m pair); price_change never carries trade effects.
- price_change — order placed/cancelled; list of per-asset changes with price, size (new TOTAL at that level), side, current best_bid/best_ask; size "0" = level removed. One order is MIRRORED as two entries (BUY Up @ p ↔ SELL Down @ 1−p, same size). **A trade at the touch arrives as a crossing change** (a bid AT the best ask) carrying post-trade tops — the consumed opposite level's removal is never sent as a delta; resolve locally by implied consumption (drop opposite levels at-or-better than the new price), confirmed by the next snapshot (naive crossed-book-recycle loops on every such trade).
- tick_size_change — fired when price crosses 0.96/0.04 and tick flips between 0.01 and 0.001; book and quoting must handle live. (Fixture synthesized from docs — not yet captured live.)
- last_trade_price — trade prints (paper fill sim + markouts); can arrive after the trade's book snapshot (ordering not guaranteed).
- best_bid_ask, new_market, market_resolved — via the custom flag. new_market is a PLATFORM-WIDE broadcast (every new market, any category — dedup + treat as refresh hint). market_resolved arrives on connections subscribed to that market's tokens; condition id in `market` (no condition_id/slug field despite docs), winner in `winning_asset_id`. A closed window's connection must linger ~20–60 s after close to receive it.
- Load: one active 5m pair ≈ up to ~800 msg/s peak; 12 tokens on one connection floods ~1500 msg/s and the server RSTs it — one connection per window pair (§5).

**User channel (auth)** — /ws/user; API credentials inside the subscription message; streams your order updates and trades filtered by API key. Read the full docs page for auth fields/event schemas; reconcile with REST open-orders on every reconnect.

**RTDS (no auth, critical)** — send PING every 5 s; subscriptions are JSON {action subscribe/unsubscribe, list of {topic, type, filters}}; messages arrive as {topic, type, timestamp, payload}.
- Topic crypto_prices = **Binance source**; symbols lowercase concat (btcusdt, ethusdt). Topic crypto_prices_chainlink = **Chainlink source — the resolution feed**; symbols slash format (btc/usd, eth/usd); subscribe with type "*".
- **One filter slot per (connection, topic), replace-not-add** (regression probes `crates/feed-rtds/tests/live_probe.rs`): a later filtered subscribe replaces the earlier filter; no multi-symbol filter form works (comma lists and JSON arrays acked then silent, incl. the docs' comma-list). Multi-symbol = the all-symbols form (crypto_prices: omit `filters`; chainlink: `"filters":""` with type "*"), ~1 update/s/symbol (~13 msg/s total), filtered client-side.
- A **filtered** subscribe (JSON-encoded-string filter `"{\"symbol\":\"btcusdt\"}"` — the only form honored) triggers a ~2-min **backfill** (`payload {symbol, data:[{timestamp, value}…]}`, type "subscribe"); all-symbols form does not. Chainlink backfills arrive under the WRONG topic (crypto_prices) — resolve stream identity from the symbol, never the topic.
- Live updates carry `full_accuracy_value` (lossless string: Binance plain decimal, Chainlink 18-dp fixed-point integer = value×10¹⁸) and `connection_id`. Subscribe ack = empty text frame; no text "PONG" from RTDS. Server can answer a subscribe with an error frame `{body:{message}, statusCode:500}` — observed leaving a symbol's stream dead a whole session; feed-rtds self-heals by recycling the connection when a tracked stream starves.
- Subscriptions can be modified without reconnecting.
- Chainlink offers a sponsored direct Data Streams API key for 15-minute-market traders (request form on the RTDS docs page) — operator to apply later; design feed-rtds so a direct Chainlink source slots in.

**Fees (exact, docs "Fees")**
- fee = shares × feeRate × p × (1 − p), charged to **takers only**, in dollars, at match time by the protocol (nothing in orders). Makers pay zero.
- **Crypto feeRate = 0.07, maker rebate share = 20%.** Per 100 shares: $1.75 @ 0.50, $1.31 @ 0.25/0.75, $0.63 @ 0.10/0.90, $0.07 @ 0.01/0.99. Symmetric around 0.50.
- Fees rounded to 5 decimals; minimum charged 0.00001.
- Per-market fee params are queryable (SDK market-info returns a fee descriptor with rate/exponent/taker-only) and feesEnabled is on the market object — **always read fee params per market at discovery; never hardcode 0.07** in logic (only as a config default).
- Maker Rebates Program: 20% (crypto) of collected taker fees, redistributed **daily in USDC**, proportional to executed maker volume, $1 min accrual (docs "Maker Rebates Program"). A tiered **Taker Rebate Program** exists (docs "Taker Rebates") — fold into taker edge math if material. A **Liquidity Rewards** program exists for resting two-sided quotes — optional income, read during the quoting task.

**Order size & price constraints (operator-confirmed + enforce defensively)**
- Minimum resting order: **5 shares AND $1 notional** — min shares = max(5, ceil_to_size_precision(1.00 / price)), re-validate after tick rounding. At 0.10, $1 binds (10 sh); at 0.50, 5 sh bind ($2.50). Also read minimum_order_size and minimum_tick_size from the market object; take the strictest. Every outgoing order passes the normalizer (§engine) — a rejected-for-size order in production is a bug.
- Tick sizes: 0.1 / 0.01 / 0.001 / 0.0001; our markets 0.01 flipping to 0.001 past 0.96/0.04. Prices land on the current tick grid; valid range [tick, 1 − tick].

**Other pages to read when relevant:** "Matching Engine Restarts" (assume orders may be gone, reconcile + re-quote), "CTF: Merge"/"CTF: Redeem" (matched Up+Down pairs merge to $1 collateral — our capital recycling; redeem after resolution), "Gasless Transactions" (relayer for on-chain ops), "Resolution", "Order Lifecycle", "Resources → Contracts", "Resources → Error Codes" (map error codes to typed errors), "Builders" (optional order attribution).

---

## 8. Strategy specification (the engine's brain)

**Fair value.** For window strike K (the "price to beat" captured at window start from the resolution-grade feed), current price S, τ seconds remaining: z = ln(S/K) / (σ_1s × √τ), p_up = Φ(z) (standard normal CDF; drift negligible at these horizons; apply the ≥-ties-go-Up rule as a tiny adjustment only if measurement shows it matters). σ_1s is an EWMA of 1-second log returns (half-life default 60 s) with a floor and cap. Chainlink (via RTDS) is ground truth; direct Binance is the fast signal, corrected by a continuously-estimated Chainlink-minus-Binance basis. The model exposes a health state; when inputs are stale/diverging it declares UNRELIABLE and the engine pulls quotes.

**Quoting.** Desired quotes = pure function of (model state, inventory, fee params, config, τ, tick): center = p_up − γ × normalized inventory imbalance; half-spread = max(configured min edge, k1 × σ_1s × √(expected holding time) + a latency premium from the measured cancel round-trip). Two-sided, post-only, small size at the touch with a ladder behind. Gates: no quoting while model UNRELIABLE; widen on vol spikes; **no at-the-money quoting in the final 20–30 s; no passive orders at all in the final ~5 s (cancel-all)**; respect per-window inventory caps.

**Cancel-first repricing.** On any signal tick that moves p_up against a resting quote beyond threshold θ, cancel first (cancel-market on large moves), then re-quote. This is the primary defense against the adverse-selection failure mode — treat its latency as a first-class metric.

**Inventory & pair discipline.** Track per window: shares and cost per side, matched pairs = min(Q_up, Q_down), pair cost = avg_up + avg_down, signed excess, worst-case-if-excess-loses dollars. Only add to a side passively if post-trade pair cost stays ≤ the configured threshold (default 0.98). Soft cap on excess → quote only the deficit-reducing side; hard cap → stop making, optionally work excess off at/above fair. Merge matched pairs back to collateral (live: CTF merge via relayer; paper: simulated instantly-credited merge) to recycle capital across windows.

**Taker modules (fee-aware).** (a) Momentum/staleness: when a confirmed signal-feed move implies the book is stale, take with FAK only if model-vs-book edge exceeds fee(p) + configured buffer (at p≈0.5 the crypto fee ≈1.75¢/share, so mid-window takes need real edge). (b) Late-window: when τ is below a threshold and p_up is beyond a certainty threshold on the Chainlink-anchored price, buy the near-certain side up to a price cap, sized against displayed depth (fees near zero there). Per-window taker budgets and cooldowns apply. Risk manager can veto everything.

**Per-window risk bound.** The configured max worst-case loss per window is the binding constraint on all sizing.

---

## 9. Paper trading specification

- Same engine, risk manager, order normalizer; only the venue adapter differs.
- **Fill simulation (conservative by design):** a passive paper order is queued behind ALL displayed size at its level at placement (pessimistic queue position); fills only as real last_trade_price prints occur at or through its price, after a simulated placement latency; cancels take effect after a simulated cancel round-trip. Marketable paper orders walk the real displayed book after the simulated latency with partial fills per available depth. Latencies are configurable distributions (defaults: operator-measured RTTs from the latency harness). Paper must never be more optimistic than reality in any code path — when in doubt, fill less.
- **Money:** paper wallet starting capital from config, adjustable at runtime from the dashboard (changes journaled). Taker fees charged using live per-market fee params and the exact formula. Maker rebates accrued as an estimate (rebate share × fee formula on filled maker volume), credited on a simulated daily cycle, reported separately from trading PnL.
- **Settlement:** positions settle on the real market_resolved outcome. Simulated merge converts matched pairs to cash instantly on engine request.
- Paper and live ledgers share one schema; every event is journaled identically so analytics and dashboard treat both modes uniformly.

---

## 10. Dashboard specification

Served by the bot (axum), token-protected, REST snapshots + WebSocket push, single-page static UI with minimal deps, phone-usable. Views:

1. **Overview:** mode badges (paper running / live armed-or-off), per-mode equity curves, paper capital editor, global kill button.
2. **Series comparison (the decision table):** one row per series with windows traded, % profitable, net PnL, PnL/window, locked-pair vs inventory PnL split, fees paid, rebates earned, average 5-second markout on passive fills, fill counts, min-sample warning. Sortable. This is how the operator picks the best 2–3 series — make it excellent.
3. **Live window view:** per active window — book ladder with our quotes highlighted, model fair vs book mid, countdown, inventory, pair cost, recent prints.
4. **Fills blotter:** chronological fills with markout coloring and attribution tags.
5. **Risk panel:** every breaker's state, feed health, WS connectivity, last cancel-all reason.
6. **Controls:** enable/disable per series, safe-listed live-tunable params, paper capital, live arming flow (multi-step, §11).

---

## 11. Risk rules (hard-wired)

All order flow passes through the risk manager. Non-negotiable: cancel-all on market-WS or user-WS disconnect, on any feed staleness beyond 500 ms, on detected matching-engine restart, and on clock-skew alarm. Per-window max loss; daily stop-loss halting all trading until manual reset; global max open notional; stand-down (pull quotes) whenever |model fair − book mid| exceeds a sanity bound for more than a few seconds; error-rate circuit breaker on API failures; manual kill from dashboard and CLI. **Live arming:** live orders are possible only when config enables live mode AND a specific env var contains a required confirmation phrase AND the operator has armed live mode in the dashboard for the session; absent any one, the live adapter must refuse to construct.

---

## 12. Coding standards & Definition of Done

Stable Rust. rustfmt enforced; clippy with warnings denied. No unwrap/expect/panic on runtime paths — typed errors (thiserror) inside crates, anyhow only at the binary boundary. Structured logging via tracing with per-subsystem targets; every order intent, placement, cancel, fill, and breaker event logged with ids and monotonic + wall timestamps. Async: tokio; no blocking calls on the runtime; channel back-pressure handled explicitly.

Done only when: (1) the whole workspace passes fmt, clippy-deny-warnings, all tests; (2) the new module has unit tests + where applicable fixture-driven integration tests (recorded real API messages as fixtures — capture and commit them); (3) public items have doc comments; (4) README/justfile updated if commands changed; (5) the Decisions Log records anything learned; (6) you state how the module integrates with its neighbors and demonstrate it (a runnable subcommand, test, or wired path in the bot binary).

## 14. Glossary

Window = one tradable interval (e.g., BTC Up/Down 11:55–12:00). Series = the repeating product (BTC-5m). Strike / price-to-beat = resolution-source price at window start. Pair cost = combined average cost of one Up + one Down share. Excess = unmatched shares on one side. Markout = signed fair-value move a short interval after a fill; persistently negative markouts on passive fills = we are being adversely selected. Edge = model fair minus executable price, after fees. Merge = converting matched pairs back into $1 collateral. Armed = live trading explicitly enabled per §11.

## 15. Decisions Log

(Claude Code: append dated entries here — API facts verified, deviations from this file, parameter defaults chosen, dependency additions with justification.)

- 2026-06-11 — **Project initialized.** Verified from docs: Rust SDK exists (`polymarket-client-sdk-v2`), RTDS carries Binance+Chainlink crypto prices unauthenticated, crypto taker feeRate 0.07 / 20% maker rebate, market-channel `custom_feature_enabled` unlocks new_market/market_resolved, GTD +60s threshold, batch limit 15, post-only supported, tick flips to 0.001 beyond 0.96/0.04.

- 2026-06-11 — **Workspace scaffolded:** 16 library crates + `bot` binary per §4. §3: `[profile.dev] debug="line-tables-only"`, `[profile.dev.package."*"] opt-level=2`, mold linker scoped to `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` in `.cargo/config.toml` (clang+mold; lld fallback), `jobs=10` (12 cores−2; VPS override `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS`). Edition 2024, rust-version 1.94. Lints via `[workspace.lints]`: clippy all=warn, unwrap/expect/panic/todo/dbg=warn (denied by `just lint -D warnings`; tests exempted in clippy.toml), `missing_docs`=warn, `unsafe_code`=deny; `clippy::print_stdout` allowed (banner). Tooling: justfile (windows-shell=powershell), bacon.toml, rustfmt.toml (style_edition 2024, max_width 100, Unix newlines). Note: crates `config`/`model` shadow crates.io packages — rename via `package=` if external chosen.

- 2026-06-11 — **`core-types` implemented** (13 modules). Deps: rust_decimal 1.42 (std/serde/macros — Decimal as lossless string, `dec!`), serde 1.0.228 (derive), thiserror 2.0.18; serde_json 1.0.150 dev-only. Decisions: (1) timestamps = `TimestampMs(i64)` unix-millis + signed `DurationMs`, no chrono/time in foundation. (2) `Price` does NOT embed TickSize — invariant strictly-inside-(0,1)∧scale≤4; grid via `on_grid`/`quantize`/`from_f64`, re-checked by engine normalizer vs `MarketInfo.tick_size`. (3) `Price::from_f64` uses `Decimal::from_f64` not `from_f64_retain` (retain: 0.29→0.2899…→0.28 silent one-tick error); clamps 0.0/1.0 to [tick,1−tick], errors non-finite/outside-[0,1]. (4) `quantize` clamping can override rounding at grid floor/ceiling. (5) Shared `taker_fee(size,rate,price)`=shares×rate×p×(1−p), 5-dp half-away, $0.00001 floor; verified vs §7. (6) `Event` bus enum Clone-cheap; guard `size_of::<Event>()≤96` (measured 88); NOT serde, NOT non_exhaustive. (7) Ids validate on construction+deserialize (`serde(try_from="String")`): ConditionId=0x+64-hex lowercased, TokenId=decimal string (256-bit). (8) `InventorySnapshot` derived fields via `derive()`. (9) `OrderState::can_transition_to` allows PendingCancel→Filled. (10) Added `BreakerKind::EngineRestart`. Deferred: NonZeroSize, UnderlyingPrice newtype, ids as Arc<str>.

- 2026-06-12 — **feed-clob design decisions:** (1) **Venue `book` snapshots are ground truth, replace local state wholesale**; delta-maintained book audited against every snapshot (~1% drift, repaired by replace, never a recycle). (2) **Crossing deltas are trades at the touch, resolved by implied consumption** (`BookState::apply_change` drops opposite-side levels at-or-better than the new price; `ChangeOutcome::Applied{consumed}`); `BookUnreliableReason::Crossed` = a venue snapshot itself arrived crossed (latch only, no recycle). (3) Connection recycling reserved for what snapshots can't fix: derived-top vs venue-top divergence past 2 s grace, active-window book silence past `clob_book_stale_after_ms` (10 s; quiet-market adaptive: content-identical stale recycle doubles effective threshold, capped 6×, reset on first genuine delta; pre-open/post-close exempt), dead socket at 3× ping. (4) Bus: `TopOfBook` immediately on real top change, `LastTrade`/`TickSizeChange` immediate, full `Event::Book` immediate on snapshots + coalesced to `clob_book_publish_interval_ms` (200 ms) for delta-built books (`seq_hash: None`; only snapshots carry the hash). (5) New `Event::BookHealth` (per-window, latched Unreliable{Disconnected|Stale|Crossed|TopDivergence}/Recovered, every Recovered paired with one Unreliable) — feeds report, risk reacts (§11). (6) Wire parser lenient: string-or-number numerics, single-object OR array frames, `"0"`/`""`/absent tops → None ((None,None) = "no report"), per-event failures in an array don't poison siblings. (7) Five `[feeds]` keys: `clob_ping_interval_ms` 5000, `clob_book_stale_after_ms` 10000, `clob_presubscribe_lead_ms` 90000, `clob_book_publish_interval_ms` 200, `clob_resolution_linger_ms` 180000.

Capture findings folded into §7 (PING/PONG, array frames, trades-only-via-snapshots, crossing-delta semantics, mirror pattern, new_market platform-wide broadcast, market_resolved field shape, per-pair load).

- 2026-06-13 — **`model::vol` implemented** (first model module; deps rust_decimal + thiserror) + `bot vol` smoke. Per-asset, per-(source,kind) EWMA variance of 1-second log returns; sans-IO. Decisions: (1) **Bars keyed by `floor(ts_exchange/1000)`**; RTDS backfill seeds warmup instantly; older-than-open-bar ticks dropped (`OutOfOrder`, also discards reconnect-backfill overlap, no double-fold); glitch guard rejects ts_exchange > ts_local + 10 s. (2) **Bias-corrected EWMA** (`acc`/`wsum`, var = acc/wsum). (3) **Gap-aware fold** `var ← λᵏ·var + (1−λᵏ)·(r²/k)`, λ = 2^(−1/half_life)/s; gaps > `max_gap_secs` (30) re-anchor WITHOUT folding, freeze acc/wsum/count. (4) Warmup = `warmup_returns` folded samples (60). Quality {NotReady, Ready}. (5) Engine path `sigma() -> Option<f64>` — `Some(clamped σ)` ONLY when Ready; `estimate()` exposes warming sigma + `Clamp {Inactive, Floor, Cap}`. (6) Config: half_life/floor/cap map from `engine.defaults` at the boundary (`bot/src/vol.rs::vol_params`); source/kind + warmup_returns/max_gap_secs are `VolParams` defaults (BinanceDirect/Mid) — config exposure + per-series→per-asset resolution deferred to model-driver task. Live-verified (σ ≈ 0.36–0.6 bps/√s, READY at fold 60).

- 2026-06-13 — **Fair-value engine implemented** (`model::{math, strike, basis, fair}`) + `bot fair` subcommand + `discovery::event_by_slug` verify seam. §8 brain: `p_up = Φ(z)`, `z = ln(S/K)/(σ_1s·√τ)`. **Strike source verified live (§2.7 resolved):** Gamma objects carry **no live strike** during a window; venue publishes `eventMetadata.priceToBeat`/`finalPrice` only **~1–4 min after resolution** (absent 51 s post-close); chain continuity exact — `priceToBeat(N) = finalPrice(N−1) = closePrice(N−1)`. ⇒ **runtime self-captures strike from RTDS Chainlink ticks; post-hoc `priceToBeat` is the verification anchor.** Decisions: (1) **Φ via Cody's CALERF** rational erf/erfc (~1e-16; `Φ(z)=½·erfc(−z/√2)`); hand-rolled, no new dep. (2) **`ModelSnapshot` extended** `z`/`sigma_tau`/`anchor: AnchorSource{Chainlink,BinanceCorrected}`; `basis` redefined to bps; `z` clamped ±39. **Event size guard 96→128** (measured 104, still Copy). (3) **Strike capture settle-gated, arrival-order-independent** (`model::strike`): two monotonically-refined candidates (`before`/`after`), freeze only once a live at-or-after-open print proves wall-clock passed open; default rule `LastAtOrBefore`; post-freeze improvements emit `RevisedCandidate` (never move K); `for_market` picks feed/rule from `ResolutionKind` (Chainlink tested; Binance-candle 1h `FirstAtOrAfter` provisional; Other errors). (4) **Basis tracker** (`model::basis`): bias-corrected EWMA of log basis `b = ln(P_cl/P_binance_mid)`, HL 120 s, warmup 30, decay-clamp 30 s; **backfill guard rejects RTDS backfill** (only live Chainlink ticks fold — opposite of strike). (5) **Anchor + health** (`model::fair`): anchor = basis-corrected Binance when fast feed fresh AND basis warmed, else fresh Chainlink, else none; Warming/Unreliable(Chainlink age > `feeds.rtds_stale_after_ms` OR divergence > 50 bps ≥ 10 s)/Healthy; τ=0 saturates to ≥-ties-Up; per-asset state (vol, basis) lives OUTSIDE per-window engine. (6) Config: zero new keys; `feeds.{rtds,binance}_stale_after_ms` map into `FairParams`/`BasisParams`; divergence/basis-HL/heartbeat model consts. (7) `bot fair` wires scheduler+feed-clob+rtds+binance on one bus; recomputes per tick + 100 ms heartbeat; publishes `Event::Model` via `try_send`; calibration JSONL → `data/calibration/fair-{series}-{stamp}.jsonl`; post-hoc verify polls `event_by_slug` (8×30 s), reconciles `priceToBeat` (exact/mismatch/unavailable, rel-tol 1e-9). Deps added to `bot`: `serde`, `rust_decimal`. **Calibration finding:** mean |p_up − mid| ≈ 0.086, excursions to 0.163 — p_up sharply sensitive near-the-money, fast anchor leads book ~2.7 s by design.

- 2026-06-13 — **Composite model-health gate implemented** (`model::health` — sans-IO `HealthMonitor`) + reworked `model::fair` + core-types health vocab + `bot fair` wiring. Decisions: (1) **`core_types::ModelHealth` renamed `Warming/Healthy/Unreliable` → `Ready/Degraded/Unreliable`** (operator-named) with `allows_quoting()` (Ready), `requires_pull()` (Unreliable), `is_degraded()`; derives `Ord` (Ready < Degraded < Unreliable). Warming folded into Unreliable (operator left to me), cause in Copy `ModelHealthReason{Nominal,Warming,ChainlinkStale,NoAnchor,DivergenceHard,FastLeadLost,DivergenceSoft}` + `tier()`; `ModelSnapshot` gained `reason`. (2) **DEGRADED = fast-lead-lost ∨ sustained soft divergence** (operator left to me; anchor Chainlink-only → stop new quotes without force-cancelling; changes prior "Chainlink-only = Healthy"). UNRELIABLE = Chainlink stale ∨ no anchor ∨ sustained hard divergence ∨ not-warmed. (3) **`HealthMonitor` sans-IO, per-asset**, in `bot::fair::FairState` beside vol/basis (survives rollover); mirrors `timeutil::SkewMonitor`. (4) **Hysteresis = dual magnitude bounds + dwell timers, worsen-fast/ease-slow** (`HealthParams` consts): Chainlink stale ≥ 5000 ms / clear ≤ 3000 ms (immediate); divergence SOFT 30 bps (clear 20) → DEGRADED, HARD 50 bps (clear 40) → UNRELIABLE, each gated by 10 s confidence dwell (filters transient ~80 bps Binance-lead, collapses ~3 s); fast-lead-lost gated by 2 s dwell; every recovery honours 3 s exit dwell (`recovering_since`); stale/no-anchor bypass exit dwell. Emits `HealthTransition` only on gate-tier change (latched). (5) **Retired `divergence_since` latch** in `FairValueEngine`; dropped `FairParams.divergence_bps`/`divergence_persist_ms`; engine only measures `divergence_bps`, monitor owns persistence; `FairValue.health`/`reason` caller-stamped (driver runs monitor on `FairValue::health_inputs()`). (6) **Latched `Event::ModelHealth(ModelHealthEvent{asset,health,reason,ts})`** (~16 B, widest variant stays `Model(ModelSnapshot)` ≤ 128); driver buffers in `FairState.pending_health`, publishes from `on_heartbeat` via `try_send`, re-queues on full channel. (7) Config zero new keys; `health_params(feeds)` maps `feeds.rtds_stale_after_ms` → `chainlink_stale_ms`, derives `chainlink_fresh_ms = stale·3/5`; other knobs `HealthParams` consts. (8) `bot fair` rendering `[{health}/{reason} via {anchor}]` + `MODEL HEALTH` transition line; calibration `Snapshot` gained `reason`, boxed into `SnapshotRecord` behind `CalRecord::Snapshot(Box<…>)` (internally-tagged serde still flat).

- 2026-06-13 — **Order normalizer implemented** (`engine::normalize`, first `engine` module, pure/sans-IO) — §7/§8 chokepoint every outgoing intent passes before a venue. `engine` deps `core-types`/`rust_decimal`/`thiserror` (`model` does NOT re-export `core_types`). **Shape:** input `OrderDraft` (raw `price: Decimal` → testable reject); `normalize(draft, market, book: Option<&TopOfBook>, params) -> Result<Normalized, NormalizeReject>`, `Normalized{order:NewOrder, adjustments:Adjustments}`, `Adjustments{price:PriceSnap, size_rounded_down, size_bumped_to_min}`. Step order: qty-kind gate → price snap → size/notional → post-only cross check (last, fully-sized `WouldCross`) → assemble. Min-size floor `max(5 shares, ceil_to_size_precision($1/rounded_price), market.min_order_size)`; on `Price::quantize`+`RoundDir::passive_for`+`Size::round_down_dp`; fallible ops `?`-mapped. **Decisions:** (1) **size precision in `NormalizerParams{size_decimals:u32}`** (default 0), mapped at binary boundary; `MarketInfo` has no size-precision field (verify vs live ack). (2) **Marketable price rounding permissive** (rounds outward: BUY→Up, SELL→Down via `passive_for(side.opposite())`); resting keeps passive rounding. (3) **Sizing never silently shrinks** — positive sub-minimum bumped to floor + flag `size_bumped_to_min`; literal zero → `ZeroSize`; marketable-BUY notional `<$1` → `BelowMinNotional`. (4) **Engine-local `NormalizeReject`** (PriceOutOfDomain/ZeroSize/QtyKindMismatch/BelowMinNotional/WouldCross) + `as_reject_reason()` bridging to `venue_api::RejectReason`. (5) `Price::quantize` only returns `OutOfDomain`; others mapped defensively. Cases: 0.10→10sh, 0.50→5sh, 0.001-grid near 0.99/0.01, tick-flip 0.961, notional-just-below-$1→bump, post-only would-cross. **Integration:** `Normalized.order: NewOrder` == `venue-live::convert::build` input. **Deferred:** wiring `normalize()` into risk-manager→`VenuePort` + `NormalizerParams` from config.

Store + wire parser sans-IO. **`OrderStore` idempotency:** (1) **state + cumulative `filled_size`** from `order` events + reconcile, monotone-max + terminal latch + `can_transition_to`. (2) **discrete `Fill`s** from `trade` events deduped by trade id (FIFO `seen_trades` cap 50 000); `Retrying`/`Failed` emit nothing and DON'T consume the id; role taker iff `taker_order_id` ours, maker per `maker_orders` leg → `Liquidity`+fee (`Dollars::ZERO` maker / `taker_fee` taker); fills use tracked order's limit price (marketable untracked); qty clamped to `original_size`. (3) **Missed fills on reconnect → synthetic maker `Fill{trade_id:None,Maker,fee 0}` + `OrderUpdate`** for `size_matched − emitted`, `synthetic_covered` suppresses later real `trade`; `LiveParams.emit_synthetic_fills` (default true). **`StoreEffect{Order,Fill}` `pub(crate)`** → `VenueEvent::{Order,Fill}`. **Attribution seam:** `WindowIndex` (token → `WindowCtx{window,outcome,fee_rate,tick}`) + enriched `RawOpenOrder` (`token_id/side/price/condition_id`); `sdk::open_orders` maps SDK `B256` market → `0x`+64-hex condition id, `U256` asset → bare-decimal token id (re-verify R2/R3). **Orphan adoption** implemented (REST-poll survivor adopted when index resolves, price `Price::on_grid` fail-closed) **inert until orchestrator wires `WindowIndex` via `LiveVenue::set_window_index`**. `fee_rate` from index else `LiveParams.default_taker_fee_rate` (0.07). **Markets** via `LiveVenue::set_markets` → `watch::Receiver<Vec<ConditionId>>`; change reconnects. **Creds** `UserWsCreds::from_secrets` (redacting Debug, never serialized). `connect` spawns `user_ws::run`; `LiveVenue` holds `markets_tx`/`shutdown_tx`. **Reconcile** on every (re)connect (before streaming) + `safety_reconcile_interval` tick. **`LiveParams` gained** `user_ws_url`, `ws_ping_interval` (5 s), `ws_dead_after` (3× ping), `ws_connect_timeout` (10 s), `ws_backoff` ({250 ms,10 s,2.0}), `safety_reconcile_interval` (30 s), `subscribe_all_when_empty` (true), `emit_synthetic_fills` (true), `default_taker_fee_rate` (0.07); **dropped `Eq`** (f64 multiplier). **Fixtures** synthesized from docs + SDK 0.5.1 `clob::ws` types. **Live-verify:** R1 markets-empty; R2/R3 SDK order-row field names/id formats + maker/taker provenance; R8 rustls provider (SDK `aws-lc-rs` + feed-util `ring`). **Deferred:** scheduler→`set_window_index`/`set_markets`; `forget_window` pruning.

- 2026-06-13 — **`venue-paper` fill simulator implemented** (`params`/`latency`/`book`/`wallet`/`engine`/`driver`) + `bot paper-sim` smoke + `bot::paper::paper_params` mapping — §9 paper venue: real Polymarket data, paper money, **never more optimistic than reality**. Mirrors `venue-live`: sans-IO `MatchEngine` (clock-injected mutators return `Vec<PaperEffect{Order,Fill}>`), wrapped by `Arc<Mutex<…>>` `PaperVenue` (std Mutex never across `await`) + one spawned **timer task** driving latency deadlines via `on_clock`, woken by `Notify`. Data in via `PaperVenue::on_bus_event(&Event)`; order/fill stream same `VenueEvent` channel as live. **No arming gate.** **Dependency decision (§2.8, diverges from §4):** dropped `feed-clob` dep — paper consumes only `core-types` (`Event`/`BookSnapshot`/`BookLevel`/`LastTrade`/`TopOfBook`/`MarketInfo`/`taker_fee`). Deps: core-types, venue-api, tokio (rt/sync/time/macros), tracing, rust_decimal, thiserror. **Conservative fill rules:** (1) **placement latency** — `place()` registers `PendingNew` (`activate_at = now + sample(placement)`), returns `Ok(Accepted{PendingNew})`, outcome async once latency elapses; only structurally-invalid (qty kind ⊥ TIF/side) rejects synchronously; post-only cross judged at activation vs activation-time book. (2) **queue behind ALL displayed size** at activation (`SimBook::displayed_at`), only drains. (3) **resting fills** only from prints whose aggressor is OPPOSITE side, at-or-through our price; print size a finite shared budget in priority (best price, then `activate_at`/`seq`); Maker fee 0 at our limit; never gated on whether snapshot reflected the trade (§7). (4) **cancel race** — `cancel()` sets `cancel_effect_at = now + sample(cancel)`, returns `canceled:[id]` ack but keeps order fillable, no `PendingCancel`; prints in `[request,effect)` fill the stale quote (§9), then `on_clock` cancels remainder. (5) **marketable (FOK/FAK)** walk real depth respecting worst-price limit (BUY by $ vs asks, SELL by shares vs bids), Taker `taker_fee(per-market rate)`, deplete local book copy. FOK→`Rejected{FokUnfilled}`; FAK partial→`Canceled`, zero→`Rejected{FakNoMatch}`. (6) post-only-would-cross→`Rejected{CrossedBook}`. (7) window `Closed`/`Resolved`→`Canceled`; GTD expiry→`Expired` (floored `now+60s`). Ids `paper-{n}`/`paper-t{n}`. Time: `now` drives deadlines, print **price** drives eligibility, `Fill.ts_venue`=print ts. **Latency** uniform `[mean, mean+jitter]` one-sided-up via inlined xorshift64* seeded `PaperParams.rng_seed`. **Wallet** fill-driven ledger (collateral cash-flow + signed net positions + maker-rebate-estimate accumulator); `set_capital` seam. **Deferred to paper-ledger task:** settlement on real `market_resolved`, instant simulated pair-merge, daily maker-rebate credit cycle, open-order collateral reservation (`available==total` v1), negative-net reporting. **`PaperParams`** plain (no `config` dep); `paper_params` maps `config.paper`; zero new config keys. `bot paper-sim <series>` (default BTC-5m) wires scheduler+feed-clob → `PaperVenue` → trivial two-sided post-only quoter re-quoting every 5 s. Headline tests `a_print_fills_the_stale_quote_before_the_cancel_lands`, `simultaneous_takers_cannot_double_eat_displayed_depth`.

- 2026-06-14 — **Paper ledger implemented** (`venue-paper`: `wallet`/`engine`/`driver`) — settlement, instant pair-merge, daily maker-rebate credit cycle, runtime capital set/adjust, separate fee/rebate income lines. New `PaperWallet`: `fees_paid` (taker only), `rebate_credited`, `capital_injected`. Ops: `settle_window(up,down,winner)` pays winning net×$1, zeroes positions (**signed handles shorts**); `merge(up,down,requested)` removes `min(matched,requested)` pairs, +$1/pair, both-long only; `credit_rebate(min)` flushes accrued to cash at $1 floor else carries; `set_capital`/`adjust_capital(delta)`. Engine: `on_window` settles **once** on `Resolved{outcome}` (idempotent via `settled:HashSet<WindowId>`; `Closed` never settles), tokens from event metadata; `merge(window,requested,now)` looks up `TokenPair` in `self.markets` (unknown ⇒ `VenueError::VenueInternal`); daily rebate via engine-global `rebate_next_credit_at` armed lazily on first accrual, folded into `next_deadline`+`on_clock` (`run_rebate_cycle` catches up 24h periods, sub-$1 carry). `REBATE_PERIOD=24h`+$1 floor engine consts (no config keys). `PaperVenue` gains sync `set_capital`/`adjust_capital`/`ledger_snapshot` (lock-and-mutate, no `await`) + async `merge`; `balances()`/`VenueEvent{Order,Fill}` unchanged. **Decision (operator):** `merge` concrete `PaperVenue` method, NOT on `VenuePort` — port-promotion + live CTF merge deferred. **Decision (operator):** capital journaling = `ControlEvent::PaperCapitalSet` on bus by binary + `tracing` (`target:"paper-ledger"`); durable persistence deferred to `journal`. New `PaperLedgerSnapshot` (cash, signed positions, fees_paid, rebate_accrued, rebate_credited) in `wallet.rs`. Conservation test: `wallet::conservation` 8 seeds×400 ops xorshift64* vs independent shadow oracle, exact `Decimal`; closing identity `wallet.collateral==oracle.cash`. **`bot paper-sim` (§12.6):** quoter bids Down so pairs form + `try_merge` recycles (`MERGE n pairs`); `SETTLED slug -> outcome collateral X`; one-shot `adjust_capital(+$1000)` emits `PaperCapitalSet`. No new deps/config keys. **Deferred:** open-order collateral reservation (`available==total`), `merge` port-promotion + live CTF, durable journaling, config exposure of rebate period/floor.

Fills §4 stub (sqlite index a follow-up; raw segments source of truth). **Deps added to `journal`** (§3-allowlisted except flate2): serde, serde_json, tokio (`sync` only), tracing, thiserror, time, **flate2** `default-features=false,features=["rust_backend"]` — **the one allowlist deviation, operator-mandated** (pure-Rust miniz_oxide: no C/cmake, no openssl; `gunzip`-replayable; §3 prose updated); `rust_decimal` dev-dep. **`JournalRecord`** (`record.rs`) = serde projection of non-serde `Event`: internally-tagged enum (`#[serde(tag="type",rename_all="snake_case")]`) mirroring `Event` 1:1, wide variants (`Book`/`Window`-`MarketInfo`/`OrderUpdate`/`Fill`/`Inventory`) **boxed**. `from_event` derefs `Arc`s, `to_event` re-wraps — **lossless round-trip**, `from_event` match **exhaustive**. One `RecordEnvelope{seq,ts_local_ms,rec}` per JSONL line (nested, not flattened). **`Recorder`** off-hot-path: `record(&Event)` `try_send`s onto bounded tokio channel (default 16384), **drop+count on full**; dedicated **OS thread** projects+serializes+writes `GzEncoder<BufWriter<File>>`, buffered periodic+catch-up flush, **rotating on 128 MiB-uncompressed OR 1 h** → `journal-{YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS}-{NNNNN}.jsonl.gz`. `finish()` → `RecorderStats{records,dropped,segments}`. **`ReplayReader`** `read_dir`-globs+sorts, `MultiGzDecoder`, yields `RecordEnvelope`; **`.events()`** maps back to `Event`. Unreadable/crash-truncated trailing segment yields decoded prefix then stops. **`bot record`** = `fair`/`ladder` wiring minus model: scheduler (`config.engine.enabled_series()` or `--series`)+feed-clob+rtds+binance; forwards `Event::Window` to `window_tx`; `--out-dir` default `data/journal/`. **Venue-parity golden tests** (§2.5/§9 paper==live at `VenuePort`/`VenueEvent`): **operator scope** = drive venue-paper only against golden normalized `VenueEvent` sequences (NOT live/paper side-by-side diff — would pierce §11 `LiveVenue::connect`; follow-up). Local `normalize(&VenueEvent)` keeps {kind,alias(=client_id),state,side,price,size,liquidity,reject-class,fee-zero}; 13 `#[tokio::test(start_paused)]` scenarios (resting→partial-maker, post-only-cross, FOK-full/unfillable, FAK-partial/no-match, cancel-race, cancel_all/unknown, qty-kind-mismatch, window-close, GTD-expiry, record→replay dogfood). **Pinned:** paper cross/FOK/FAK rejects are **async** `VenueEvent::Order{Rejected}` from timer task; only **sync** `place()`→`VenueError` is qty-kind mismatch. **Engine architecture lint:** section-aware scan of engine `Cargo.toml` (`include_str!`, no `cargo_metadata`) asserts `venue-api` declared, `venue-live`/`venue-paper` not (§4 inward-arrows). bot gains `[dev-dependencies] tokio` `test-util`; no lib deps/config keys.

dropped planned `tokens`/`fee_rate`). `InventoryManager{books:HashMap<WindowId,_>, settled:HashSet}` folds `Event::Fill`→`InventoryEffect::Snapshot`(`fill.ts_venue`) and `Window{Resolved}`→`InventoryEffect::Settled`(`market.close_time`, idempotent; untraded emits nothing); **never iterates `books`**, no wall clock ⇒ `rebuild(events)` reproduces state AND effect stream bit-for-bit (§3/§9 restart req, via `inventory_replay.rs`). **Money (conservation-tested):** `cash_flow` single signed accumulator — BUY `−=p·sz−fee`, SELL `+=p·sold−fee` at **moving-average cost basis captured before mutating** (long-only, over-sell clamps silently per §12), MERGE `+=n·$1`; `realized_pnl=cash_flow+winning_side.shares·$1`. 8-seed×300-op xorshift64* shadow-oracle, exact `Decimal`. **Pure §8 queries:** `pair_cost_after_add`/`authorizes_passive_add` (`avg_up+avg_down≤threshold`), `excess_constraint(soft,hard)→Normal|SoftCapped|HardCapped{excess_side}`, `merge_intent(min)→Option<MergeIntent>`, `worst_case_exceeds(limit)`. **Merge advisory (operator left to me):** `merge_intent` requests, `apply_merge` reflects a confirmed merge (reduce both at avg,+$1/pair,cap at matched), but `on_event` **never drives a merge** (execution deferred); follow-up: execution must add a journaled merge-confirmation event to keep rebuild exact. **`SettlementSummary`** (new core-types): `{window,outcome,up,down,matched_pairs,pair_cost,excess,merged_pairs,fees_paid,realized_pnl,ts}` via `::close` deriving via `InventorySnapshot::derive`; analytics owns §10 locked-pair-vs-inventory split. New `Event::Settlement(Arc<SettlementSummary>)` (size guard untouched) + `JournalRecord::Settlement(Box<…>)`. **Config:** `merge_min_pairs:Size` to `EngineParams`/`EngineParamsPatch`/`apply`/`default.toml` (default **25** pairs; orthogonal to excess caps) + `validate_into` `>0`; anti-drift `patch_covers_every_params_field` auto-pins. `InventoryParams{pair_cost_threshold, soft/hard_cap_excess, merge_min_pairs, max_worst_case_loss}`+`Default` engine-local; **`config::EngineParams→InventoryParams` boundary map deferred** (NormalizerParams precedent). No new deps. **Deferred:** bot boundary map + anti-drift test; merge execution + journaled confirmation event; analytics consuming `Event::Settlement`; `forget_window` pruning.

- 2026-06-14 — **Quoting calculator implemented** (`engine::quoting` — new module, §8 strategy brain) + three shared `*_sides` free fns extracted into `engine::inventory`. Pure `calculate_quotes(model, inv, fees, params, tau_secs, tick) -> QuoteDecision` → desired resting maker quotes or `NoQuote(reason)`. **Sans-IO/pure** (every fallible step → skipped level or typed reason). No new deps; engine stays `model`/`venue-api`/`core-types`-only. **Market model (operator left to me):** no shorting ⇒ two-sided MM = **two BUY ladders** (§7 mirror BUY Up @ p ≡ SELL Down @ 1−p): Up-buys descending from `center−half_spread`, Down-buys from `(1−center)−half_spread`, both `Side::Buy` post-only `Gtc`. `down_fair=1−center` so fairs sum to 1, pair fair `1−2·hs`. **Skew:** `center=p_up−γ·norm_imbalance`, `norm_imbalance=clamp(excess/hard_cap,−1,1)`; long Up lowers center. **Decisions (operator deferred all to me):** (1) **latency premium=`σ_1s·√(cancel_rtt_secs)`**; `cancel_rtt_secs` knob (default 0.2 s ≈ 180 ms REST p50). `half_spread=max(min_edge, σ·(k1·√hold·spike_mult+√rtt))`. (2) **vol-spike:** `σ_1s≥vol_spike_threshold_1s ⇒ ×vol_spike_mult` (defaults 0.001 / 1.5×). (3) **engine-local `QuoteParams`+`Default`; config→`QuoteParams` map DEFERRED**; new knobs (cancel_rtt, atm_band, vol_spike threshold/mult) crate-local; **config crate not touched.** **Gate precedence (early-exit):** G1 health (`allows_quoting()` — Ready only) → G2 active window+matches inventory → G3 expiry (τ≤0/NaN) → G4 `no_passive_final_secs` (whole `NoQuote`) → G5 usable p_up∈(0,1)/σ>0 → S1 imbalance, S2 skewed+clamped center, S3 half-spread, **S4 caps:** `HardCapped⇒NoQuote(InventoryBlocked)`, `SoftCapped{side}`/worst-case-breach ⇒ silence excess side (`SoftCapExcessSide`/`WorstCaseExcessSide`); per side S5 ladder → S6 `no_atm_final_secs` ATM gate (per-level, `|price−0.5|≤atm_band`) → S7 pair-cost auth (`authorizes_passive_add_sides`) → S8 within-side grid-collision dedupe. One-sided survivor is `Quote`; empty set ⇒ `NoSurvivingLevels`. `QuoteSet` carries `center`/`half_spread` (6-dp), `p_up`, `normalized_imbalance`, `vol_spike`, `suppressed:Vec<Suppressed{outcome,level,reason}>`. Carries **no market metadata** (emits `Outcome`; caller maps via `MarketInfo::tokens`); does **not** call `normalize()` — emits on-grid prices + sizes ≥ minimums so downstream `normalize` only bumps. **Float→grid (likely silent bug):** `price_down` does `Decimal::from_f64_retain(target).round_dp(9)` (absorb ~1e-16) **before** `Price::quantize(..,Down)`; both ladders buy ⇒ round Down. **Shared-helper refactor:** extracted `excess_constraint_sides`/`pair_cost_after_add_sides`/`authorizes_passive_add_sides` free fns (over `&SideInventory`) into `inventory.rs`; `WindowInventory` methods delegate. Anti-drift `QuoteParams`-vs-`EngineParams` test NOT in engine (§4) — lands in `bot` with deferred map. **Canonical adverse-selection jump** test (p 0.50→0.85, σ spike: Up→0.83/0.82/0.81, Down withdrawn 0.49→0.13/0.12/0.11 — no price survives). §8 fee params in signature but unused by passive quoting (makers pay zero; reserved for takers). **Deferred:** bot config→`QuoteParams` map + anti-drift test; wiring `calculate_quotes→normalize→risk→VenuePort` (quote manager: cancel-first repricing on θ/cancel_market_theta); taker modules consuming `fees`; `bot quote` smoke.

- 2026-06-15 — **Quote manager implemented** (`engine::quote_manager` — `client_id`/`view`/`plan`/`driver` + `mod`) + `crates/bot/tests/quote_manager_paper.rs`. The §8 cancel-first convergence engine: converges open orders to the calculator's desired set through `venue-api`. **Operator decisions:** (1) **split-cycle (strict) cancel-first** — stale/endangered quote cancelled immediately, replacement placed only on a later converge once the cancel terminalizes and the slot empties (old+new never co-rest, §8); (2) engine module + paper integration test only — bot orchestration + `bot quote` subcommand **deferred** to engine-bus-wiring. **Architecture:** pure planner/view/client_id (sans-IO, tracing-free) + async `driver`. `QuoteManager` not generic — methods generic over `P: VenuePort`, no `tokio`, takes `now: TimestampMs`. Engine added only `tracing` (target `quote-manager`). **`view::RestingView`** (can't use venue-live's `OrderStore`, §4): `by_id: HashMap<OrderId,RestingOrder>` (folds `OrderUpdate`) + `slot: HashMap<(Outcome,u32),OrderId>`; `record_pending` from place `Accepted`, terminal drops both. Strict-overlap: cancelled order marked `PendingCancel`, kept in slot until terminal `Canceled`. **`client_id` `qm:{open_ms}:{U|D}:{level}:{seq}`** (process-global `seq`; `parse` recovers attribution). **`plan::ConvergencePlanner` (pure):** P0 NoQuote→`cancel_market`/AlreadyConverged; P1 `|Δp_up| > cancel_market_theta` (vs fair at last quote)→bulk `cancel_market`, replace next cycle; P2 incremental — places only into empty slots, cancels stale (`|p_new−p_old| > θ`, Decimal-space)/unmatched, pending-cancel slot `WaitingForCancel`. One converge never both cancels+places a slot. Cancel ids sorted `(outcome,level,seq)`. **Directionality, layered (operator left to me, §2.8):** *Rule A* (planner, symmetric `|Δdesired_price|>θ`) keep-vs-replace; *Rule B* (driver fast-path, directional) cancels endangered side immediately on a model tick (Up-buys when p_up fell, Down when rose), bypassing rate budget, escalating to `cancel_market` past `cancel_market_theta`; `p_up_at_last_quote` updates only on a placement. **Driver inventory:** owns an `InventoryManager` folded only from venue stream (`on_venue_event(Fill)→Event::Fill`); `on_event` ignores bus `Fill`/`OrderUpdate`; empty inv derives empty snapshot for active window. **`QuoteManagerParams` (engine-local Default, config map deferred):** `min_requote_interval_ms` 250 (throttles placements; cancels never throttled), `reprice_threshold_theta` 0.005 / `cancel_market_theta` 0.02 (mirror `config::EngineParams`, anti-drift pin), `max_batch` 15. **Dirty model:** Model/ModelHealth/Fill/book-change-at-our-levels set `dirty`; `on_requote_tick` clears only on `AlreadyConverged`; book changes coalesce (only top at/through our price dirties). **Lifecycle/risk:** Window `Open` resets per-window; `Closing`→`cancel_all`+stand down; `Closed`/`Resolved` drop window; `Event::Risk(BreakerTripped|CancelAllIssued)`→`cancel_all`+stand down until `BreakerCleared` empties tripped set→re-dirty. Non-Ready `ModelHealth`→`NoQuote`→pull. Integration asserts: (a) convergence == direct `calculate_quotes` ladder (Up/Down 0.49/0.48/0.47 at p=0.50) off journal record→replay; (b) cancel-before-replace on jump to p=0.85; (c) rate-budget (8 supra-θ ticks→1 placement cycle); (d) final-seconds withdrawal (`Closing`→empty; τ=4s ≤ `no_passive_final_secs` 5s→`NoQuote(FinalSecondsNoPassive)`→`cancel_market`). Tested. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → QuoteManagerParams`/`QuoteParams`/`NormalizerParams`/`InventoryParams` maps + anti-drift; orchestration wiring + `bot quote` smoke; merge-request execution; a `cancel_batch` port method.

- 2026-06-15 — **Momentum taker implemented** (`engine::taker` — `signal`/`edge`/`driver` + `mod`) + `crates/bot/tests/momentum_taker_paper.rs`. The §8 fee-aware momentum/staleness module: a fresh fast-feed move implies a stale book and model-vs-book edge after fee+buffer is positive ⇒ fire one **FAK marketable BUY** sized against displayed depth + per-window taker budget. **Operator decisions:** (1) scope = engine module + unit + paper integration test; config map/keys/orchestration/`bot momentum` smoke **deferred**. (2) **NO τ floor** — purely signal/edge/budget/cooldown/risk driven; only a defensive `τ>0` guard. **Architecture (mirrors quote_manager):** pure `signal`/`edge` + async `driver`; not generic (`port: &P`, `now: TimestampMs`); engine added no deps. **`signal::SignalWindow`/`ConfirmedMove`:** per-asset ring `(ts_local, value)` pruned to lookback; `confirmed_direction(now, sigma_1s, sigma_mult)` → `Some` iff ≥2 in-window ticks and `|ln(S_newest/S_oldest)| > sigma_mult·σ_1s·√T` (T = actual oldest→newest span). Inputs: only `BinanceDirect`/`Mid` ticks (~2.7s-leading); σ_1s from `ModelSnapshot` (gated on model `Ready`). **`edge::plan_take` → `TakePlan`/`NoTakeReason`:** walks `BookSnapshot.asks` ascending, accepts each level while per-share net edge clears rebate-discounted fee+buffer, caps notional at remaining budget (truncates mid-level), requires final notional ≥ $1, `worst_price` = highest accepted ask (FAK slippage cap; no absolute price cap). **Decisions (operator left to me, §2.8):** (a) per-share marginal gate, proven monotone — net edge `(fair−p) − rate·p·(1−p)·(1−reb) − buffer` strictly decreasing in p (derivative `−1 − rate·(1−2p)·(1−reb)`, `|·| ≤ rate < 1`) ⇒ stop-at-first-failing-level correct; exact `core_types::taker_fee` (5-dp round, $0.00001 floor) summed per slice for gross `expected_fee`. (b) `BookAlreadyRepriced` (best ask ≥ fair) vs `EdgeBelowFeePlusBuffer` distinct reasons. (c) fire-once (partial FAK leaves single-sided position to inventory/merge). **Taker-rebate finding (docs "Taker Rebates"):** tiered on 30-day weighted volume `wV = size·(1−entry)·weight·bonus` (Bronze $2k→3% … Obsidian $10M→50%), paid daily 00:00 UTC in pUSD NOT at execution ⇒ immaterial → 0%. Wired as default-zero `taker_rebate_pct` discounting only the decision fee (`effective_fee = taker_fee·(1−rebate_pct)`); `expected_fee` stays gross. **`driver::MomentumTaker` (target `momentum-taker`):** sync `ingest`/`decide`/`on_venue_event`/`on_window`/`on_risk` + async `on_event`/`attempt_take`/`fire`. Triggers on `Event::Model` + active-token `Event::Book`; `PriceTick` only updates the ring. **Budget committed-in-flight aware:** `effective_spent = realized_spent + Σ pending`; place `Accepted` commits planned notional to `pending`, each `Fill` moves it to `realized` + decrements, terminal `Order` drops residual. Fill attribution by `our_orders` `OrderId` set (`debug_assert` liquidity==Taker). Risk veto (`tripped`/`standing_down`), no cancel-all (FAK terminal). Gate ladder→typed `NoTakeReason`: standing-down→active window→model→window match→`Ready`→usable fair/σ→`τ>0`→confirmed move→cooldown→budget→cached book→`plan_take`. FAK `client_id "mt:{open_ms}:{seq}"`, routed through `normalize` (book `None`, cross check skipped; re-checks $1 notional + snaps worst price). **`MomentumTakerParams` (engine-local):** `momentum_buffer`/`budget_per_window`/`cooldown_ms` mirror `config::EngineParams` (`taker_momentum_buffer` 0.005, `taker_budget_per_window` $10, `taker_cooldown_ms` 5000), anti-drift pin; `signal_lookback_ms` 2000, `signal_sigma_mult` 3.0, `taker_rebate_pct` 0.0 crate-local. **Config crate NOT touched.** Edge test: refuses fee-marginal at p≈0.5 (fee 0.0175 + buffer 0.005 ⇒ need edge > 0.0225). Integration: confirmed up-move + stale-cheap Up book → one FAK fills 12.5 sh @ 0.80 ($10 cap), fee == `taker_fee(12.5,0.07,0.80)` = $0.14, off journal record→replay; cooldown blocks 2nd take, releases after 6s. Tested. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → MomentumTakerParams` map + new keys (`signal_lookback_ms`/`signal_sigma_mult`/`taker_rebate_pct`) + anti-drift; shared `taker_budget_per_window` reconciliation across momentum+late (driver-local for now); orchestrator wiring + `bot momentum` smoke; late-window taker.

- 2026-06-15 — **Final-seconds behavior implemented** = (A) defense-in-depth final-seconds invariant + (B) late-window certainty taker (`engine::late_window`). bot integration test `crates/bot/tests/late_window_taker_paper.rs`. **Operator decisions:** (1) invariant = pure checker wired into the manager; (2) tie rule = rely on Chainlink-anchored `p_up`; (3) budget driver-local, shared reconciliation deferred. **(A) Invariant** (`quoting::{PassiveLevelRef, FinalSecondsViolation, check_final_seconds_invariant}`): independent re-check of G4 (no-passive: any passive level when `τ ≤ no_passive_final_secs`) + S6 (ATM lockout: passive level with `|price−0.5| ≤ atm_band` when `τ ≤ no_atm_final_secs`), mirroring calculator `≤` boundaries + `dec!(0.5)`/`atm_band`. Wired into `QuoteManager::execute_plan` after `execute_cancels`, before draft loop: recomputes `tau` locally from same `now`/`market.close_time`, `debug_assert!` (hard fail in dev/test), on non-empty logs `tracing::error!` per violation + `plan.places.retain(...)` drops offenders (degrades gracefully, §12). No-op normally (no-passive path already → `NoQuote(FinalSecondsNoPassive)`). **(B) `engine::late_window`** (`mod`/`edge`/`driver`, top-level sibling to `engine::taker`; taker's `signal.rs`/`plan_take` not reusable): when `τ ≤ tau_threshold_secs` (30) and Chainlink-anchored `p_up` beyond `certainty_threshold` (0.97), fires one FAK BUY of near-certain side up to `price_cap` (0.99), sized against depth + per-window budget. Mirrors `MomentumTaker` (not generic; committed-in-flight budget; `our_orders`; `tripped`/`standing_down`, no cancel-all; per-window reset on `Open`; `lw:{open_ms}:{seq}` client id). **`plan_certainty_take` → `CertaintyTakePlan`:** walks asks ascending accepting `price ≤ cap` (NOT the fee+buffer gate — fees near zero this deep), caps shares by `remaining/price`, requires ≥$1 notional, exact gross `taker_fee` per level; reasons `NoAsks`/`AllAsksAbovePriceCap`/`BudgetExhausted`/`BelowMinNotional`. **Decision differences from momentum (operator left to me, §2.8):** (i) anchor gate `model.anchor != AnchorSource::Chainlink ⇒ NotChainlinkAnchored` before any `p_up` use (never rests on fast feed); (ii) `p_up` accepted on closed `[0,1]` (UNLIKE momentum's strict `(0,1)` — wants saturated endpoints); (iii) no signal/σ gate, consumes no `PriceTick`s (`ingest` drops them), triggers on `Event::Model`/active-token `Event::Book`. **Tie rule (§6):** model decides ties (`model::fair` `if s >= k { p_up = 1.0 }`, S==K resolves Up); taker honors saturated p_up: `p_up >= certainty_threshold ⇒ Up`, `p_up <= 1 − certainty_threshold ⇒ Down`; no core-types/model change. **`LateWindowTakerParams`** (engine-local, Default mirrors config `late_window_tau_secs`/`late_certainty_threshold`/`late_taker_price_cap`/`taker_budget_per_window`/`taker_cooldown_ms`; `price_cap` via `Price::try_from(dec!(0.99))`; dropped momentum's `taker_rebate_pct`); anti-drift pin. **Config crate NOT touched** (five fields exist; `config::EngineParams → LateWindowTakerParams`/`QuoteParams` map deferred). Budget driver-local (each taker tracks own against shared `taker_budget_per_window`; combined worst-case 2× until shared reconciliation). Tests cover threshold edges (`p_up==0.97`⇒Up, `0.969`⇒`NotCertain`, symmetric Down at 0.03/0.031), tie lean (`1.0`⇒Up, `0.0`⇒Down), refusal when only fast feed confirms (`BinanceCorrected`+0.99⇒`NotChainlinkAnchored`), budget exhaustion. Integration: Chainlink p_up 0.99 + Up book 0.98×10 ⇒ one FAK fills 10 sh @ 0.98 = $9.80 ≤ $10, off journal record→replay; `BinanceCorrected`⇒zero takes. Tested. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → {LateWindowTakerParams, QuoteParams, MomentumTakerParams, …}` maps + anti-drift; shared momentum+late budget reconciliation; orchestrator wiring + `bot late`/`bot quote` smoke.

Cause journaled via `tracing target:"risk"` + `RiskStateSnapshot`; durable `Event::Risk` carries breaker kind. **Double cancel-all kept** (authoritative + forwarded `BreakerTripped`; idempotent). **core-types/venue-api additions:** `ControlEvent::{Kill,Reset}` (Event ≤128 intact); `VenueEvent::Connectivity{connected:bool}`; `RejectReason::RiskRejected(RiskRejectDetail{Halted|OpenNotionalCap|WindowHalted})`; `BreakerKind` gained `PartialOrd,Ord`. **No new deps; no new config keys. Deferred:** `config → RiskParams`/`series_caps` boundary map; six-series run-loop wiring + `bot risk` smoke; venue-live emitting `Connectivity` on real user-WS drop; enriching `Event::Risk` with cause; config field for `engine_restart_cooldown_ms`. **Chaos (`#[tokio::test(start_paused)]`, real `PaperVenue`):** each converges quoter then injects fault, asserts §11 invariant (orders cancelled + no order reaches venue until recovery/reset) — market-WS / user-WS disconnect, fast-feed staleness, daily stop, per-window loss, manual kill. Tested.

- 2026-06-15 — **`analytics` crate core implemented** (`crates/analytics/src/`: `params`/`datekey`/`markout`/`attribution`/`health`/`rollup`/`engine`+`lib`) + `crates/bot/tests/analytics_replay.rs`. Fills §4 stub: per-passive-fill markouts, per-window PnL attribution reconciling to the ledger, daily roll-ups per series×mode, per-series adverse-selection alarm. **Architecture:** `Analytics` = pure sans-IO event-time fold (`on_event`+`rebuild`) so live fold and replay yield identical state (`#[derive(PartialEq)]`) + identical `Vec<AnalyticsEffect>`. **Deps (§3-allowlisted):** `core-types` (journal does NOT re-export it), `rust_decimal`, `thiserror`, `serde`; `serde_json` dev-only; no `tokio`. **Operator decisions:** (1) markout basis = fair-value MOVE `markout_h = position_sign·(fair_O(t+h)−fair_O(t_fill))`, `fair_O = p_up`(Up)/`1−p_up`(Down), `position_sign = +1` Buy/`−1` Sell; (2) PnL = comprehensive (5 buckets sum `realized_pnl+estimated_rebate`; 4 trading buckets = bare `realized_pnl`); (3) near-close markout = clamp-to-settlement (deadline past close uses resolved 0/1, `MarkoutSource::Settled`). **Mode = per-instance** (`Analytics::new`). **`DayKey` = `millis.div_euclid(86_400_000)`** (pure-int UTC days, no `time` dep). **Markout (`markout.rs`):** per active window full `(ts,p_up)` ring (cap `snapshot_ring_max`=65536, dropped at resolution); all markouts in one pass at resolution by LOCF over ring; expiry = terminal outcome; fill with no at-or-before snapshot dropped **NoAnchor**. **Attribution (`attribution.rs`) — load-bearing:** anchor on division-free `held_mark = (winning-side shares)·$1 − (up.cost+down.cost)`, NOT rounded `pair_cost`. `realized_pnl = held_mark + traded_out_pnl − fees_paid` exact (avg-cost rounding cancels), so `settlement_remainder = (realized_pnl+fees_paid) − held_mark` = departed-share PnL, `excess_pnl` clean directional (0 for balanced book), `locked_pair_pnl = held_mark − excess_pnl`. `trading_sum() == realized_pnl` **exact**; `bucket_sum() == realized_pnl+estimated_rebate`. **Decimal finding:** shadow-oracle cross-checks agree only at 28th Decimal digit → `round_dp(18)`; division-free buy-only path asserts exact. `attribute()` = pure fn of `SettlementSummary` + `maker_rebate_base` (`Σ taker_fee(size,fee_rate,price)` over MAKER fills; maker `fee`=$0). **Rebate verified:** `fee_rate`/`rebate_rate` from `Event::Window`→`market.fees.{rate,rebate_rate}`, `AnalyticsParams.default_fee_rate/default_rebate_rate` (§7 0.07/0.20) fallback. **Adverse-selection (`health.rs`):** per-series `AdverseSelectionMonitor` (sans-IO) folds finalized 5s markouts → `InsufficientSample`(n<`min_sample` 50) → `Alarm`(rolling-mean over last `window`=200 < `negative_threshold` 0.0)/`Ok`, latched `SeriesHealth{series,mode,from,to}`. **Roll-ups (`rollup.rs`):** `DailyRollup` keyed `(Series,DayKey)`; `series_aggregates()` sums across days; `SeriesComparisonRow` = §10 columns (windows traded, fraction profitable, net/per-window PnL, locked-pair vs inventory split = locked vs `excess+remainder`, fees, rebates, avg 5s markout, fill counts, min-sample warning `<min_sample_windows` 30, health). **24/7 bounded:** markout ring + attribution accum + market meta dropped at resolution/settlement; only roll-ups + monitors persist. **No config crate touched.** **Effects:** FillMarkouts at `Window{Resolved}`, `WindowSettled`+`SeriesHealth` at `Event::Settlement`; stable order. Integration test through `journal::Recorder`→`ReplayReader`: lossless round-trip, `rebuilt==live`, effect-stream equality, `trading_sum==realized_pnl` ($3) exact, `bucket_sum==comprehensive` ($3.69930 incl $0.69930 rebate), Up/Down mirror markouts (±0.05/5s, ±0.50 expiry). **Deferred:** `config → AnalyticsParams` boundary map + anti-drift test; dashboard wiring (REST/WS of `SeriesComparisonRow`, blotter) + analytics sqlite table; orchestrator feeding live bus into `Analytics::on_event` per mode + smoke.

- 2026-06-15 — **`journal` crate completed**: queryable sqlite index, config-driven rotation+retention, fsync option, boot-time restart-rebuild, atop the gzip recorder/replay. **Operator decisions:** (1) sqlite is a queryable index **alongside** the gzip log — ALL events go to `journal-*.jsonl.gz` segments (ordered source of truth; replay+rebuild read only those), six **structured** kinds *also* in sqlite (high-rate tick/book/model/health/inventory don't); (2) retention = age + total-size cap (`0` disables each). **Dep (§3 "rusqlite or sqlx-sqlite"): rusqlite `=0.37` `bundled`** (journal-only) + `rust_decimal` promoted to a journal dep. Context7-verified both compile SQLite C via `libsqlite3-sys`/`cc`; rusqlite wins for its **synchronous** API fitting the writer OS thread (`bundled` statically links SQLite 3). **`crates/journal/src/index.rs` (NEW):** `JournalIndex` (writer) owns a `rusqlite::Connection` used entirely on the writer thread, `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL`+`synchronous=NORMAL`; lazy `BEGIN`/`COMMIT` batching via `execute_batch`; `index(seq,ts,&JournalRecord)` matches the six structured variants (`_=>no-op`); `prune(cutoff_ms)` DELETEs by `ts_local_ms`. `JournalIndexReader` = separate read-only conn + `fills_for_window`/`recent_fills`/`orders_for_window`/`windows`/`settlements`/`breaker_trips`/`control_events`. **Lossless columns:** decimals as exact `Decimal::to_string` TEXT (never REAL); ids/enum labels TEXT via total-`match` helpers (stable DB labels, NOT serde names); timestamps INTEGER ms; `WindowId` split into `series`+`open_ms`; `meta(schema_version=1)`; corrupt cell **rejected** (`JournalError::Corrupt`/`Sqlite`). **6 tables** + `(series,open_ms)`+`(ts_local_ms)` indexes; no PK/UNIQUE on `seq` (best-effort over append-only log). **`recorder.rs`:** `RecorderParams` gains `sqlite_path:Option<PathBuf>` (None=gzip-only), `fsync_on_flush`, `retention_max_age_ms`, `retention_max_total_bytes`; `RecorderStats` gains `indexed`/`pruned_segments`; `Segment` gains a `try_clone`d `File` so `fsync_on_flush` `sync_all`s without finalizing gzip. Sqlite index **secondary/best-effort** (any error → log + drop index + continue gzip). Writer fans out per event, commits on flush, retention sweep on every rotation + finish. Retention = pure `segments_to_delete(infos,now,max_age,max_bytes)` (oldest-first, protects newest/active) + IO `sweep_retention` parsing open-ms from the **filename stamp** (`parse_segment_open_ms`, no mtime), then `index.prune(now−max_age)`. **Config (`sections/journal.rs`):** replaced the 2-field stub with `dir` (renamed from `tick_dir`; `deny_unknown_fields` makes stale `tick_dir` a hard error), `sqlite_path`, `max_segment_bytes`/`max_segment_age_ms`, `channel_capacity`, `flush_interval_ms`, `fsync_on_flush`, `retention_max_age_ms`/`retention_max_total_bytes`; `validate_into` (size ≥ 4096, cap 0-or-≥ one segment). **`bot` boot — `crates/bot/src/boot.rs` (NEW, named `boot` not `journal` to avoid crate shadow):** `journal_params(config)->RecorderParams` (§4 boundary map) + `rebuild_from_journal(dir)->RebuiltState{inventory,orders,events}` replaying `ReplayReader::events()` → `InventoryManager::on_event` (==`::rebuild`) + bot-local order fold. **Finding (verified in `quote_manager/view.rs`):** `RestingView` **cannot** rebuild from journaled `OrderUpdate`s — `apply_order_update` early-returns for ids not seen via `record_pending`, which needs the `ClientId` (outcome/level) that exists only at placement and never travels the bus. So rebuild uses a bespoke `RebuiltOrders(HashMap<OrderId,OrderUpdate>)` (last-write-wins, dropped on terminal); **live** restart = last-known view corrected by `venue_live::OrderStore::reconcile`, **paper** = journal is the only source. `rebuild_and_log` wired into `Command::Paper`/`Live`/`paper-sim` (missing dir=clean start, corrupt journal=warn+fresh, never blocks); `bot record` sources params via `journal_params`. No run loop consumes `RebuiltState` yet. **Deferred:** orchestrator seeding live `InventoryManager`/`OrderStore` from `RebuiltState` + run loop; `bot reindex` rebuilding the sqlite from segments.

- 2026-06-15 — **Per-series comparison aggregates extended** (`analytics`: `params`/`attribution`/`rollup`/`engine`/`lib`) — §10 decision-table data over **selectable time windows** with a markout **distribution**, **taker-budget usage**, **maker/taker mix**, window-aware **min-sample flag**, serializable **query interface with sorting hints**. Built on `(Series,DayKey)` `RollupStore`+`SeriesComparisonRow`; sans-IO, deterministic-rebuild, bounded; money `Dollars`/`Decimal`, only markouts f64. **`config` untouched** (`config::EngineParams→AnalyticsParams` map deferred). **Operator deferred both design calls to me (§2.8).** **Decisions:** (1) `ComparisonWindow{Today,LastDays(u16),All}` (Copy/serde) + `contains(day,today)`; "today" a `DayKey` **param** (no clock; bot passes `DayKey::from_ts(now)`). `LastDays(n)`=inclusive `[today-(n-1)..=today]`. New `RollupStore::series_aggregates_over(window,today)` + `Analytics::series_comparison_over(window,today)->SeriesComparison`; legacy `series_comparison()`/`series_aggregates()` kept (=`All`) atop it. (2) **Markout distribution = fixed-bin histogram + moments** (operator: "whatever is good") — only representation **exactly additive across days** AND bounded (raw retention rejected). `MarkoutDistribution{n,mean,stddev,min,max,p50,p95,histogram:[u64;17]}` (Copy); population stddev `sqrt(max(0,Σx²/n−mean²))`; **17 bins** via `MARKOUT_BIN_EDGES` (16 interior edges symmetric, finest near zero: `±{0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.03,0.05,0.10}`); `markout_bin(v)`=count edges `≤v`; p50/p95 interpolated. `DailyRollup`/`SeriesAggregate` gain additive `markout_5s_sumsq`/`_min`(+INF)/`_max`(−INF)/`_hist:[u32;17]`. (3) **Taker-budget usage** — `WindowAttrib::apply_fill` reconstructs `taker_notional=Σ price·size` over Taker fills, via `WindowAttribution::from_summary`. **Independent accumulator, NOT a PnL bucket** — `trading_sum`/`bucket_sum`+ledger identities byte-unchanged. Row: `taker_notional` + `taker_budget_used_fraction = mean-per-window-notional/taker_budget_per_window` (`None` if budget 0 or no windows). New `AnalyticsParams.taker_budget_per_window` (default `$10`, §8 shared budget) + `ParamsError::Negative` (allows 0→`None`). (4) `maker_fill_fraction` (0 when none). (5) **Sorting = pre-sorted rows + hints** (operator: "im not sure") — `SeriesComparison{window,day,rows,default_sort}`, `rows` best-first by `default_sort` (NetPnl desc, `Series` tiebreak), serializable `SortColumn` (13 cols, `ALL`/`label()`/`higher_is_better()`/`#[default]=NetPnl`) + `SeriesComparisonRow::sort_key(col)->f64` (None sorts last via −INF; `Health` ordinal Ok=0<InsufficientSample=1<Alarm=2). (6) **min-sample** against the **selected window's** traded count; **health** stays the rolling adverse-selection gate (window-independent). (7) `SeriesComparisonRow.avg_markout_5s` mirrors `markout_5s.mean`. **Determinism fix (load-bearing):** `series_aggregates_over` folds matching days in sorted `(series,day)` order (not HashMap order) → f64 markout sums **bit-stable across instances**, `live==rebuilt` exact; cross-day additivity exact on histogram/n/min/max, mean/stddev within 1e-12. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams→AnalyticsParams` map (`taker_budget_per_window`/`min_sample_windows`/markout-window) + anti-drift; dashboard wiring (REST/WS of `SeriesComparison`, server-side `SortColumn` sort, histogram chart); orchestrator feeding the live bus into `Analytics::on_event` per mode + `bot analytics` smoke.
