loud `still NOT trading` warning re-fires every `readiness_timeout_ms` (never exits). **Risk re-entrancy (verified `risk/core.rs`):** `core.on_event` reacts only to `Event::Risk(ClockSkew)` + `Event::Control(Kill/Reset/DailyStopReset)`; own breaker kinds fall through `_=>{}`, so orchestrator records+projects `take_published` directly (no re-bus, no double-react); bus arm feeds ClockSkew always, market-data only when `order_path_open`. **Venue events:** `risk.on_venue_event` always (authoritative inventory/open-notional from stream); journal+project; orchestrator's own `InventoryManager` (dashboard `Inventory`/`Settlement`); `working:HashSet<OrderId>` for shutdown proof. **Control plane** (`apply_control`): venue action, then `risk.on_event` with `Event::Control` (authoritative Manual/DailyStop via `take_published`), skips the pre-baked `Event::Risk` (avoid dup). **Shutdown (§5):** `armed=false`→`venue.cancel_all()`→`drain_open_orders` (until `working` empty or 5s, logs "zero open orders")→signal `shutdown_tx`+drop bus senders→drain-join 5 tasks→stop server→`recorder.finish()`. `shutdown_signal()` = Ctrl-C + `#[cfg(unix)] SignalKind::terminate()`. **Memory:** every channel bounded (bus 256 await+keepalive; window/market 64 try_send-drop; control 16; recorder 16384 drop+count; dashboard broadcast 1024; venue 1024); `rss_report_secs` tick logs RSS (Linux `/proc/self/statm` field 2 ×4KiB, dep-free) + prunes settled inventory in both `InventoryManager`s (`prune_settled_before` drops settled windows >2h; risk core drops only old+settled `window_cids`). **config→engine maps (deferred item discharged):** `risk_params(config)` + 5 `config.engine.defaults → {QuoteParams, QuoteManagerParams, MomentumTakerParams, LateWindowTakerParams, NormalizerParams}` mappers (fields without a config key — `cancel_rtt_secs`/`atm_band`/vol-spike, `min_requote_interval_ms`/`max_batch`, signal lookback/sigma/rebate, `size_decimals`, `engine_restart_cooldown_ms` — keep engine default) + `series_caps`; anti-drift pins `risk_params(AppConfig::default()) == RiskParams::default()`. **Architectural reality:** `RiskManager` owns single strategy instances (per-window state inside each), consumes one `EngineParams` set (defaults) + per-series worst-case caps; per-series strategy-param overrides beyond cap not consumable by the single-instance architecture (documented limitation). **New `[run]` section:** `require_clock_check`/`require_discovery_check`, `clock_check_grace_ms` 15s, `readiness_timeout_ms` 120s, `supervision_{initial,max}_backoff_ms` 1s/30s + `multiplier` 2.0, `stable_secs` 60, `rss_report_secs` 60, `risk_tick_ms` 500 (anti-drift covered). **Runtime kill** gates order path on `control_state.is_halted()` (defense-in-depth with risk Manual breaker); full per-series runtime enable/disable of the single-instance engine = deferred. **No new deps.** Engine→venue wiring integration-tested at engine level (`risk_manager_paper.rs`+`quote_manager_paper.rs`); run.rs logic unit-tested; end-to-end = live acceptance. **Deferred:** orchestrator seeding live engine from `boot::rebuild_and_log`'s `RebuiltState`; full per-series enable/disable + param-override to the single-instance engine; config exposure of strategy params; live-execution pipeline (LiveVenue+gate-3) replacing paper venue under the same orchestrator.

- 2026-06-16 — **Chaos test suite implemented** (`crates/bot/tests/chaos.rs`, feature-gated `chaos`; 9 deterministic scenarios) + `just chaos` recipe + README 24h-soak checklist. Paper-only scripted fault-injection asserting four §5/§9/§11 invariants per fault: no orphan open orders, right breaker trips with journaled cause, trading halts/resumes per rules, state rebuilds exactly from journal. No production code changes, no new deps. **Operator deferred all 3 design calls (§2.8):** (1) feature-gated test suite not subcommand — `[features] chaos=[]`, file `#![cfg(feature="chaos")]` (0-test binary in default loop, §3); (2) inject manifested `BookHealth`/`FeedHealth` events for WS-kill/feed-stall (transport-death→event link already proven by feed crates' driver tests; chaos proves event→invariant half); (3) subsystem-integration level (`risk_manager_paper` harness: `RiskManager`+`PaperVenue`+real `journal::Recorder` on shared `start_paused` clock, `PaperParams{rng_seed:Some(1),jitter_ms:ZERO}`), not a `run.rs` refactor. **Scenarios (6 fault classes):** kill market-WS (`BookHealth::Unreliable{Disconnected}`→`WsDisconnect`), kill user-WS (`VenueEvent::Connectivity{false}`→`WsDisconnect`), stall fast feed (binance-mid silence >500ms+`on_tick`→`FeedStale`), stall chainlink (`FeedHealth::Stale{ChainlinkRtds}`→`FeedStale`), stall book (`BookHealth::Unreliable{Stale}`→`FeedStale`), matching-engine restart (test-local `FaultyPort` wrapping `&PaperVenue` returns `VenueError::EngineRestarting` on place→guard `observe()`→`EngineRestart`), clock jump (real `timeutil::SkewMonitor`→`Trip`/`Clear`→mint `Event::Risk(BreakerTripped/Cleared{ClockSkew})`), discovery failure at rollover (real `scheduler::run`+2 series+starving `Refresh`), process restart mid-window (record→`ReplayReader`→`InventoryManager::rebuild`). **Findings:** (a) `EngineRestart` needs two `on_tick` turns (guard drains observations at turn top); (b) the trigger reprice must stay inside `FairVsMid` bound — 0.62 vs 0.50 mid = 0.12 trips `FairVsMid` (>0.10 for >3s) during the 5s cooldown; 0.55 (0.05 move, >`cancel_market_theta` 0.02 so reprices, <0.10) isolates it; (c) `ClockSkew` is `RiskOutput::Forward` not published — journaled cause is the *input* `Event::Risk(BreakerTripped{ClockSkew})` (still indexes `breaker_trips` table); (d) `FaultyPort` fails only placements not cancels (cancel-all still evacuates); (e) `bot` is binary-only so `tests/` can't import `bot::boot` — restart scenario replicates via `ReplayReader`+`InventoryManager::rebuild`+local last-write-wins/drop-on-terminal order view; (f) scheduler `on_discovery(None)` keeps current window+backoff-retries — starved series goes `discovering` while healthy rolls (per-series isolation), recovers when discovery returns. **Determinism:** breaker scenarios `#[tokio::test(start_paused=true)]` single-test-task (only venue spawned); per-PID temp dirs; `recorder.finish()` synchronous; `just chaos` pins `--test-threads=1`; verified 10× identically. EngineRestart/ErrorRate/ClockSkew logic stays unit-tested in `engine::risk::core`; window-loss/daily-stop/manual-kill stay in `risk_manager_paper.rs`. **Deferred:** thin full-orchestrator chaos test once `run.rs` supervision becomes injectable over `Transport`/`Refresh`/`Clock`; optional `feed-util/fake` transport-kill scenario.

- 2026-06-16 — **Replay harness + parameter sweep implemented** (`analytics::replay`, feature-gated) + **`bot replay`/`bot sweep` subcommands** (`crates/bot/src/replay_cmd.rs`, feature `replay`). Deterministically re-runs the *full engine* (§11 `RiskManager` + quote manager + momentum/late takers) over a recorded journal against a seed-stable `PaperVenue`, faster than real-time → live-paper-shaped analytics; plus a grid sweep over four quoting params → ranked dashboard-shaped table. **Operator decisions:** sweep "cancel threshold" dim drives `reprice_threshold_theta` and scales `cancel_market_theta` by the same ratio (§8 cancel-market ≥ reprice); grid = CLI comma-separated lists (`--grid` TOML deferred). **§3 gating:** `analytics`'s `replay` feature = `["dep:engine","dep:venue-paper","dep:venue-api","dep:tokio"]`; default build pulls none (verified `cargo tree`); `bot`'s `replay = ["analytics/replay"]` gates subcommands (`#[cfg(feature="replay")]`, off → `bail!`). **§4 acyclic:** harness takes constructed `RiskParams`/`series_caps`/`PaperParams`/`AnalyticsParams`; bot maps `config →` these via `run.rs` mappers + `paper::paper_params` (+ `replay_paper_params` forcing `rng_seed=Some(1)`/zero-jitter). **Re-run loop (`replay::drive`):** real `PaperVenue` (timer task) on a current-thread `start_paused` runtime, advancing virtual time via `tokio::time::sleep` to each record's `ts_local_ms` (auto-advance fires due deadlines + runs task before `try_recv`-drain). **Input filtering:** drop recorded engine/venue outputs (`OrderUpdate`/`Fill`/`Inventory`/`Settlement`/`ControlAudit`, classified on `JournalRecord` variant); feed every other event to `venue.on_bus_event` + `risk.on_event`; recorded `Model`/`ModelHealth` **replayed** (model params not swept → `model` crate out of dep set). Re-run fills → fresh `InventoryManager` + `Analytics`; `Window{Resolved}` → `Event::Settlement`. `risk.take_published()` drained+discarded; periodic `risk.on_tick`s merged at `config.run.risk_tick_ms`. **Determinism:** single-thread runtime + paused clock + required `rng_seed:Some(_)` + `try_recv`-only draining + analytics sorted-order fold; output = canonical `serde_json`; `today` from tape's last `ts_local_ms`. **Honest:** *guaranteed* = replay-vs-replay byte-identical; "same as live paper" is *approximate* (live used wall clock + real async order + wall-seeded RNG). **Sweep:** `SweepGrid` (4 `Vec`s, empty dim → config base) → nested cartesian → `run_replay` over the **shared** tape (read once into `Vec<RecordEnvelope>`); `SweepReport` rows ranked best-first by `SortColumn` (`higher_is_better`-aware) + stable cartesian-index tiebreak; `SweepAggregate` folds per-point `SeriesComparison.rows`. **Optional `--parallel`** fans points across `available_parallelism()−2` `std::thread::scope` threads (each own runtime); parallel == sequential. **New deps:** `analytics` optional `engine`/`venue-paper`/`venue-api`/`tokio[rt,time,sync,test-util]` (feature `replay`) + `flate2` **dev**-dep (test writes a gzip segment preserving exact `ts_local_ms`, reads via production `ReplayReader`); `bot` no new lib deps; all §3-allowlisted. Tests: byte-identical, nontrivial-work (maker fill @0.49 + Up settlement, net PnL +5.10), unseeded-rejected, sweep-ranks-deterministically (2×2 → 4 rows, sequential==parallel), gzip-roundtrip, `parses_replay_flags`/`parses_sweep_flags`. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → AnalyticsParams` map (still `::default()`); `--grid <toml>`; re-run-the-model mode if model/vol params swept; streaming/`--re-read` sweep for very large tapes.

- 2026-06-16 — **Deployment scaffolding prepared (paper phase, no live keys)** — VPS release profile, hardened systemd service + secrets layout, log/journal rotation guidance, drain-before-swap upgrade flow, backup/restore, VPS-region-selection. **Docs + config + profile only — NO Rust source/dependency/config-crate changes** (FHS paths + retention use existing keys). **Operator decisions:** extend `[profile.release]` in place (one production profile); default dashboard to **loopback + SSH tunnel** (no port/token/TLS). **(1) Release profile** (`Cargo.toml [profile.release]`): `lto="thin"`+`codegen-units=1`, `opt-level=3`, `debug="line-tables-only"`+`strip="debuginfo"` (keep `file:line`+named frames), **`panic="unwind"`** (a panicked task unwinds in isolation so the §5 supervision tree restarts it; `abort` would SIGABRT the 24/7 process — do NOT change to abort), `overflow-checks=true` (traps integer counter/retention math; money is rust_decimal, hot math f64). **`target-cpu=native` NOT committed** — opt-in via `RUSTFLAGS` only when build-host CPU == run-host (SIGILL otherwise). **(2) Build** ON the VPS or identical Linux (same distro/glibc/CPU) — cross-compile impractical (mold Linux-only; aws-lc-rs + rusqlite-bundled compile C). README VPS notes: apt `build-essential clang cmake mold pkg-config`, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=$(nproc)` (committed `jobs=10` is dev-PC), **≥4 GB RAM / ≥2 vCPU**, `--timings`, `ldd` check (libc/libm/libgcc/pthread only). **(3) FHS paths need NO code change** (verified): `journal.dir`/`journal.sqlite_path`/`log.dir` are `PathBuf`; `Env::prefixed("BOT_").split("__")` maps `BOT_JOURNAL__SQLITE_PATH→journal.sqlite_path` (single `_` preserved in leaf keys); set in `bot.local.toml` so the unit keeps `/opt`+`/etc` read-only, only `/var/lib/bot` writable. **(4) systemd** (`deploy/bot.service`): `Type=exec`, dedicated `bot` user, `ExecStart=/opt/bot/bin/bot run --config-dir /etc/bot/config` via **symlink** for atomic swaps, `EnvironmentFile=/etc/bot/bot.env` (0600); `KillSignal=SIGTERM`+`KillMode=mixed`+`TimeoutStopSec=30` (> §5 5 s drain); `Restart=on-failure`+`RestartSec=10`+`StartLimitIntervalSec=300`/`Burst=5`; `StandardOutput/Error=journal`; full sandbox hardening (NoNewPrivileges, ProtectSystem=strict, ProtectHome, PrivateTmp, ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/bot, ProtectClock, Protect{KernelTunables,KernelModules,ControlGroups}, Restrict{Namespaces,Realtime,SUIDSGID}, LockPersonality, SystemCallFilter=@system-service, LimitNOFILE=65536, RestrictAddressFamilies=AF_INET AF_INET6 AF_UNIX **AF_NETLINK** — AF_NETLINK required for glibc DNS); **`MemoryDenyWriteExecute` left commented** (can break aws-lc/ring TLS — verify via `systemd-analyze verify`/`security`). **(5) Secrets** (`deploy/bot.env.example`, 0600): paper-only `RUST_LOG=info`, commented `BOT_SECRET_DASHBOARD_TOKEN` (non-loopback only), commented path overrides + fenced commented live `BOT_SECRET_PM_*`/`BOT_SECRET_LIVE_CONFIRM`; `EnvironmentFile` = KEY=value, no `export`/`$VAR`. **(6) Dashboard:** loopback + `ssh -N -L 8080:localhost:8080` (default, no token) primary; public-bind+token+firewall+TLS-proxy (plain HTTP) alt. **(7) Rotation:** automatic = app logs daily/keep-14, journal segments 128 MiB/1 h; **enable** journal retention (`retention_max_age_ms`/`retention_max_total_bytes` in `bot.local.toml`; defaults 0 = unbounded; size cap 0 or ≥ `max_segment_bytes` 128 MiB). **logrotate must NEVER touch journal gzip/sqlite** (corrupts WAL + desyncs index; no `bot reindex`). journald via `journald.conf SystemMaxUse`/`journalctl --vacuum-*`. **(8) Upgrade** (`deploy/upgrade.sh`): drain-then-swap — install versioned binary → `systemctl stop` → grep journal for `zero open orders`+`journal flushed` → atomic `ln -sfn` → start → poll `/health`+`ARMED` (≤60 s) → auto-rollback. **Honest:** paper state resets per session (no `RebuiltState` consumer) — drain buys **zero orphaned open orders**, not continuity; upgrade via SIGTERM, never `bot control kill`. **(9) Backup** (`deploy/backup.sh`): primary = gzip segments + config; sqlite via online `.backup` (never raw-`cp` a live WAL DB), rotated segments immutable; `bot.env` separate/encrypted; off-box rsync + nightly cron; restore = stop → restore `data/`(drop stale `-wal`/`-shm`)+config → chown → start. **(10) VPS selection:** `bot latency --label <region>` (read-only, no keys) per candidate, **choose lowest CLOB REST `p99_ms`** (`rest[].stats.p99_ms` for `clob/time`+`clob/ok`); tie-breakers WS `connect_ms`+RTDS `gaps.p50`; **NTP-unreachable region (UDP/123 blocked) = hard disqualifier** (permanent ClockSkew → won't arm); feed measured p50/p95 into `paper.placement_latency`/`cancel_latency`. **`.gitignore`:** added `/deploy/bot.env`. **Files:** `Cargo.toml`, `justfile`, `.gitignore`, `.env.example`, README, new `deploy/{bot.service,bot.env.example,bot.local.toml.example,upgrade.sh,backup.sh}`. **Deferred:** `bot reindex` tool; single-instance/PID-flock guard; seeding live engine state from journal on restart; live-execution pipeline (LiveVenue + gate-3) under the same unit.

(b) `rollup::{DailyRollup,SeriesAggregate}` gained additive `matched_pairs`/`merged_pairs`/`stranded_shares`/`pair_cost_sum`/`pair_cost_n`; `SeriesComparisonRow` exposes `pairs_completed`/`stranded_shares`/`avg_pair_cost`/`avg_stuck_leg_pnl`(`None`-guarded). `SortColumn` unchanged (`ALL.len()==13`). NOT in `Analytics::on_event` (best-effort live like `LiveMarkout`); dashboard owns one per mode in `ModeState`, fed from the single `project()`. **Backend:** `GET /api/summary?mode&series&window` → `SummaryDto{headline,status,explainer,activity}` from raw `SeriesAggregate` sums; `compute_status()` derives `Healthy`/`Caution`/`WarmingUp` from stuck-leg sign/magnitude + adverse-selection alarm + min-sample, tooltip `reason`. `GET /api/settlements?mode&series&limit` splits windows into the two buckets via `from_summary`. - 2026-07-03 — **Binance `depth20@100ms` capture + standalone `model-lab` (Python research).** Two decoupled deliverables. **(A) ONE bot-side change — depth capture** (`crates/bot/src/depth_capture.rs`, new): a **self-contained** side channel that subscribes to Binance `depth20@100ms` for BTCUSDT+ETHUSDT over its **own** dedicated WS connection and journals raw frames to a **separate** per-UTC-day gzip series `data/depth/binance-depth20-{YYYYMMDD}.jsonl.gz` — **deliberately NOT on the event bus** and **NO new `core_types::Event` variant** (which would ripple through every exhaustive `match Event` in model/risk/analytics/dashboard/journal), so the engine/trading logic is untouched. Reuses `feed-util`'s `Transport`/`Backoff`/`WsFrame` seams (the `feed-clob` hand-rolled connect→recv→reconnect loop, minus subscription — combined-stream URL subscribes by itself); dead-socket watchdog 30 s; dedicated OS writer thread (house idiom) with **UTC-day rotation** (append-mode `OpenOptions` ⇒ same-day restart writes a second gzip member, `MultiGzDecoder`-readable); bounded writer channel, drop+count on full. Line shape `{"recv_ms","stream","data":{bids/asks verbatim}}` (payload unparsed; the lab parses levels offline). **Wired into `bot record` AND `bot run`** (operator choice), gated on new config `feeds.binance_depth_capture` (bool, default true); in `run.rs` it is a supervised but **non-critical** `Task::Depth` (its death never triggers cancel-all — research-only, never reaches a venue), restart-with-backoff like the feeds. **Config (2 keys, anti-drift in lockstep):** `feeds.binance_depth_capture` + `journal.depth_dir` (default `data/depth`) added to the section structs, `Default`, `config/default.toml`, `journal.validate_into` (non-empty), and the `rejects_bad_values` count (8→9); the `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` pin passes. **Deps added to `bot`:** `flate2` + `time` promoted to direct deps (both already transitive via `journal`); `feed-util { features=["fake"] }` as a **dev**-dep (resolver v2 ⇒ the scripted fake transport is in test builds only, never production). `feed.rs::depth_params` maps `[feeds]`+`[journal]`→`DepthCaptureParams` at the binary boundary. Reader-invisible to journal machinery (`ReplayReader`/retention only glob `journal-*`). 5 `#[cfg(test)] start_paused` tests over `FakeTransport` + an `AtomicI64` clock: URL shape, ack/data-frame parsing, **UTC-day-boundary rotation → two files**, peer-close reconnect (URL pinned), connect-failure backoff floor. **(B) New top-level `model-lab/`** (Python, fully separate from the cargo workspace — cargo never touches it): venv + exact-pinned `requirements.txt` (numpy/pandas/pyarrow/matplotlib/pytest — **no scipy/sklearn**; Φ via stdlib `math.erfc`, AUC via a rank-based numpy Mann-Whitney). One entry command per stage (`python -m model_lab.<stage>`): **ingest** (journal gz + depth gz → tidy parquet), **features** (1s grid: returns, EWMA vol, Chainlink−Binance basis_bps, depth imbalance/microprice/spread), **labels** (forward 5s returns + per-window outcomes), **validate** (Φ(z) identity + engine σ_1s EWMA reproduction vs journaled `sigma_1s` + calibration/Brier), **research** (does depth microstructure add signal: IC + AUC vs momentum baseline), **report** (self-contained `out/report.html`). `lib/math.py` faithfully reproduces `crates/model` (gap-aware bias-corrected σ_1s EWMA `var←λ^k·var+(1−λ^k)·r²/k`, λ=2^(−1/hl), warmup 60, floor 2e-5/cap 5e-3; `p_up=Φ(ln(S/K)/(σ√τ))`) and `crates/analytics` (markout). `io/journal.py` decodes the **verified** on-disk shape: nested `{"seq","ts_local_ms","rec":{"type":<snake_case>,…}}` with **PascalCase** inner enums (`BinanceDirect`/`Mid`/`Up`/`Buy`/`Maker`/`Ready`). `fixtures.py` generates a deterministic synthetic journal+depth (p_up==Φ(z) exactly, σ from the same EWMA, imbalance leads price) so `verify.py`/`pytest` exercise the whole DAG with zero real data; `run_all.py` chains the six stages over the real journal. **Verification env notes (this Windows box):** (1) full-workspace `cargo clippy --all-targets -D warnings` is **clean**; every test WDAC allowed to run **passed** — across repeated runs essentially every crate executed at least once with **zero genuine failures** (bot bins 85 incl. the 5 depth tests, config 76 incl. the anti-drift pin, model 68, venue_live 63, venue_paper 59, timeutil 40, discovery 36, dashboard 30, journal 28, scheduler 23, feed_rtds 23, feed_binance 22, feed_util 18, …). The remaining ~18 test exes stay blocked by Windows App Control **`os error 4551`** on fresh exe hashes: re-running clears many (WDAC's async cloud-reputation check, ~10–15 min) but a stubborn subset persisted this session — **empirically, reminting the marker recompiles to a new hash that also blocks immediately**, and 20-min-old unchanged hashes still block, so neither the marker workaround nor waiting forced them past on this box. Definitive full `cargo test --workspace` = CI/Linux or the operator's WDAC-managed environment. (2) `cargo fmt --all --check` flags **pre-existing** files with CRLF from `core.autocrlf=true` (no `.gitattributes`); the committed blobs are LF and my files are rustfmt-clean — left unfixed per §2.2 (don't touch unrelated code). (3) **Python 3.12 (later) installed** at `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Python\Python312` (the shell's `python`/`py` still resolve to the MS-Store alias stub — used the full path): `python -m venv` + `pip install -r requirements.txt` clean (all prebuilt cp312 wheels, no compile), **`python -m model_lab.verify` = PASS** and **`pytest` = 10 passed** (14 matplotlib↔pyparsing deprecation warnings, third-party). One fix surfaced + applied: stage prints (Φ/σ/→, and `--help` docstrings) crashed on the legacy cp1252 console → `config._enable_utf8_console()` reconfigures stdout/stderr to UTF-8 on import (covers every entry point); README gained a Windows note (Store-alias shadowing → create the venv with `py -3.12`/full path; output is UTF-8). **Deferred:** exposing depth capture's dead-socket/channel-capacity as config keys; a lab stage that consumes the sqlite index; wiring a proven depth signal back into the engine's fair value. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-03 — **model-lab calibration audit stage** — the model-vs-market calibration verdict tool. New **self-contained** command `python -m model_lab.calibration_audit` (`model_lab/calibration_audit.py`) reads the gzip journal **directly** (via `io/journal.py`; **NO** ingest/labels prerequisite — one command on fresh data) and, per resolved window, scores the **formula model `p_up`** against the **Polymarket Up-token mid** `(bid+ask)/2` (from `top_of_book`) vs the realized outcome. Emits **Brier + log-loss for BOTH predictors, split by series AND by duration-aware time-remaining bucket** (`final_20s` ≤20s / `final_minute` 20–60s / `mid` 60s–½·window / `early` >½·window), a **reliability table** (10 prob-bins × empirical Up-rate, per-bin snapshot **and** distinct-window counts), a generated **plain-language verdict**, and a **minimum-sample flag** keyed on distinct **windows** (default 50 — snapshots in one window share one correlated outcome ⇒ windows, not snapshots, are the unit of trust). Outcome from the `window` `Resolved` lifecycle (fires for every resolved window, traded or not ⇒ max sample), settlement outcome fallback; scored set defaults `health==Ready` (`--health all`, `--min-windows N` flags). Snapshot-level scoring (a window feeds snapshots to multiple τ buckets); market as-of-joined backward with ≤120 s staleness; head-to-head over the both-defined intersection + coverage; log-loss clamps p∈[1e-15, 1−1e-15] (model `p_up` saturates to 0/1 as τ→0). Writes `out/calibration_audit/{metrics.json, scores.csv, reliability.csv, report.html}` (own subdir; HTML = matplotlib→base64 reliability + Brier-by-τ plots + amber-shaded low-sample rows, `report.py` style). **`lib/math.py` gained** `log_loss` + `reliability_table` (counts); `reliability_curve`/`brier_score` untouched. **`fixtures.py` extended** (additive) — Up-token `top_of_book` (noisy market prob, model Brier ≤ market by construction) + a `Resolved` window event; existing stages ignore the extra record types (`ingest` counts unchanged). Wired into `verify.py` + `run_all.py` (after `validate`); unit tests (`log_loss`/`reliability_table`) + a smoke test added. **No Rust / no `ingest.py` / no new deps** (numpy/pandas/matplotlib already pinned). Verified: `verify` PASS, `pytest` **13 passed**, and the single command over the **real 443 MB journal** (1m47 s) — 53 windows / 397k model snapshots / 5.4M tops, **model beats market: Brier 0.1091 vs 0.1108, log-loss 0.3375 vs 0.3466**, final-20s Brier 0.0063 vs 0.1610 early, 24 thin breakdowns correctly flagged `low_sample`. **Operator directive:** run the bot 24/7 in paper from today — a week of continuous six-series paper produces thousands of resolved windows, clears the low-sample flags, and turns the audit from hint to verdict; re-run the one command in ~7 days and compare. Note: the calibration inputs (`model` `p_up`, `top_of_book` mids, `window` `Resolved` outcomes) are journaled by the feeds/model/scheduler **whether or not the bot ARMs**, so the dataset accrues even if the clock is unsynced and the ClockSkew breaker keeps it un-armed. **Deferred:** a lab stage over the sqlite index; wiring a validated signal back into engine fair value. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-03 — **model-lab historical-data acquisition stage** — bulk-download Binance **spot aggTrades** daily archives for backtesting/feature research over a long history (independent of the bot's own captures). **Python-only; NO Rust / no new deps / requirements+pyproject untouched** — stdlib `urllib`/`zipfile`/`hashlib` + existing pandas/pyarrow. Two commands (project's one-module-one-command convention, no subcommands): **`python -m model_lab.hist`** downloads/refreshes, **`python -m model_lab.hist_integrity`** validates. Shared primitives in new `model_lab/io/binance_archive.py`. **External contract verified this session** (github.com/binance/binance-public-data): URL `{BASE}/spot/daily/aggTrades/{SYMBOL}/{SYMBOL}-aggTrades-{YYYY-MM-DD}.zip`, sibling `.zip.CHECKSUM` in `sha256sum` format (`"<hex>  <name>"`); CSV inside is headerless on older / header-carrying on some 2025+ archives and 7-or-8 columns (trailing `is_best_match` dropped) — **sniffed** from the first line; `transact_time` is **microseconds from 2025-01-01, milliseconds before** → normalized to µs (detected by magnitude, ms×1000); daily archive publishes ~next day so the most recent day(s) 404. **Storage** = one **zstd-parquet per symbol-day** `aggtrades-{SYMBOL}-{YYYY-MM-DD}.parquet` under `../data/aggtrades` (root `.gitignore` `data/` already covers it — no gitignore change), sibling to `data/journal`/`data/depth`; schema `AGGTRADES_COLS` = ids int64, price/quantity float64 (research-not-accounting boundary), `transact_time` int64 µs, `is_buyer_maker` bool. **`manifest.json`** rebuilt from disk each run (ground truth, no drift): per-symbol `days_present`/`first_day`/`last_day`/`missing_in_range`/`rows_total`/`bytes_total`/`per_day{rows,bytes}` + top-level `schema_version`/`updated_utc`/`time_unit`/`base_url`/`disk_usage_bytes`+human. **`dir_disk_usage`** counts only `aggtrades-*.parquet` (excludes the self-referential manifest → stable/reproducible). **Resumable/incremental** — `download_day` skips if the final parquet exists; atomic write (`*.tmp`+`os.replace`, no partial finals on Ctrl-C); the same command extends 90 d (default, ending yesterday UTC) → 18 mo via `--days`/`--start`/`--end`. **Checksums best-effort** — verified when present, mismatch reported `checksum_failed` and **not stored**, absent sidecar stores with `checksum:absent`, `--no-verify-checksum` skips. **Missing days self-heal** — 404 → `missing`, no permanent marker, filled on a later run. **Fetch seam** (`FetchFn`, default `http_fetch` w/ retry+backoff, `ArchiveNotFound` on 404) so the whole path tests offline via synthetic in-memory ZIPs. **Concurrency** `--jobs` (default 4, `ThreadPoolExecutor`; progress printed from the calling thread only). **Integrity** (`hist_integrity`) per day-file: rows>0, rows==manifest (truncation/corruption drift), `transact_time` non-decreasing (equal µs allowed), `agg_trade_id` strictly increasing + unique (dup check); cross-day id continuity = informational note (missing-day gaps expected); writes `out/hist_integrity/report.json`, nonzero exit on any failure. **`config.py`**: `hist_dir` field on frozen `Paths` (`field(default_factory=_default_hist_dir)` so existing `Paths(...)` in verify/tests keep working) + `--hist-dir` + `MODEL_LAB_HIST_DIR` (mirrors the depth trio). **Deliberately NOT in `run_all`** (network acquisition, not the journal→parquet analysis DAG); **`verify.py` gained an offline `check_hist_offline`** in `run_checks` (synthetic archive via the injected fetch seam, run in a throwaway store under `out/` via `dataclasses.replace` — never touches the real store or the network, so the smoke test stays offline too; deviates from the plan's "set verify.main hist_dir" — isolating under `out/` also protects `test_smoke`'s `run_checks` call). **Tests** `tests/test_hist.py` (15, offline): checksum verify/tamper, header/7-8col/ms-vs-µs parse, download→skip incremental, missing 404, checksum mismatch-not-stored / absent-stores, full-run manifest + disk-usage self-consistency + same-command range extension, integrity pass + corruption detection (dup/out-of-order/empty) + manifest-drift + continuity note. **Verified (this Windows box, venv `.venv/Scripts/python.exe`):** `python -m model_lab.verify` = **PASS** (now `+ hist`), `pytest -q` = **28 passed** (13 prior + 15 new; 14 pre-existing matplotlib↔pyparsing third-party warnings); `hist --help` renders. Live network smoke deferred to next step. **Deferred:** a research stage consuming the aggTrades store (align trades to windows / build features); config exposure of `--jobs`/retry/timeout; a monthly-archive fast path for very long backfills. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-03 — **model-lab `dataset` stage — window-aligned, leakage-safe training data.** New self-contained stage `python -m model_lab.dataset` (`model_lab/dataset.py`) reads the gzip journal directly (like `calibration_audit`, no `ingest` prereq) and emits a **train-ready** `out/dataset.parquet` (one row per window×feature-timestamp, 32 cols) + `out/dataset/{SCHEMA.md, metadata.json}`. Per in-scope window (default **5m only, BTC-5m+ETH-5m**, `--series` override; `--grid-secs` 15 default): reconstructs the **strike** (=Chainlink `Vendor` price at open via `LastAtOrBefore`, `lookback 180s`/`tol 2.5s` — every `window` record has `strike:null` on disk, per `crates/model/strike.rs`); takes the **outcome** from the `Resolved` lifecycle (all resolved windows, NOT `settlements` which is traded-only → **68 resolved 5m windows vs labels.py's 12**); samples `open+k·grid_secs` while `<close`; attaches **as-of features** (reuses the no-look-ahead kernel `features.build_asset_features` + engine `sigma_1s` + derived `log_s_k`/`z`/`p_up_model=Φ(z)`) via one **backward** `merge_asof`; attaches **two labels** — (a) 30s-forward mid direction `fwd_up_30s` (`shift(-30)` on the gap-free 1s grid, ties→Up) and (b) window `outcome_up`. **Hard no-look-ahead rule enforced 3 ways** (first such enforcement in the repo): (a) runtime `_assert_no_lookahead` (feature bar ≤ sample, strike_ts ≤ open, τ>0, feature/label column-sets disjoint); (b) **AST source scan** (`test_no_lookahead_ast_scan`) over the feature functions flagging negative `shift`/`diff`, non-`backward` `merge_asof`, or any label-name reference — with a negative-control test proving the scanner non-vacuous; (c) **differential/perturbation** test (`test_no_lookahead_differential`) — recompute one sample's features from full vs future-truncated vs future-corrupted history, assert **bit-identical**. Separate **`in_live_coverage`** bool = per-window presence of a `top_of_book` for its token in `[open,close)` (where a real Polymarket market-mid benchmark exists; per-window, never over-claims). Chronological per-series **`split`** (latest `val_fraction`→val, no sample straddles). **Feature-staleness guard** (`max_feature_staleness_secs` 120, mirrors calibration_audit's `MAX_MID_STALENESS_MS`): nulls as-of features whose matched bar is a cross-recording-gap relic (bounded live max-lag 912624s→1.0s), keeping the row+outcome. Wired into `run_all` (after `labels`) + `verify` (`run_checks` block, PASS banner). **No Rust / no new deps** (numpy/pandas/pyarrow only). Verified on this box (`.venv\Scripts\python.exe`): `pytest -q` **35 passed** (28 prior + 7 new), `verify` **PASS**, real journal (2026-06-22→07-03, 2.2min) → 1360 samples/68 windows, **strike↔outcome agreement 1.0**, `run_all` full chain green. **Deferred:** consuming this dataset in a learner/research stage; 1h Binance-candle strike rule (`FirstAtOrAfter`, out of default scope); optional pre-split train/val parquet files. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-03 — **model-lab `dataset` stage extended to the full Binance aggTrades history (proxy windows) + committed the whole `model-lab/`.** The dataset now reconstructs 5m windows two ways: journal-covered windows keep the real Chainlink strike + `Resolved` outcome (**unchanged path**), and **historical** windows are reconstructed across the downloaded `data/aggtrades` store (BTCUSDT+ETHUSDT) using **Binance as a proxy** (strike = last trade ≤ open; outcome = `end ≥ strike ⇒ Up`, §6 tie rule). **Python-only; no new deps** (pyarrow already pinned). New columns (COLUMN_SPEC grew 32→35, single source of truth still drives SCHEMA.md): **`label_source`** (`chainlink`|`binance_proxy`, meta), **`strike_distance_at_close`** (abs USD) + **`strike_distance_at_close_vol`** (=`|ln(close/strike)|/(σ_realized_1s·√300)`, new **`diag`** category = reads-the-future-but-not-an-input) so knife-edge proxy windows (~13 bps Binance↔Chainlink basis could flip the outcome) are filterable/down-weightable. Proxy rows: price-only features populated (`mid`/`sigma_1s`/`log_s_k`/`z`/`p_up_model`), `chainlink`/`basis_bps`/depth = NaN, `in_live_coverage`=False. **`fwd_up_30s` stays the primary label** (price-only ⇒ proxy-safe). **Design decisions:** (1) **historical features go through the SAME scanned kernel** — `_binance_ticks_from_bars` reshapes 1s bars into synthetic `BinanceDirect/Mid` ticks → `build_feature_grid`/`build_asset_features`, so the AST scan + differential guard stay authoritative with no new feature function; added `test_no_lookahead_historical` (corrupt future bars ⇒ features bit-identical). (2) **Memory: streamed month-by-month** — each aggTrades day reduced to 1s bars then discarded (raw trades never all-resident); a month batch loads the calendar month + one buffer day each side (EWMA warmup + edge close/forward-label); output streamed via `pq.ParquetWriter` (never holds all rows). (3) **Journal subset never altered** — journal batch written first by the unchanged path; historical windows colliding with a journal `(series, open)` are **dropped** (collision-counted); split computed **independently** per source (journal via existing `_assign_split`, historical via precomputed per-series cutoffs) so history never reshuffles the journal split. Proven byte-identical in `verify` + `test_journal_subset_unchanged` (read with `dtype_backend="pyarrow"` to dodge null-driven dtype flips). (4) **`_assert_no_lookahead` runs per batch** (both sources). **Report** (`metadata.json` + stdout): per-symbol-per-month window/sample counts, knife-edge proportions at vol-thresholds {0.05,0.10,0.25,0.50}, chainlink-vs-proxy counts, dedup collisions. New `--no-history` flag. **Fixture:** `make_aggtrades_fixture` writes a tiny synthetic zstd-parquet store (2 symbols × 2 days) so `verify`/tests exercise the proxy path deterministically; `verify` now points `hist_dir` at it; `test_dataset.py` isolates `hist_dir` (hermetic) + 6 new tests; `test_smoke` builds the aggTrades fixture. **IO hardening (surfaced by running against the live 24/7 capture):** `io/journal.py` + `io/depth.py` now catch **`zlib.error`** (partial trailing deflate member of a still-being-written gzip) in addition to OSError/EOFError — decoded-prefix-then-stop, fulfilling the journal reader's documented intent. **Verified (venv):** `pytest -q` **41 passed** (35 prior + 6), `verify` PASS (now exercises the proxy path), real journal run (journal-only — **aggTrades store is empty**) 76 windows/1520 samples, strike↔outcome 1.0. Committed the entire previously-untracked `model-lab/` (29 files, commit `d9c7727`). **Then downloaded 90 days of real Binance aggTrades** (operator-approved; BTCUSDT+ETHUSDT, 180 day-files, **1.4 GiB**, checksums verified, `hist_integrity` 180/180 PASS — first live network run of `hist.py`, confirming the external contract) and ran the full stage over the real journal + store: **51,882 windows → 1,037,640 samples** (830,780 train / 206,860 val) = **84 chainlink** journal + **51,798 binance_proxy** over 8 symbol-months (BTC/ETH × 2026-04..07); knife-edge proportions {0.05σ:3.8%, 0.1σ:7.5%, 0.25σ:18.8%, 0.5σ:36.1%}; **dedup dropped 42** colliding proxy windows (journal won — the store now covers our 06-22 recording day); journal subset byte-unchanged. Correctness cross-checks on the real output: **all** window opens (both sources) 300s-aligned, **0** `(series,open)` keys shared between sources (no double-counting), month-by-month streaming held RSS bounded. Journal `strike↔outcome` 0.987 (one knife-edge journal window's reconstructed Chainlink end disagreed with the authoritative outcome — a live example validating the knife-edge columns). **Deferred:** consuming the dataset in a learner; extending history to 18 months (`hist --days 540`, resumable/incremental). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-03 — **model-lab `feature_set` stage — curated ML feature matrix on the dataset.** New self-contained stage `python -m model_lab.feature_set` (`model_lab/feature_set.py`) — the 8th pipeline stage, wired after `dataset` into `run_all`/`verify`/README. Reads `out/dataset.parquet` for the vetted sample skeleton (ids/framing/labels/split — never re-derived) and **rebuilds the per-second grids from raw sources**, attaching engineered features via backward `merge_asof` on `(asset, sample_ts_ms)` → 1:1 row-aligned `out/feature_set.parquet` + `out/feature_set/{SCHEMA.md, sanity.json, sanity.html, metadata.json}`. **16 features / 36 output cols:** `ret_{1,5,15,60}s`, `sigma_fast`(hl10)/`sigma_slow`(hl60=engine σ_1s)/`vol_ratio`, `flow_imb_{30,120,300}s`, `trade_intensity_{30,120,300}s`, `z=log(mid/strike)/(σ_slow·√τ)`, `seconds_remaining`(=τ), `hour_of_day`. **One config** = frozen `FeatureSpec` dataclass list expanded from knob lists `RETURN_HORIZONS_SECS`/`FLOW_LOOKBACKS_SECS`/`VOL_HALFLIVES` (adding a horizon/lookback = one-line edit); that single list drives the output columns, parquet schema, dtype coercion, `SCHEMA.md`, and the sanity bounds/coverage. **Signed aggressor flow + trade intensity are aggTrades-only** (`is_buyer_maker == True` ⇒ SELL-side aggressor ⇒ `signed = −quantity`, pinned by a unit test; the journal's Binance `Trade` price_tick has no side/size); reuse `binance_archive.read_aggtrades_columns` + dataset's `_month_batches`/`_load_month_bars`. **Two deliberate treatments:** returns on a gap-free ffilled per-second reindex (positive look-back via numpy slicing, nulled when the contiguous run `_run_lengths` ≤ h, reset across gaps > `MAX_GAP_SECS`=30); vols on the **raw** bars (two `lm.sigma_1s_from_bars`, slow hl=60 reproduces the engine σ_1s). `flow_imb = Σsigned/Σ|q|` **clipped to [−1,1]** (independent rolling sums overshoot the exact bound by ~1e-12 float noise — 35/1.04M real rows); `trade_intensity = count/L`. **No-look-ahead (same three guards as dataset):** the 8 grid kernels (`build_price_grid`, `mid_return_grid`, `ewma_vol_grid`, `_run_lengths`, `build_flow_grid`, `_flow_rolls`, `_attach_feature_set`, `_derive_scalar_features`) appended to `tests/test_dataset.py::_FEATURE_FUNCS` (AST scan covers them free); differential/corruption tests (journal mid path + aggTrades-proxy mid+flow path + a flow-only control) in new `tests/test_feature_set.py`; runtime `_assert_no_lookahead` per batch. **Distribution sanity report** (`sanity.{json,html}`, column-streamed 2nd pass, bounded memory): per-column NaN/outlier/constant with PASS/WARN/FAIL. **Expected-NaN aware** (operator-chosen): "no NaN" judged on *excess* (unexpected) NaN via exact per-family masks (`mid_fresh`/`flow_covered`/`price_run_secs` diag cols) — warmup + out-of-aggTrades-coverage NaN are expected and left in the parquet (a learner must tell real from missing); hard-bounded families (`flow_imb`/`z`/time) FAIL on any violation, soft WARN ≤0.5%; a constant **full**-coverage feature FAILs (bug), a constant **partial** one WARNs (coverage artifact — e.g. the fixture's 1-trade/sec `trade_intensity`). `main` returns 1 on any FAIL. **Memory:** chainlink in one pass; binance_proxy streamed per `(symbol, month)` mirroring dataset — only the light skeleton (~1M rows) held whole. **Drift guard:** metadata cross-checks rebuilt `mid`/`σ_slow`/`z` vs the skeleton. **No new deps** (numpy/pandas/pyarrow/matplotlib already pinned); no Rust / no config-crate change. **Verified (venv):** `pytest -q` **55 passed** (41 prior + 14 new incl. the extended AST scan); `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (`… → dataset → feature_set → validate → …`); over the real journal + 90-day aggTrades store → **1,037,640 samples** (1,680 chainlink / 1,035,960 binance_proxy), **sanity PASS 16/0/0**, distributions sane (`flow_imb` exactly [−1,1] post-clip, `ret_60s` p1/p99 ±0.0019, `trade_intensity_30s` p50 ~6/s, `hour_of_day` 0–23, z 99.99% finite). **Cross-check finding:** the **1.036M proxy rows (frozen aggTrades inputs) diverge from the dataset skeleton by EXACTLY 0** (bit-identical mid/σ/z) — proving the rebuild+attach+derive logic is exact; the only divergence is a handful of **chainlink** rows in the last ~5 windows (σ_slow Δ 7.8e-6, mid Δ ~1 USD at z≈10, model-irrelevant), growing with wall-clock — the signature of the **live-appending journal vs the frozen `dataset.parquet` snapshot** (the bot runs 24/7), not a logic bug. **Note:** `out/` is git-ignored/regenerable; `out/dataset.parquet` was truncated by an interrupted cross-check regen and rebuilt. **Deferred:** consuming `feature_set.parquet` in a learner; cyclic hour encoding / extra families (one-line adds); a clean back-to-back dataset+feature_set cross-check once the 24/7 journal is paused. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab `feature_set` hardening — parquet-ready guard, warmup-mask off-by-one fix, cross-check on the grown data.** Re-ran `dataset`→`feature_set` end-to-end after the data grew (24/7 journal: chainlink 84→**263 windows** / 1,680→**5,260 samples**; aggTrades store extended ~90d→**~18 months**: proxy 51,798→**~311k windows** / **6,219,960 samples**) → **dataset == feature_set == 6,225,220 rows, 1:1 exact** on `(series, window_open_ms, sample_ts_ms)`. **Definitive cross-check (6.22M proxy rows): max|Δmid| = max|Δz| = max|Δσ_slow| = EXACTLY 0** — bit-identical to the skeleton, proving the rebuild+attach+derive path is exact; only **9 of 5,260 chainlink** rows diverge (≤ tail 5 windows, σ Δ 6e-6, mid Δ ~1 USD at z≈10), the live-appending-journal-vs-frozen-snapshot skew, not a bug. **(1) Warmup-mask off-by-one fix** (`_expected_nan_mask`): `sigma_1s_from_bars` needs `warmup` **folds** = `run_length − 1` (a run's first bar yields no return), so σ/vol_ratio/z are NaN while `run ≤ warmup` — the mask used `run < warmup`, missing the exact boundary bar of every fresh run, which surfaced as a *tiny* excess-NaN WARN on the 18-month data (per-month streamed grids restart the EWMA). Now `<=` → excess-NaN **exactly 0** for σ/vol_ratio/z; sanity **PASS→ still WARN overall but 13 PASS / 3 WARN / 0 FAIL**, the 3 WARN being legitimate soft-outliers (`ret_60s` 8 rows |>5%/60s| = real crypto extremes, `trade_intensity_30s/120s` a few flash-burst seconds — honest fat tails, not bugs; ret masks were already `<=` and correct). **(2) Parquet-ready guard** (operator-requested — no stage may silently consume a truncated / still-being-written parquet): new `config.{ParquetNotReady(RuntimeError), assert_parquet_ready(path, *, label, min_rows=1) -> int, require_tables(paths, *names, min_rows=1)}` — reads **only the footer** (`pq.ParquetFile(p).metadata`, cheap, no data pages), raising a clear actionable message on absent / no-footer (truncated or mid-write) / under-min-rows files, else returns the row count. Wired into **every** parquet-consuming stage *function* (so all callers — CLI, `run_all`, `verify` — are covered): `feature_set`(dataset), `features`(ticks), `labels`(features min1 + settlements/windows footer-only min0), `validate`(model/ticks min1 + window_labels min0), `research`(features/labels); `min_rows=0` = footer-only for legitimately-sparse inputs. `feature_set.main` catches `ParquetNotReady` → clean `[feature_set] …` message + exit 1 (not a traceback). Prompted by an interrupted `dataset` regen (harness ~2-min background-command cap; the multi-GB 24/7 journal makes `dataset` slow) truncating `out/dataset.parquet` — **`out/` is git-ignored/regenerable**; restored via a detached `Start-Process` that escapes the cap. **Verified (venv):** `pytest -q` **57 passed** (55 + 2 new guard tests: bad-file rejection incl. missing/truncated/under-min-rows/valid, and `feature_set` refusing a truncated `dataset.parquet` via both the function guard and a clean `main` exit); `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (guard adds no pipeline regression — all fixture tables validate). No new deps; touches `config.py` + the 5 stage read-points + `tests/test_feature_set.py`. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab evaluation harness — the single source of truth for scoring any model.** New `model_lab/eval_harness.py` (library, no CLI — like `lib/math.py`) + new `model_lab/evaluate.py` stage + `calibration_audit.py` refactored to a thin caller + `lib/math.directional_accuracy` + `fixtures.make_predictions_fixture`; wired into `run_all`/`verify`/`report`; new `tests/test_evaluate.py`. Given *any* model's probability-of-Up outputs on a test period it scores **Brier, log-loss, directional accuracy, and a calibration curve**, **always side-by-side against two benchmarks**: (1) the **formula model** `p_up` recomputed at the same timestamps, (2) the **market-implied** prob (Polymarket Up-token mid `(bid+ask)/2`) on the coverage subset. Emits **one standardized verdict table**: model vs each benchmark, **per calendar-day period** (+ weekly rollup), with **relative-improvement %** and a **stability** summary across periods. Every later model-scoring stage must report through this. **Python-only; NO Rust / no new deps** (numpy/pandas/matplotlib/pyarrow already pinned). **Operator decisions:** (1) test period = **calendar day UTC** (`day = open_time // 86_400_000`, mirrors `crates/analytics` `DayKey`; pure int, no `time` dep) + **weekly `day//7` rollup**; (2) model input contract = **grid parquet keyed `(series, window_open_ms, sample_ts_ms)` + `p_up`** (joins 1:1 onto `dataset`/`feature_set`), **default with no `--predictions` = formula self-baseline** (model-vs-market meaningful, model-vs-formula = identity ~0% sanity check); (3) **route `calibration_audit` through the harness now**. **Architecture:** `eval_harness.py` holds the journal→canonical-frame assembly **moved out of `calibration_audit`** (`read_journal`/`windows_frame`/`market_frame`/`tau_bucket`, made public + new `build_self_baseline_frame` / `build_external_frame`), a **3-predictor scorer** (`model`/`formula`/`market` — Brier/log-loss/dir-acc each, distinct-window `low_sample`), the per-period **verdict table** + `_stability`, `_verdict_text`, shared HTML/CSV/JSON render, and one `run(df, *, counts, min_windows, health, title, model_label, self_baseline)` orchestrator both stages call. **Canonical frame** cols `series, open_time, ts, tau, tau_bucket, day, week, outcome_up, model, formula, market, in_coverage, health`. **Self-baseline path** = per-second journal `model` snapshots (`model = formula = p_up`, `market = up_mid` via backward `merge_asof` + reused `MAX_MID_STALENESS_MS` 120s guard) — journal-only, no prereq. **External path** = anchored on the prediction grid rows; **formula benchmark as-of joined from journal engine `p_up`** (new `FORMULA_STALENESS_MS` 5s) = "recomputed at the same timestamps"; market as-of; outcome from `Resolved` lifecycle (settlement fallback); engine `health` carried from the formula as-of source. **Model-vs-benchmark scored head-to-head** on rows where **both** predictors are defined (`coverage` reported); model-vs-market only where a recorded mid exists. **`_stability`** over non-low-sample daily periods → `win_rate`, `mean/std_brier_improve_pct`, `reliably_better` (= `win_rate ≥ RELIABLE_WIN_RATE 0.6` AND mean > 0). **`calibration_audit`** now = `build_self_baseline_frame` → `eval_harness.run(..., title="Calibration audit", self_baseline=True)`; keeps its `calibration_audit(paths, min_windows, health)` worker + `main()` signatures + journal-only no-prereq property; **return dict is a strict superset** (scope_rows still carry `model_brier`/`model_logloss`/`market_brier`/`market_logloss` → `verify`/`run_all`/`test_smoke` consumers unchanged; new `formula_*`/`*_diracc`/`verdict_table` added). **Outputs** (both stages, `out/{stage}/`): `metrics.json`, **`verdict_table.csv` (new; day+week rows)**, `scores.csv` (extended: `formula_*` + `*_diracc` cols), `reliability.csv` (gains `formula` predictor), `report.html` (per-benchmark verdict tables + per-period edge plot + model/formula/market reliability). Aggregate `report.py` folds a compact evaluate section (`_load_json(out/evaluate/metrics.json)`). **`make_predictions_fixture`** = deterministic **oracle-nudge** grid parquet (formula `p_up` pushed `edge` toward the *realized* outcome ⇒ provably `model_brier < formula_brier`) so `verify`/tests get a clean numeric invariant (peeks at the label — harness exercise only, never a real model). **Wiring:** `run_all` runs `evaluate` (self-baseline) between `research` and `report`; `verify` adds self-baseline identity (`model_brier == formula_brier`) + external-predictions-beats-formula (`min_windows=5`) checks + banner (`…→research→evaluate→report`). **Tests:** `test_evaluate.py` (self-baseline identity, external beats formula, output schema == module col lists, determinism, `ParquetNotReady` on a truncated preds file, multi-day stability via direct `eh.run` on a synthetic frame, market coverage-subset); `test_math.py` `directional_accuracy` (perfect/none/ties/tie_is_up/empty); `test_smoke.py` updated for the superset shape + `verdict_table.csv`. **Reusable programmatic entry** `evaluate.evaluate_predictions(paths, predictions_df, ...)` for the coming learner. **Verified (venv `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`):** `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (chain now `…→calibration_audit→research→evaluate→report`); `pytest -q` **65 passed** (64 prior-ish + new; 14 pre-existing matplotlib↔pyparsing third-party warnings). Real-journal `evaluate` run bottlenecked by the multi-GB live-appending 24/7 journal (full scan > 7 min, same property as `calibration_audit`/`dataset`) — determinism proven on fixtures; real-data headline pending a bounded run. **Deferred:** routing `validate`/`research` through the harness too; `config::EngineParams → harness params` map; the learner producing a real `predictions.parquet` to score; a bounded/`--since` journal window for the harness on the giant live journal. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab `learn` stage — first challenger: walk-forward logistic regression (the lab's first trained + persisted model).** New `model_lab/lib/logreg.py` + `model_lab/learn.py` + `tests/test_learn.py`; wired into `run_all`/`verify`/README; one behavior-preserving refactor of `eval_harness.py`. An **L2-regularized logistic regression** (hand-written numpy — the lab has **no scikit-learn/scipy**) on the 16 `feature_set` features, trained to predict **two targets separately**: `fwd_up_30s` (30-second mid direction) and `outcome_up` (window resolution). **Evaluation = strict walk-forward**, reported **through the harness** (`eval_harness`), with a **shuffled-label control**; the model artifact + a reproducibility seed are saved. **Python-only; NO Rust / no new deps** (numpy/pandas/pyarrow already pinned). **Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion):** (1) the 30-second model is routed through the harness (its Up-prob scored vs the **window** outcome — a "does the short-horizon signal predict the window?" transfer view) **and additionally** scored on its own `fwd_up_30s` label with the same metric primitives; (2) train/test over the **full ~90-day span** = Binance-proxy history + the real journal (Chainlink) windows, the harness self-restricting to the journal windows where the formula+market benchmarks exist. **`lib/logreg.py`** (reusable, unit-tested, numeric-primitives-in-`lib/` like `lib/math.py`): stable `sigmoid` (via `e^{-|x|}`, no overflow), **NaN-aware** `standardize_fit`/`standardize_apply` (fit over finite entries only — no `np.nanmean` warning on all-NaN cols; apply imputes NaN→0 = the standardized mean, so `feature_set`'s legitimately-missing flow/intensity/warmup NaN never drop a row and an all-missing feature contributes nothing), `fit_logistic_ridge` (**IRLS/Newton**, `(XᵀWX+λI)Δ=grad`, bias **unpenalized**, `W=p(1−p)` floored, `np.linalg.solve`+`lstsq` fallback; **zero-init ⇒ deterministic**; ridge bounds separable data), `predict_proba`. **Decisions (left to me, §2.8):** (a) **one pooled model per fold across all series** (per-series models + cyclic `hour_of_day` encoding deferred; `hour_of_day` is a plain numeric feature). (b) **walk-forward** = sliding **trailing** train window of `--train-weeks·7` days → test the next `--test-days` block, roll by `test_days`; `day = window_open_ms // 86_400_000` (mirrors `crates/analytics` `DayKey`). **Purge ≥ label horizon at every boundary:** drop train rows with `label_info_ms > test_start_ms − purge_secs·1000`, where `label_info_ms = sample_ts+30s` (fwd30) or `window_close_ms` (outcome); `--purge-secs` default = the label horizon (30 s / the window length 300 s). **Adaptive fallback** to a single chronological ~80/20-by-day split when the span is too short (keeps the tiny-fixture `verify` green — the fixture spans only ~4 UTC days). Test blocks are disjoint ⇒ each row is predicted exactly once out-of-sample; OOS grids pooled. (c) **Harness efficiency refactor:** scoring 2 grids/target (real + shuffled) over the **multi-GB live journal** would re-read it 4× — so `eval_harness` gained `load_benchmarks(paths)` (read journal once → `{windows, market, model}` ctx) + `build_external_frame_from(ctx, preds)`, and `build_external_frame` now **delegates** (behavior-preserving; benefits every future model-scoring stage). `learn` reads the journal **once** and reuses the ctx across all grids. The harness scores model-vs-formula-vs-market on the journal (Chainlink) subset; the 30-second model **additionally** gets own-label pooled Brier/log-loss/dir-acc + `reliability_table` (the native eval the harness, hardwired to `outcome_up`, can't express). (d) **Shuffled control** = permute the label column once (`np.random.default_rng(seed)`), rerun the *identical* walk-forward; `collapsed` verdict = shuffled OOS Brier ≥ chance Brier (base-rate `p(1−p)`)−0.03 **AND** real Brier < shuffled **AND** not harness-`reliably_better` than formula. (e) **Artifacts (`out/learn/`)** — the lab's **first persisted models**, inspectable JSON (no pickle/joblib/npz): `model_<target>.json` (`feature_names`, `standardize:{mean,std}`, `coef`, `intercept`, `l2`, **`seed`**, `train_span`, `class_balance`) — round-trips to `predict_proba`; `predictions_<target>.parquet` (+ `_shuffled`) on the `(series, window_open_ms, sample_ts_ms, p_up)` harness contract; `harness_<target>/` (+ `_shuffled/`) = the 5 harness files each; `metrics.json` (config+seed, per-target `{folds[], pooled OOS, own-label, harness summary, final_model}`, `shuffled_control` collapse verdict, caveats); `folds.csv`. **Reproducibility seed** `DEFAULT_SEED=20260704` + `--seed`, saved into every artifact + `metrics.json`; training deterministic (IRLS zero-init), the seed drives the shuffle. **Data loading** = pyarrow predicate-pushdown on `window_open_ms` (read the column, `--days`-cutoff, filtered read of the needed cols) so only the trailing window loads from the 831 MB matrix. **Flags:** `--days`(90)/`--train-weeks`(4)/`--test-days`(7)/`--purge-secs`(per-target)/`--l2`(1.0)/`--seed`/`--targets`(fwd30,outcome)/`--source`(all|chainlink|binance_proxy)/`--series`/`--min-windows`(50)/`--health`/`--no-harness`/`--no-shuffle-control`. `run_all` runs `learn` after `feature_set`; `verify` asserts the wiring/artifacts/contract + banner (`…→feature_set→learn→validate→…`); `test_learn.py` (12) covers `sigmoid`/standardize/`fit_logistic_ridge`, `_fold_schedule` chronology+non-overlap+fallback, `_fold_masks` purge (boundary leakage dropped), **real-beats-chance + shuffled-collapse** on a synthetic predictive matrix, determinism (same seed → identical model+predictions), the contract+1:1 join, artifact round-trip, `ParquetNotReady` guard, and a fixture-journal harness end-to-end. **Verified (venv `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`):** `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (chain now `…→feature_set→learn→validate→…`); `pytest -q` **77 passed** (65 prior + 12 new; 14 pre-existing matplotlib↔pyparsing third-party warnings). **Real-data smoke** (real `out/feature_set.parquet` 831 MB/6.2 M rows, `--days 90 --no-harness` → 1,033,900 rows over 91 days, seed 20260704): **outcome** walk-forward **9 folds / 718,660 OOS → Brier 0.166, dir-acc 0.741** (strongly beats chance — the formula `z`/vol/τ genuinely predict the window), shuffled control **collapsed=True** (Brier 0.250, dir-acc 0.501); **fwd30** 9 folds / 578,227 OOS → Brier 0.249, dir-acc 0.531 (barely above chance — short-horizon direction is near-efficient, an honest negative-ish result), shuffled **collapsed=True** (Brier 0.249). **Real-journal harness verdict** (`--targets outcome`, live 24/7 journal read once → **5,260 snapshots / 257 Chainlink windows**): the challenger **does NOT beat the formula** — model Brier **0.295** / dir-acc 0.606 vs **formula 0.136** (−118 %) and **market 0.134** (−121 %), `reliably_better=False`; the shuffled control likewise not reliably better (collapsed). An **honest, valuable negative result**: a logistic regression on these features trained on the Binance-**proxy** history scores well on proxy OOS (0.166) but can't match the formula's sharp near-expiry calibration on the real windows — exactly the verdict the harness exists to surface (the deliverable is the leakage-safe pipeline + honest scoring, not a winning model). **Bug found+fixed via the real run:** the CLI `main()` summary crashed formatting the harness block (`_fmt(x, 1)` — the helper didn't forward the precision arg); `learn()`/artifacts were unaffected (written before the print). Fixed `_fmt(x, *args)` + a `test_learn` case that runs `learn.main([...])` with the harness so the print path is covered. **Deferred:** per-series models + cyclic time encoding; feature selection / non-linear challengers (all still report through the same harness); `config::EngineParams → learn params` map; a bounded/`--since` journal window for the harness on the giant live journal; wiring a validated signal back into the engine's fair value. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab `learn_gbt` stage — second challenger: walk-forward LightGBM, plain + formula-residual variants + a comparison report.** New `model_lab/learn_common.py` (shared scaffolding extracted from `learn.py`) + `model_lab/lib/gbt.py` + `model_lab/learn_gbt.py` + `model_lab/compare.py` + `tests/test_learn_gbt.py`/`test_compare.py`/`test_learn_common.py` + `requirements-gbt.txt`; wired `--with-gbt` into `run_all`, `[gbt]` extra into `pyproject.toml`, README updated. **LightGBM** on the same 16 `feature_set` features, same two targets (`fwd_up_30s`/`outcome_up`), same strict walk-forward + purge + shuffled control + harness reporting as `learn`. **Opt-in dependency (operator choice, AskUserQuestion):** LightGBM is a compiled OpenMP lib pulling `scipy` — a departure from the lab's "pure-wheel, no scipy/sklearn" core — so it ships as an **optional extra** (`requirements-gbt.txt` / `pip install -e .[gbt]`, both pinned `lightgbm==4.6.0`+`scipy==1.18.0`); `lib/gbt.py` is the **only** module importing lightgbm (clean `ImportError` if absent), `learn_gbt`/`compare` are standalone one-command stages, tests use `pytest.importorskip("lightgbm")`, and the **core pipeline / `verify` / smoke stay lightgbm-free** (proven: `import model_lab.verify` leaves `lightgbm` out of `sys.modules`; `--with-gbt` and the two module imports in `run_all` are lazy/guarded). **Both formula variants (operator choice, AskUserQuestion):** a design check (Plan agent) found that adding `Φ(z)` as a literal 17th feature is a **no-op for trees** (invariant to monotone transforms of `z`, already feature #14), so the formula is injected **two ways and compared** on the `outcome` target — **plain** (16-feature GBT, no prior) and **residual** (formula supplied as LightGBM `init_score` base margin `logit(Φ(z))`; trees learn only the correction; `predict` doesn't carry *test* base margins so `predict_proba` re-adds them: `sigmoid(base+raw)`). `fwd30` gets plain only (`Φ(z)` predicts the window outcome, not 30-s direction); `VARIANTS_BY_TARGET={outcome:(plain,residual), fwd30:(plain,)}`. No `feature_set` schema change (stays 16 features). **Tuning is forward-chained inside the training window, never on test:** each fold carves an inner validation split = the latest `inner_val_frac` (0.25) of the training rows by `sample_ts_ms`, **ms-granular purge** (drop inner-train with `label_info_ms > inner_val_start_ms − purge_ms`), early-stops rounds on it, then **refits the full train window** for `best_iteration` rounds and predicts OOS; the deployable `_final_model` uses the **median fold best_iteration**. **Determinism = a hard contract** (mirrors `learn`): `gbt.default_params` sets `deterministic=True`+`force_row_wise=True`+`num_threads=1`+`bagging/feature_fraction=1.0`+all four seeds; **bit-identical booster (`model_to_string`) + predictions across seeded runs** (verified in-wrapper and via the stage's determinism test). `best_iteration==0` guarded (LightGBM `0`≡"all trees" → callers pass `None`). Modest-depth/strong-reg defaults: `num_leaves=15, max_depth=4, lr=0.03, min_child_samples=100, reg_lambda=5, max_bin=127, max_boost_round=2000, early_stopping=50`. **GBT-specific analyses beyond the harness scorecard (the real deliverable — "does GBT add anything the formula lacks, and where"):** per-final-model **feature importances** (gain+split → csv + `metrics.json`), **vol regime** (median `sigma_slow` split, low/high, GBT vs `Φ(z)` Brier/logloss/diracc), **time-remaining buckets** (`eh.tau_bucket`), and a **residual analysis** — disagreement `|p_gbt − Φ(z)| > 0.05`, cross-tabbed by `tau_bucket × |z|` bucket (`[0,.5)/[.5,1)/[1,2)/[2,∞)`), reporting per cell n / mean|d| / GBT-vs-formula Brier+diracc / `gbt_better`; all filtered to finite-`z` (past warmup), outcome target only. Strict 4-col harness grid `predictions_<target>_<variant>.parquet` preserved; enriched analysis columns (`z/sigma_slow/τ/dur/p_up_formula/y_true`) go in a separate `oos_*.parquet`. Artifacts `out/learn_gbt/`: `model_<t>_<v>.txt` (native booster) + `.json` (params+importances+seed), `predictions_*`/`oos_*`, `harness_*/`, `feature_importance/regime/residual_*.csv`, `folds.csv`, `metrics.json` (nested `targets[t][v]` + `shuffled_control[t][v]`; every `json.dumps(default=eh._json_default)` — importances are numpy). **`compare.py`** (one command, operator asked for it): the harness is single-model (`build_external_frame_from` hard-selects one `p_up→model`), so it builds **one** canonical frame from the GBT-plain grid (brings in `outcome_up`+formula+market+day/week/tau), **key-joins** logistic + gbt_residual `p_up` by `(series,open_time,ts)`, applies the `health=Ready` gate, and scores `{logistic, gbt_plain, gbt_residual, formula, market}` per day/week/ALL/tau with `lm.*` primitives — formula/market **pairwise-defined** (coverage reported, no 4-way common set claimed); reports `n_common` + warns on grid misalignment; writes `comparison.csv`/`metrics.json`/`report.html` + one-line verdict. **Architecture:** `learn_common.py` holds the model-agnostic shared bits (`_fold_schedule/_fold_masks/_fold_metrics/_label_info_ms/_load_matrix/_parse_series/_harness_grid/_harness_summary/TARGETS/MS_PER_DAY` + generalized `score_through_harness(subdir=…)`); `learn.py` re-imports them (behavior-preserving — `test_learn.py`'s `from model_lab.learn import _fold_schedule,…` still resolves; all 12 `test_learn` tests green after). **Verified (venv `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`, lightgbm installed):** `pytest -q` **91 passed** (77 prior + 14 new), `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (unchanged core chain), `run_all --with-gbt` green end-to-end on the fixture. **Real-data smoke** (real `out/feature_set.parquet` 6.22 M rows, `--days 30 --targets outcome --no-harness`, 342,700 rows / 31 days, 27,460 OOS): **outcome/plain Brier 0.1773, dir-acc 0.7338** — and the headline residual finding — **GBT disagrees with the formula on 22% of snapshots and there is MORE accurate (Brier 0.2064 vs formula 0.2218, `gbt_better=True`)**; residual variant Brier 0.1780, disagrees 15%, also `gbt_better=True` in-disagreement. A concrete, positive answer to "where does GBT add signal the formula lacks" (the full per-period harness verdict vs formula/market on real data needs the multi-GB journal read, deferred like the other stages). **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → {gbt hyperparams, compare}` maps; per-series GBT models; a bounded/`--since` journal window for the harness; wiring a validated GBT signal back into engine fair value. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab feature-layer fix: basis-corrected `z` + a permanent offline-vs-live guard (root cause of the challengers' real-slice under-performance).** A forensic session found the GBT/logistic challengers scored ~0.29 Brier / 0.60 dir-acc on the real Chainlink slice vs the journaled formula's ~0.14, and diagnosed it (not an inference bug, not compression) to a **feature defect**: the offline `z = log(mid/strike)/(σ√τ)` used the fast **Binance** mid against the **Chainlink** strike with **no basis correction**, so near expiry the uncorrected ~13 bps Binance↔Chainlink basis (÷σ√τ→∞) made Φ(feature z) confidently wrong — Φ(feature z) itself scored ~0.31 Brier flat across all τ buckets, tying the challengers. The engine's *journaled* p_up applies its live basis tracker; the feature layer did not. **Fix (Parts 1+3):** (1) new causal **rolling log-basis** kernel in `features.build_asset_features` → `basis_ewma = EWMA(ln(chainlink/mid), halflife=120s, min_periods=30, ignore_na)` (offline analogue of the engine basis tracker; NaN ⇒ 0 on same-feed `binance_proxy` rows), added to `features.FEATURE_COLS` + `dataset.COLUMN_SPEC` (feature) + `_attach_features` value_cols. (2) **`z` basis-corrected in ONE consistent definition on both sources**: `z = (log(mid/strike) + basis_ewma)/(σ√τ)` clipped ±39, applied in **both** `dataset._derive_asof_features` **and** `feature_set._derive_scalar_features` (the models consume the feature_set-rebuilt z; `basis_ewma` added to `feature_set._SKELETON_READ_COLS`, read-not-renamed, so the z cross-check stays consistent). Proxy basis=0 ⇒ proxy z bit-identical; only chainlink z changes. Did **not** feed the journaled engine p_up as a feature/anchor (absent on proxy ⇒ new covariate shift). The GBT-residual anchor `logit(Φ(feature z))` is corrected automatically. AST-scan / differential / no-lookahead all still green (the EWMA lives inside already-registered `build_asset_features`; causal). (3) **Permanent guard** (`verify.check_basis_offline_vs_live` + `basis_calibration_by_bucket`, wired into `verify.run_checks` so `test_smoke` exercises it, + new `tests/test_basis_guard.py` with a **negative control**): on chainlink windows Φ(basis-corrected feature z) must track the journaled engine p_up per τ-bucket (Brier gap ≤ 0.03, corr ≥ 0.97) — a reverted/broken basis correction now fails loudly ("never discoverable only by a forensic session again"). **Fixture proof:** `basis_ewma = ln(1+BASIS) = −0.001331` exactly, cross-check z-diff **0.0**, Φ(corrected z) reproduces journaled p_up **EXACTLY** (|ΔBrier|=0.0000 corr=1.0000 every bucket) while uncorrected diverges (|Δ|→0.10, corr→0.90 at final_20s). `pytest -q` **93 passed** (91 + 2 guard); `verify` PASS with `…→feature_set→basis-guard→learn→…`. **Part 2 (flow/intensity NaN on chainlink) — NOT a code fix:** the chainlink flow path already sources aggTrades (`feature_set.py:581-598`); the 84 % NaN is a **Binance-archive publish lag** — the 2026-07-03 chainlink windows' daily aggTrades archive is not published yet (`hist` backfill returns 404), while the 2026-06-22 windows (840 rows) already have flow. Fixed by re-running `hist` once Binance publishes, not by editing code. **Part 4 retrial:** the exact production regen **OOMs this 16 GB box** (`dataset._read_journal` holds all ~145 M ticks of the 3.2 GB / 12-day journal), so the retrial used a **detached** `Start-Process` (escapes the 10-min bg-cap) for one journal read → cached benchmarks (631 windows / 4.3 M journaled p_up / 9.7 M market mids), then re-scored on the real chainlink slice with a **rolling-basis stand-in** from the existing dataset's `chainlink`/`mid` (median basis −0.001127; production 1s-EWMA proven exact on the fixture). **Before→after, Φ(feature z) Brier on the real slice:** early 0.333→0.190, mid 0.314→**0.119** (journaled 0.120), final_minute 0.303→**0.054** (0.056), final_20s 0.307→**0.042** (0.044) — Φ(feature z) now **reproduces the journaled engine formula**, sharpening toward expiry (dir-acc 0.60→0.94). **Challengers (5,260 chainlink snaps / 263 windows):** logistic 0.2964→**0.1458**, gbt_plain 0.2839→**0.1411**, gbt_residual 0.2889→**0.1408** — from catastrophic to **at parity with market 0.135 / journaled ~0.13**. **Disagreement pockets on clean features (honest reversal):** GBT-plain disagrees with Φ(corrected z) on 19 % and is **no longer better** (0.1548 vs 0.1526) — small GBT wins mid-window (|z|∈[0.5,2)), the formula dominates every final_minute/final_20s bucket; the pre-fix "GBT beats formula in disagreement" was an artifact of the broken formula. **Files:** `features.py`, `dataset.py`, `feature_set.py`, `verify.py`, new `tests/test_basis_guard.py`. **Deferred:** rerun the exact production regen on a ≥32 GB box (or a streaming/bounded `_read_journal`) to replace the stand-in; backfill 2026-07-03 aggTrades once published to close the flow NaN; wire the basis-corrected z into the engine only if a challenger ever earns it. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab `short_horizon` stage — dense-grid short-horizon dataset (10s/15s forward labels + per-sample recording coverage).** New self-contained stage `python -m model_lab.short_horizon` (`model_lab/short_horizon.py`) → `out/short_horizon.parquet` (+ `out/short_horizon/{SCHEMA.md, metadata.json}`), a **companion to `dataset`** focused on short horizons. Same self-contained window alignment, as-of feature kernel (incl. **basis-corrected `z`**), and month-by-month streaming over **both** sources (journal Chainlink + Binance aggTrades proxy); the only new code is the labels, the coverage marks, and the per-day report. **Python-only; NO Rust; no new deps** (numpy/pandas/pyarrow already pinned); `dataset.py` **not modified** (imported as `ds` — the blessed cross-module private-import pattern `feature_set.py` already uses). **Design decisions:** (1) **grid default 5s** (operator choice via AskUserQuestion; `--grid-secs`, `1` for max density) — 60 samples/5-min window, ~19M rows / ~1.1 GB over 18 months; 1s ⇒ ~95M/~5.7 GB (heavy overlap), 15s = light. (2) **Two forward labels** `fwd_up_10s`/`fwd_up_15s` = sign of `log(mid(t+h)/mid(t))` on a gap-free 1s Binance-mid grid, ties→Up, NA if `+h` unobserved (new `forward_direction_labels_multi` — the only new future-reading fn, kept OUT of the AST `_FEATURE_FUNCS`); window `outcome`/`outcome_up` carried as free secondary labels. (3) **Per-sample coverage** `depth_covered`/`book_covered` (category `coverage`, **not** features) = does **our own** Binance `depth20@100ms` capture (`data/depth` `recv_ms`, per asset) resp. Polymarket `top_of_book` capture (per window's up/down `token_id`, `top.ts`) have a frame within `--coverage-tolerance-secs` (default 2s) of the sample — vectorized searchsorted (`_covered`), the per-sample analogue of `dataset._coverage_flags`. **`binance_proxy` rows → both False** (our captures predate the historical archive; the public aggTrades data is not "our recording"). Coverage may reference a recording just after the sample, so it is provenance metadata **exempt** from the no-look-ahead rule (like `in_live_coverage`); `FEATURE_COLS.isdisjoint(COVERAGE_COLS)` asserted at import + runtime. (4) **Own `COLUMN_SPEC`** (38 cols) = dataset's id/meta/feature rows verbatim + the 4 short labels + 2 coverage flags + `fwd_crosses_close_{10,15}s`; **replicated** `_assert_no_lookahead` (dataset's binds to *its* module globals — the 30s labels) + a **local `_sample_points`** that retains `up_token`/`down_token` (dataset's drops them, needed for per-sample book coverage). (5) **Per-day report** (`sample_ts_ms // 86_400_000` → UTC date): sample counts + depth/book coverage % per day in `metadata.json` + printed (journal days in full, historical-proxy days summarized as one 0%-coverage line to avoid ~550-line spam) + overall coverage %. (6) **Journal (chainlink) subset byte-identical** with vs without history (journal batch written first, colliding proxy windows dropped, arrow schema pinned per write, `dtype_backend="pyarrow"` reads). **Wiring:** `run_all` runs it after `dataset`; `verify.run_checks` gained a short_horizon block (samples>0, both label domains⊆{0,1}, coverage cols bool, depth-covered *mix* + some book-covered, per_day non-empty, journal-subset-unchanged) + banner (`…→dataset→short_horizon→feature_set→…`); README stage §4b + layout. **Look-ahead guards extended:** `test_dataset._FORBIDDEN_LABELS` gained the 4 short-label names (the shared feature funcs must never reference them; `_FEATURE_FUNCS` unchanged since features are reused verbatim); new `tests/test_short_horizon.py` (11) mirrors the three guards — smoke+schema, **differential/perturbation** over `FEATURE_COLS` (journal+proxy, three-world bit-identity, coverage excluded), a **positive "labels react to the future"** test (perturbing mid at t+10/t+15 flips `fwd_up_10s/15s` via `_attach_labels`, proving the differential isn't vacuous), tie/NA rule, coverage correctness (nearby vs far tick, proxy→False), per-day+coverage-% reconcile, journal-subset-unchanged, `--no-history`, determinism. **Verified (venv `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`):** `pytest -q` **104 passed** (93 prior + 11 new; 14 pre-existing matplotlib↔pyparsing third-party warnings), `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (chain now includes `short_horizon`), and the CLI end-to-end on the fixture (20 journal + aggTrades proxy) → 70,320 samples at grid 5s (60/window), journal day depth 100% / book 99% coverage, proxy days 0%, journal subset unchanged. **Deferred:** consuming `short_horizon.parquet` in a learner (short-horizon challenger scored through the same harness); a research stage on the 10/15s labels; `config::EngineParams → short_horizon params` map. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-04 — **model-lab `short_horizon` gains depth + Polymarket-book microstructure features + a distribution sanity report.** The `short_horizon` stage now turns our own recordings into real features (previously they only drove the `depth_covered`/`book_covered` coverage flags), **computed only where a recording covers the timestamp** — missing-coverage rows get honest **`NaN`, never build-time imputation**, and all are `NaN` on `binance_proxy` rows. **Python-only; NO Rust; no new deps** (numpy/pandas/pyarrow/matplotlib already pinned); **`dataset.py` / `features.py` / `feature_set.py` NOT modified** (feature_set imported lazily only for pure render helpers). `COLUMN_SPEC` 38→55. **New features (16 cols + 1 diag):** depth (per asset, from the Binance `depth20@100ms` capture) — multi-level book imbalance `depth_imb_{1,5,10,20}` (operator-chosen level set), `microprice_gap` (top-level size-weighted microprice − mid), `bid_depth_slope`/`ask_depth_slope` (cum size per USD of price distance over top `DEPTH_SLOPE_LEVELS`=10; NaN with <2 levels), `depth_spread_bps`; PM (per window **Up** token, from the `top_of_book` capture) — `pm_mid`∈[0,1], `pm_spread`, `pm_book_imb`∈[−1,1], and a **staleness** signal `pm_staleness_{1,2,3}s` = Binance mid log-move minus the PM mid change over the same span. **Config-driven** via module knobs `DEPTH_LEVEL_DEPTHS`/`DEPTH_SLOPE_LEVELS`/`STALENESS_LOOKBACKS_SECS`; the `_depth_feature_spec()`/`_pm_feature_spec()` knob-expansion drives columns/parquet-schema/dtype/SCHEMA.md docs. **Decisions:** (1) **new depth reader `io/depth.read_depth_levels`** (additive) yields the full 20-level ladder as numpy arrays — leaves `parse_frame`/`read_depth_rows`/`DEPTH_COLS` untouched so `ingest`/`dataset`/`feature_set` are unaffected (a 2nd, small-file depth pass). (2) **PM books via a 2nd *filtered streaming* journal pass** `_read_pm_books(paths, up_tokens)` (over `jio.records_of_type("top_of_book")`, one-sided books skipped) — chosen over extending the shared `dataset._read_journal` to keep memory bounded (avoids the documented multi-GB-journal OOM) and shared code untouched; the cost is one extra journal scan (noted as a future single-pass optimization). (3) **All causal:** new depth/PM grids are per-second **last** (`ts_ms=sec·1000`, mirroring `features._depth_bars`) attached via backward `merge_asof` (`_attach_asof` → `depth_feat_asof_ts_ms`/`pm_asof_ts_ms` ≤ sample; stale→nulled); the staleness uses **positive** shifts on a per-token contiguous 1s reindex; proxy batches pass empty grids. Look-ahead enforced three ways — runtime `_assert_no_lookahead` (new asof-≤-sample checks), the AST scan (`test_dataset._FEATURE_FUNCS` += `_side_slope`/`_frame_depth_features`/`build_depth_feature_grid`/`build_pm_grid`/`_attach_asof`), and the differential/perturbation tests (helpers extended to build + corrupt future depth ladders, PM books, and Binance mids; every `FEATURE_COLS` value bit-identical). (4) **Distribution sanity report** `out/short_horizon/sanity.{json,html}` (feature_set-shaped: PASS/WARN/FAIL for unexpected-NaN / absurd-outliers / constant-column) over **all** feature columns (operator choice) — local `SanitySpec`/`_column_sanity`/`sanity_report` (reuses `feature_set` render helpers lazily), a per-family `_expected_nan_mask` classifying legit NaN by coverage; `sigma_1s`-isna is reused as the vol-warmup signal and a new diag `pm_run_secs` classifies the staleness run-start warmup, so the fixture reports a clean **PASS** (28/0/0); `main()` exits non-zero only on FAIL.
