(5) **Fixture (`make_fixture`) extended** to a 20-level **proportional** depth ladder (full-ladder `imbalance` preserved exactly → calibration/research/basis tests unchanged) + varying `top_of_book` half-spread & sizes (non-trivial `pm_spread`/`pm_book_imb`), all drawn from a **separate RNG** (`rng2`) so the primary stream the calibration audit is tuned against is byte-for-byte unchanged. **Verified (venv):** `pytest -q` **110 passed** (104 prior + 6 new microstructure/positive/coverage/`_read_pm_books`/sanity tests; 14 pre-existing matplotlib↔pyparsing warnings), `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS**, CLI on the fixture → 70,920 samples, chainlink depth 100%/book ~99% coverage, proxy 0%, sanity PASS. **Deferred:** consuming the microstructure features in a learner/research stage; folding the PM read into a single journal pass if the extra scan bites; wiring a validated depth/PM signal back into engine fair value. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-05 — **model-lab journal first pass made streaming/bounded (the blocking `_read_journal` OOM fix) + `--since`/`--until` on every journal-reading stage.** The journal grows ~2.5 GB/day; `dataset._read_journal` materialized **every** raw tick (~100/s → tens of GB/day of Python objects), OOMing the 16 GB box (only ~5–7 GB free). **Root fix (`dataset._read_journal`, `stream=True` default):** reduce ticks **on the fly** to exactly what downstream reads — `BinanceDirect`/`Mid` collapsed to **per-second bars** (the last max-ms tick/second, matching `features._bars`) + **all** `ChainlinkRtds` ticks raw (~1/s, the strike needs exact ts); `BinanceDirect`/`Trade` + `BinanceRtds` (read by nothing — verified on real data: 492k Mid, 166k Trade, 11.7k BinanceRtds, 9.8k Chainlink over 72 min) are **dropped**. Memory now grows with the **time range, not the tick rate** (~40× less). **No feature/label logic changed;** the one enabling tweak is `features._bars` sort → `kind="stable"` so same-ms Mid ties resolve to the last-arriving deterministically (was pandas-quicksort-arbitrary) — required so the streamed bar and the full-tick collapse are **bit-identical** rather than merely equal off-ties. `_read_journal(stream=False)` keeps the old full-tick path (for the proof only). **Reduced `ticks_df` proven output-identical:** downstream (`reconstruct_strikes` = Chainlink Vendor raw; `build_feature_grid`→`_binance_mid_bars`/`_chainlink_bars` = per-second bars; `sigma_1s`, labels = grid mid) reads only those two tick kinds. **`--since`/`--until` (+ `--max-rss-mb`) added to `config.add_common_args` → honored by every journal-reading stage** (`dataset`, `short_horizon`, `feature_set`, `ingest`, `calibration_audit`, `evaluate`, `learn`, `learn_gbt`; `eval_harness.read_journal`/`build_self_baseline_frame`/`load_benchmarks` threaded); shared `config.parse_time_bound` (date `2026-07-03` → 00:00Z / ISO datetime / raw unix-ms) + `config._in_bounds` (`[since, until)`, unbounded passes all incl. no-ts, bounded excludes no-ts) filter records by time (ticks by exchange/local ts, windows/settlements by open, books by ts) **identically in stream and full modes**. `ingest` gets bounds but stays raw (it *is* the raw-tick export). New `lib/procmem.peak_rss_mb()` (cross-platform, no psutil — `resource.ru_maxrss` on Unix, `GetProcessMemoryInfo().PeakWorkingSetSize` via ctypes on Windows; the pseudo-handle needs `c_void_p(-1)` + argtypes) + `report_peak_rss(stage, peak, ceiling)`; every journal stage reports peak RSS against the advisory `--max-rss-mb` (default 3072, `DEFAULT_MAX_RSS_MB`) and warns if over. **`_in_bounds` lives in `config`, re-exported through `dataset` so `ds._in_bounds` still resolves for `short_horizon._read_pm_books` (also bounded).** **Proof (new `tests/test_stream_journal.py`, 7 tests):** a synthetic journal with **many ticks/second** (3 Mid + a same-ms tie + Trade + BinanceRtds + Chainlink) → `dataset` and `short_horizon` `stream` vs `--no-stream` produce **byte-identical** parquets (`assert_frame_equal`); the reduction keeps only Mid-bars+Chainlink and is strictly smaller than the full set; `--since`/`--until` filter (2 of 4 windows) and stream==full under a bound; `parse_time_bound`/`_in_bounds` units. **Real-data proof + peak RSS (venv, this 16 GB box):** over a real 72-min slice (492k Mid ticks) `dataset` stream vs `--no-stream` = **byte-identical** 1,560 rows/36 cols, peak RSS **223 MB vs 484 MB**; `short_horizon` = byte-identical 1,560 rows/55 cols, **592 MB**; and streaming `dataset` over the **full 5.2 GB journal** bounded to one day (`--since 2026-07-03 --until 2026-07-04`) → **421 windows / 25,260 samples at peak RSS 2,249 MB** (under the 3,072 MB ceiling; the full-tick path would need ~15–30 GB/day and OOM). Full suite **117 passed** (110 prior + 7 new), `verify` **PASS** (fixture has 1 Mid/s + no Trade/BinanceRtds → reduced==full, so every existing fixture-based test is unaffected). README gained a "Large journals: streaming + `--since`/`--until`" section. **Usage for the day-7 workload:** process a day (or few) at a time via `--since`/`--until`; each day ≈ 2.2 GB, under the ceiling; the reported peak RSS lets you size the range. **Deferred:** segment-filename-based scan-skipping so a bounded run doesn't stream-decompress the whole journal for I/O (memory is already bounded; only scan *time* is full-journal); folding `short_horizon`'s separate PM/depth reads into the bounded first pass; a hard (spilling) memory limiter if a single day ever exceeds the ceiling. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-05 — **model-lab `short_horizon`: feature-presence coverage flags `depth_feat_covered` / `pm_feat_covered`** (COLUMN_SPEC 55→57). The prior microstructure work left only `book_covered` (up-**OR**-down token, **symmetric ±tol** recording provenance) and the `pm_asof_ts_ms` timestamp — so "covered" did **not** mean "the PM features are present": `book_covered` can be True with the PM (Up-token, backward-as-of) features NaN, and vice versa. Since the learner filters training rows by coverage, that mismatch would poison training with NaN rows. Fix: two new **boolean** `coverage`-category flags, `pm_feat_covered = pm_asof_ts_ms.notna()` and `depth_feat_covered = depth_feat_asof_ts_ms.notna()`, derived in `_build_samples` from the **causal** backward-as-of columns (≤ `sample_ts`, within staleness) — so each is **exactly** "the core book features are present" (`pm_feat_covered ⟺ pm_mid`/`pm_spread`/`pm_book_imb`; `depth_feat_covered ⟺ depth_imb_*`/`microprice_gap`). The old `depth_covered`/`book_covered` are kept as recording-provenance (unchanged, backward-compatible), with docs (SCHEMA + module docstring) steering row-filtering to the `*_feat_covered` flags. Derived sub-features that carry their own structural NaN (`pm_staleness_*` in the first N run seconds via `pm_run_secs`; `bid`/`ask_depth_slope` on a <2-level book) are documented as exceptions to the "all features present" reading. Counts/coverage/per-CLI now report `samples_{depth,pm}_feat_covered` + a "features present (filter on these)" line. **Verified (venv):** `pytest -q` **118 passed** (117 + 1 new `test_pm_feat_covered_matches_feature_presence`; the smoke + `verify` assert the row-by-row invariant `*_feat_covered == feature.notna()`); on the real 72-min slice `pm_feat_covered` (93.1%) vs `book_covered` (94.5%) **diverge on 25 rows — 23 are `book_covered`=True with PM features ABSENT** (exactly the rows a `book_covered` filter would wrongly keep), confirming the flag is necessary and correct. No Rust / no new deps. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-05 — **model-lab `learn_short_gbt` stage — GBT challenger on the short-horizon dataset.** New opt-in stage `python -m model_lab.learn_short_gbt` (`model_lab/learn_short_gbt.py`) + `tests/test_learn_short_gbt.py` (11) + wired into `run_all --with-gbt` (after `learn_gbt`) + README subsection/layout/test-list. A deterministic **LightGBM** classifier on `short_horizon.parquet` predicting the short-horizon forward-direction labels **`fwd_up_10s` (primary) / `fwd_up_15s` (secondary)**, scored on its **own** label (dir-acc/Brier/`reliability_table`) — NOT the window outcome. Reuses `lib/gbt.py` (determinism contract) + `learn_common` (`_fold_schedule`/`_fold_metrics`/`_label_info_ms`/`_harness_grid`/`score_through_harness`/`_harness_summary`/`_parse_series`) verbatim; residual-free walk-forward **parametrized by `feature_names`**; own matrix loader (short_horizon has no `in_live_coverage`). Same strict walk-forward + purge (day-granular outer + ms-granular inner-val) + inner-validation round selection + determinism + shuffled-label `collapsed` control as `learn_gbt`. **Python-only; NO Rust; no new deps** (rides the existing `[gbt]` extra). **Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion):** (1) **join `feature_set.parquet` flow** — `short_horizon` has no signed-aggressor-flow columns (`flow_imb_*`/`trade_intensity_*` live only in `feature_set`), so the six are attached by a **causal backward as-of join** per `(series, window_open_ms)` on `sample_ts_ms` (feature_set is on the dataset **15s** grid, short_horizon on **5s** — never an exact key join), `tolerance` `FLOW_ASOF_TOLERANCE_MS=90_000`; look-ahead-safe (matched feature_set ts ≤ sample ts, verified 616/616 windows overlap + `frac_asof_matched=1.0` on the fixture). Raw price *levels* (`mid`/`chainlink`/`microprice`/`depth_mid`) + legacy single-level depth (`imbalance`/`spread`) excluded. `--no-external-flow` ⇒ base = native short-horizon features only. (2) **3-way ablation** `{base, depth, full}` (base=16 price/vol/model+flow → +Binance `depth20` (8) → +Polymarket book (6), FULL=30) so Binance-depth and PM-book contributions isolate separately; microstructure NaN on `binance_proxy`/uncovered rows ⇒ LightGBM native missing-branch. (3) **both source scopes in one run** `("all","chainlink")`, the ablation reported for each. **New analyses:** `_abstention_curve` (coverage-vs-accuracy as `|p−0.5|≥t` rises over 10 thresholds; coverage monotone non-increasing via nested masks) + `_pair_contribution` (base→depth cov=`depth_feat_covered`, depth→full cov=`pm_feat_covered`, base→full; overall/covered/uncovered Brier+dir-acc deltas, 1:1 key join) + `_regime_blocks` (model `p_up` vs naive `p_up_model=Φ(z)` reference, split by median-`sigma_1s` vol / `eh.tau_bucket` time-remaining / source / depth-coverage). **Harness (vs window `outcome_up`) is OPT-IN** `--with-harness` (default OFF — a slow journal read + a different question than the 10/15s label; when on it mirrors `learn_gbt`'s `score_through_harness`). **external_flow metadata is honest:** `n_asof_matched`/`frac_asof_matched` (got an as-of row) vs `n_flow_present`/`frac_flow_present` (matched row carried non-NaN flow — feature_set flow is legitimately NaN where aggTrades coverage/warmup is thin: fixture shows 100% as-of match, 8% flow-present; dense on real data). **Artifacts** `out/learn_short_gbt/` per `tag=<scope>_<target>_<variant>`: `model_<tag>.{txt,json}`, `predictions_<tag>.parquet`(+`_shuffled`), `oos_<tag>.parquet`, `feature_importance/regime/abstention/reliability_<tag>.csv`; once-per-run `depth_contribution.csv`, `folds.csv` (adds `scope` col), `metrics.json` (nested `scopes[scope].{targets,shuffled_control,depth_contribution}`). **NOT in `verify.py`** (stays lightgbm-free, doesn't run `learn_gbt` either); import-time drift guards assert every named feature exists in `short_horizon.FEATURE_COLS`/`COLUMNS` and `feature_set.FEATURE_NAMES`. **Verified (venv):** the 11 new tests **pass** (real-beats-chance + shuffle-collapse across all 3 variants × both scopes, byte-identical determinism, 4-col grid contract + 1:1 join, `ParquetNotReady` guard, abstention monotone, regime, depth-contribution covered-subset, nested/distinct feature sets, external-flow join + `--no-external-flow`, nullable `fwd15` filter, CLI smoke); regression `test_learn_common/learn_gbt/learn` **24 passed**; `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS**; full suite **129 passed**; fixture-pipeline smoke (`ingest…→feature_set`→stage with `--with-harness`) ran end-to-end — both scopes, harness scored 1078/window, depth ablation deltas negative (depth helps) esp. chainlink scope (base→full ΔBrier −0.079 fwd10), 135 artifacts. **Deferred:** `config::EngineParams → learn_short_gbt params` map; consuming the short-horizon microstructure signal back in the engine if a challenger earns it; a `compare`-style cross-model roll-up including the short-horizon models. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-05 — **model-lab `money_judge` stage — the Phase-1 "does the 10s signal make money?" verdict** (`model_lab/money_judge.py` + `tests/test_money_judge.py`, 13) + `lib/math.{taker_fee,majority_baseline}` + `learn_short_gbt` majority-baseline row + `fixtures.make_short_oos_fixture` + `verify`/`run_all`/README wiring. Replays the journal and, at each timestamp where the trained 10s model fires beyond a swept confidence threshold (`|p_up−0.5| ≥ θ`, side Up/Down), simulates two actions against the **recorded Polymarket book**, pays the **exact** documented taker fee, and marks **to the window's actual resolution** ($1 on the winner): **(a) taker** (buy the leaned side at the displayed ask up to displayed size after a latency); **(b) pair-leg** (leaned fill, then complete the opposite leg from the book 5–15 s later — lock the pair when combined cost < $1, else strand the leg to resolution and book the honest loss). Reports per-day + per-window PnL after fees / trade counts / win rate, sweeps θ, and compares vs **never-trading ($0)** and the engine's own hand-coded **momentum** trigger (+ **late-window** certainty taker) replayed on the *same* journal; renders a plain-language verdict. **Python-only; NO Rust; no new deps** (numpy/pandas/matplotlib already pinned). **Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion):** (1) **trade both sides** — the lab only recorded the Up-token book, so Down fills use the documented CLOB mirror (the Up **bid** IS the mirrored Down **ask**: price `1 − bid_px`, size `bid_sz` — a real recorded order/size, §7), not Up-only; (2) headline model = **all-scope, `full` variant** OOS predictions (`--scope`/`--variant` selectable), evaluated only on `label_source=="chainlink"` (real-book/real-resolution weeks). **Honesty-first architecture:** fires = the **walk-forward out-of-sample** predictions `learn_short_gbt` already wrote (`oos_<scope>_fwd10_<variant>.parquet` — never in-sample, so the money verdict can't cheat), so **money_judge is LightGBM-free** (reads the OOS parquet + journal only; guarded by `config.assert_parquet_ready`). **Conservative-by-construction:** pay the ask; no fresh book within `BOOK_STALENESS_MS`=2000 at `fire_ts+latency` (default 200 ms) ⇒ no fill; per-fire + per-window ($10) budget caps; below the $1 min notional ⇒ skip. **Exact fee** (`lib/math.taker_fee`, replica of `crates/core-types/src/money.rs`): `raw = shares·rate·p·(1−p)`; `raw==0`→0 (no floor); else 5-dp half-away (`floor(raw·1e5+0.5)/1e5`) → `max(·, 1e-5)`; rate 0.07 (per-market a follow-up). **Baselines ported faithfully from Rust:** `_confirmed_direction` (`taker/signal.rs`: ≥2 usable Binance-mids in `[now−2000,now]`, `|ln(S_new/S_old)| > 3.0·σ_1s·√T`) + `_plan_take_single_level` (`taker/edge.rs`: reject if `fair≤price`; accept iff `(fair−price) > rate·p·(1−p)·(1−0) + 0.005`; $1 notional) over the full gate ladder (Ready/window-match/usable-fair-vol/τ>0/confirmed-move/cooldown-5000ms/budget/book); the late-window taker (`late_window/driver.rs`: Ready + `anchor=="Chainlink"` + τ≤30 + p_up beyond 0.97/0.03 → asks ≤ 0.99, no fee gate). Binance mids for the momentum ring are read from journal `price_tick` `BinanceDirect/Mid` **bucketed to 100 ms** (last per bucket) — a documented approximation of the engine's sub-second ring; the trigger is evaluated at journaled `model` snapshots (~1/s), so **fewer/possibly-divergent momentum fires than live**. **Own journal pass** `_read_signal_journal` (one stream): model snapshots **with `sigma_1s`+`anchor`** (which `eh.read_journal` drops), the `win_meta`/outcome dicts (fed to `eh.windows_frame` verbatim), + the bucketed mids; windows/resolution + Up-token book via `eh.windows_frame` + `short_horizon._read_pm_books` (best bid/ask px+size). **Majority-class baseline (task #2):** `lib/math.majority_baseline(y) = {p=mean, diracc=max(p,1−p), brier=p·(1−p)}` (the base-rate no-skill reference, matching the shuffled-control `chance_brier`/`base_rate_acc` convention); added as `block["majority_pooled"]` to every `learn_short_gbt` block + console print (model skill shown as **lift**), and as a lift table in the money-judge report scored on the fired set **vs the window outcome** (does the leaned side beat always-betting-the-common-outcome). **Best-threshold rule:** argmax taker net PnL among θ with ≥1 trade, tie-break to the **higher** θ (fewer/more-confident). **Outputs** `out/money_judge/{sweep.csv, per_day.csv, per_window.csv, metrics.json, report.html}` (report reuses `eh._png`/`_img_tag`/`_table_html`/`_json_default`; a PnL-vs-θ chart with momentum/late/never reference lines). **Verify runs it LightGBM-free** via `make_short_oos_fixture` (writes a synthetic `oos_all_fwd10_full.parquet` oracle-nudged toward each window's `outcome_up` ⇒ the fired side is the winner ⇒ provably profitable after fees); asserts fires>0, best-threshold taker net>0 + win-rate>0.9, momentum ran, late-window==0 (fixture anchor is `BinanceCorrected`), majority-lift finite, files written. **Wired** into `run_all --with-gbt` (after `learn_short_gbt`, consumes its OOS parquet) + `verify` (banner now `…→evaluate→money_judge→report`). **Fidelity caveats documented (in `CAVEATS` + verdict):** single displayed level (conservative on depth); full-displayed-size fill at the recorded price (the engine's own single-actor paper model); 100 ms mid bucketing + ~1/s momentum eval; fixed latency + staleness skip bias to fewer trades; fee 0.07; late-window uncovered by the fixture; float parsing; mark-to-resolution holds to settlement (pair-leg models locking). **Verified (venv `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`):** `pytest tests/test_money_judge.py` **13 passed** (fee reference points/half-away/floor, mirror + fee symmetry, `_confirmed_direction`, momentum edge-gate table, `majority_baseline`, no-book-skip, budget-exhaustion, pair-leg stranded-vs-locked booking, profitable-oracle→net>0, random→threshold-0 net<0, byte-identical determinism, `ParquetNotReady` guard); `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (money_judge peak RSS 341 MB); full suite **143 passed** (129 prior + new, incl. the `learn_short_gbt` `majority_pooled` test, LightGBM present on this box). **Item still pending (data, not code):** rebuild `feature_set.parquet` to close the ~1.8% flow gap = re-run `python -m model_lab.hist` (fetch newly-published aggTrades days, self-healing) then `python -m model_lab.feature_set` — a data refresh, no code change; and a real money-judge run needs `learn_short_gbt`'s OOS parquet + a `--since/--until`-bounded pass over the multi-GB journal. **Deferred:** per-market `fees.rate`; full L2 PM-book (`book` records) for deeper sizing; a `--predictions` override sharing the `evaluate` grid contract; the real-journal Phase-1 verdict once the day-7 paper soak accrues more chainlink windows. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-08 — **Opt-in fast-feed staleness grace for home testing (`risk.feed_staleness_grace_ms`, default 0).** Operator ran `bot run` on a home connection and saw `FeedStale` flap (trip → authoritative cancel-all → clear ~300 ms later, every ~8 s) so the engine never rested quotes long enough to trade — the exact jitter symptom recorded in the 2026-06-22 soak (`FeedStale flapped 916× … jittery direct-Binance link vs the 500 ms fast-feed bound`). Root cause = the §11 `FeedStale` timer trips on any `now − binance_mid_last > feed_staleness_ms` (500 ms), and a home link intermittently gaps > 500 ms on the direct-Binance `Mid` stream; each trip cancels all quotes. `risk.feed_staleness_ms` is **validation-capped at ≤ 500** (a hardwired §11 invariant, `crates/config/src/sections/risk.rs`), so it cannot be loosened in config. **Fix (additive, opt-in, off by default):** new field `feed_staleness_grace_ms` on `RiskConfig`/`RiskParams`/`RiskCore` — the fast-feed **timer** now trips at `feed_staleness_ms + feed_staleness_grace_ms`. Since `binance_mid_last` resets on every fresh tick, `now − last` measures one continuous gap, so grace acts as a dwell: a transient gap that recovers within the sum never trips, while a genuine feed death (silence past the sum) still trips + cancels. **Scope is deliberately narrow:** grace extends ONLY the `binance_mid_last` timer path in `RiskCore::feed_stale_now`; the latched `stale_feeds` (`FeedHealth::Stale`) and `stale_books` (`BookHealth::Stale`) sets, `WsDisconnect`, and every other breaker are untouched — a real disconnect or a sustained sub-Hz-feed watchdog stale still fires immediately. Default **0** = strict §11 (VPS/live unchanged); validation `[0, 5000]` ms. Operator sets e.g. `1500` in `bot.local.toml` for home paper testing (tolerates gaps up to 2 s). **Files:** `crates/engine/src/risk/params.rs` (field + `Default 0` + params test), `crates/engine/src/risk/core.rs` (struct field, constructor, `feed_stale_now` sum, doc §11 map, new test `grace_tolerates_a_transient_gap_within_the_sum_but_a_real_death_still_trips`), `crates/config/src/sections/risk.rs` (field + default + `[0,5000]` validation + accept/reject tests), `config/default.toml` (`feed_staleness_grace_ms = 0` + comment), `crates/bot/src/run.rs` (`risk_params` map line + anti-drift spot-check). **Anti-drift preserved:** `risk_params(AppConfig::default()) == RiskParams::default()` and `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` both still hold (grace 0 on all three sides). **Verified (this box):** `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` + `cargo clippy -p engine -p config -p bot --all-targets -D warnings` clean; `cargo test` ran green for the touched suites — engine `risk::` 28/28 (incl. the new grace test + unchanged strict-500 ms test), config `risk::` 5/5 + `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults`, bot `risk_params_maps_config_and_equals_default_for_defaults`. **This loosens a safety guard — home-testing/paper concession only; keep 0 on a colocated VPS.** The proper long-term fix remains a colocated VPS (removes the jitter entirely) and/or the still-deferred per-feed `FeedStale` granularity. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-08 — **Opt-out startup journal replay (`run.replay_journal_on_start`, default true).** Operator's `bot run` appeared to hang for a very long time after "bot run starting" before binding/trading. Root cause: `run.rs` calls `boot::rebuild_and_log(config)` at startup to rebuild prior inventory/order state, which reads+folds **every** committed journal segment before the dashboard binds — and the result is bound to `let _restored` and currently **unused** (the live-seed follow-up is still deferred), so it is pure boot latency. The operator's journal had grown to **~16 GB / 1119 segments** (the 2026-07-03 24/7 data-collection run with `journal.retention_*` set to 30 d / 80 GiB so it never prunes); at the prior-measured ~10 min/1.1 GB replay rate that is **~2 h** of startup grind before anything happens. **Fix (additive, default preserves behavior):** new `RunConfig.replay_journal_on_start: bool` (default `true`); `run.rs` gates the `rebuild_and_log` call on it, logging `skipping journal replay at startup … recording is unaffected` when false. Recording is **completely independent** — the `Recorder` appends new segments to `journal.dir` regardless; only the *read-back-at-boot* is skipped. Operator set `false` in `bot.local.toml [run]` for instant home-test starts. **Files:** `crates/config/src/sections/run.rs` (field + `Default true` + `replay_defaults_on_to_preserve_prior_behavior` test), `config/default.toml` (`replay_journal_on_start = true` + comment), `crates/bot/src/run.rs` (`if config.run.replay_journal_on_start { … } else { info! }`), `config/bot.local.toml` (operator's local override `= false`). **Anti-drift preserved:** `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` holds (true on both sides). **Verified (this box):** `cargo check -p config -p bot` + `cargo clippy -p config -p bot --all-targets -D warnings` clean; config `run::` tests 3/3 + default.toml pin pass; **live 5-min paper run with the operator's real config confirmed:** startup was **instant** (`skipping journal replay …`, dashboard bound ~15 ms after "bot run starting" vs the multi-hour replay), the recorder wrote a fresh segment `journal-20260707-183153-00000.jsonl.gz` to `data/journal` (recording unaffected), discovery opened windows for all six series, and — validating the 2026-07-08 grace fix — **`FeedStale` tripped 0 times** (was flapping every ~8 s before). **The run did NOT trade** because the self-check reported `still NOT trading … missing=["clock"]`: the box's clock was **−579 ms** off NTP, tripping the §11 `ClockSkew` breaker (trip bound 250 ms), exactly the 2026-06-22 "must NTP-sync before `bot run`" finding. **Operator action required for home testing: sync the Windows clock** (`w32tm /resync`, or Settings → Time → Sync now) so the offset is under 250 ms; the bot stays up and auto-arms once the clock is sane. **Deferred (unchanged):** wiring `rebuild_and_log`'s `RebuiltState` into the live engine so the replay becomes useful (at which point large-journal deployments still want this toggle, or a bounded/segment-count-limited rebuild). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-09 — **Telonex Binance order-book DEPTH validated + full history pulled** (for the short-horizon depth model; our own `depth20@100ms` capture only began 2026-07-03). Three new self-contained model-lab modules (Polymarket pull code/data untouched): `telonex_binance_trial.py` (Stage 1 discover + Stage 2 trial), `telonex_binance_validate.py` (Stage 3 five-check validation + the permanent parity guard + capture-quality diagnostic), `telonex_binance_pull.py` (Stage 4 projection + Stage 5 bulk pull) + `tests/test_telonex_binance.py` (7). All reuse `io/telonex.py` (already Binance-capable: `BINANCE_CHANNELS`, `binance_raw_path`, 302→S3 two-step, zst/roundtrip/disk). **Key upgraded to Pro** (was Single-Exchange/Polymarket-only) → Binance unlocked; access gate = a body-inspecting no-follow-GET probe (`probe_access`) since `tx.probe_remaining` collapses `exchange_restricted` into `TelonexDownloadLimit`. **Channel = `book_snapshot_25`** — the only Binance channel with ≥20 levels (25, event-driven from `@depth@100ms`; `book_snapshot_5`/`quotes` too shallow, no `_full` for Binance, no fixed-cadence `snapshot_N`); flattened cols `bid/ask_{price,size}_0..24` (decimal strings), `timestamp_us`/`local_timestamp_us` (µs). **Pulled BTCUSDT+ETHUSDT 2026-02-06→2026-07-07 = 304 files / 21.2 GiB zst**, resume-safe, jobs=3, zstd-12, per-file bit-identical roundtrip (0 failures), raw deleted after verify → `data/telonex/raw/binance/book_snapshot_25/{symbol}-{day}.parquet.zst` (+ `out/telonex/binance_{coverage.json,pull_report.html,catalog.md,validation.{json,html}}`). Disk gate = stricter of principled (free−40GiB−Polymarket-*remaining* ~19.5GiB = 310.9GiB) and conservative (free−40−188GiB gross = 142.5GiB); 21.2 GiB fits easily. **Validation = REVIEW→proceed (operator):** clock **0.0ms offset / 0 jitter / 0 drift** vs our journaled Binance trades (Telonex `timestamp_us` IS Binance exchange time); 0 dup/nonmono/malformed; cadence median 0.1s; 25 levels; range 02-06→07-07. **Feature parity (the decisive, novel check):** reshape a `book_snapshot_25` row into the exact frame `io/depth.read_depth_levels` yields, then run the **same** `short_horizon._frame_depth_features`+`build_depth_feature_grid` (byte-identical math, unit-tested) — so any divergence is data, not code. depth_imb_20 magnitude ratio ~1.00, bias ~0, but **the raw-1s point correlation is a sub-second SAMPLING CEILING** (our 100ms partial-book snapshots vs Telonex event-driven snapshots sample the book at different instants): it **converges** BTC 0.973→0.994@5s→0.999@60s, ETH 0.945→0.984@5s→0.995@60s. Convergence to ~1.0 ⇒ same book (a wrong/scaled/futures feed would not converge). **ETH is noisier than BTC = genuine market microstructure, NOT our capture** — `--capture-quality` shows recorder inter-arrival IDENTICAL for both assets (median 100ms, p99 115ms, gaps>5s 3 vs 3, 432k frames each), so ETH's faster book churn relative to its finer relative tick is the cause. **Permanent parity guard** (`--guard`, operator-mandated on every Telonex-depth feature rebuild): auto-discovers recorder∩Telonex overlap days (07-04→, growing), BTC+ETH, **divergence-focused** — FAILS on systematic |bias|>0.01, magnitude ratio ∉[0.98,1.02] on {depth_imb_20,microprice_gap,slopes}, unstable per-day clock shift, corr not ≥0.99 by the 30s bucket, or corr@5s<0.97 floor; logs the full convergence curve each run (`binance_parity_guard.json`). Both BTC+ETH currently PASS. Reads finalized `.zst` (decompress-to-scratch) since the pull deletes raw. **Env note:** background jobs on this box get killed after a couple minutes and long foreground commands auto-background, so the validation/guard are windowed to the first `PARITY_HOURS=3`h/day (thousands of 1s bars — statistically ample) and the clock scan to `CLOCK_HOURS=3`h; peak RSS ~3.6GB (advisory ceiling 3072MB, completes fine). **Operator standing requirements:** (1) the divergence-focused guard above (done); (2) **ENFORCED source-tagging** — any future training dataset mixing Telonex + recorder depth MUST tag each row's `depth_source` (telonex|recorder), and the **first ETH training run must report metrics split telonex-rows vs recorder-rows and fail loudly on a material gap** (contract recorded; no code yet — Telonex depth is not wired into `dataset`/`short_horizon`/`feature_set`, which today read only our recorder). **Deferred:** integrating Telonex depth into feature-building with the `depth_source` tag + the split-metrics training gate; folding the guard into whatever future stage rebuilds depth features; a bounded-memory guard for very large overlap spans. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-09 — **model-lab historical ingest path + momentum backtest over full Telonex history** (three new stages + a shared reconstruction library + a PM reader, all Python-only; **no Rust / no new deps**). Turns the pre-journal data on disk into training rows + an honest "does momentum make money?" verdict. New files: `io/telonex_pm.py`, `historical_common.py`, `historical_labels.py` (Part 2), `historical_dataset.py` (Part 1), `backtest_momentum.py` (Part 3) + `tests/test_historical_{dataset,labels,backtest_momentum}.py` (17); extended `fixtures.py` (+`make_historical_fixture`, `make_overlap_parity_fixture`, `write_historical_coverage`, `make_fixture` gained an additive `ts0_ms`), `tests/test_dataset.py::_FEATURE_FUNCS` (+2 historical kernels), `verify.py` (+`check_historical_offline`), README. **Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion):** universe = **4 series BTC/ETH×5m/15m** (Telonex Polymarket on disk; no 1h); row format = **short_horizon-compatible** (only it holds Telonex `book_snapshot_25` depth) + a new `depth_source` column; resolution = **quote-convergence primary, Binance cross-check**, strict, ambiguous→excluded, disagreements must cluster at knife-edge (non-knife disagreement = method bug → stop). **Data spans (disk):** aggTrades 2025-01-09→2026-07-03 (mid/vol/strike backbone), Telonex `book_snapshot_25` 2026-02-06→07-07 (depth), Telonex Polymarket quotes btc/eth-5m&15m (5m from Feb, 15m from Oct 2025; the `asset_id` column IS the CLOB token id), journal 2026-06-22→ (owns `>= 2026-07-04`). **`historical_common` primitives (reuse-heavy, feed the *scanned* kernels):** `telonex_windows_present` (globs on-disk Up/Down quote files, excludes coverage.json `missing_windows` entries `"{slug}/{outcome}/{channel}"` when `quotes` missing either outcome), `binance_bars_for_day` (aggTrades 1s bars, 3-day buffer, reuses `dataset._day_bars`), `binance_anchored_strike`/`binance_outcome` (§6 last-trade≤open, end≥strike⇒Up), **`resolve_by_quote_convergence`** (winner *final* mid ≥`win` 0.98 AND loser ≤`lose` 0.02, ≥`min_tail` 3 quotes in the last 30s; Telonex quotes stop ~at close so the final mid caps ~0.97–0.99 → the strict defaults resolve ~58% at ~96% Binance agreement, exclude the rest), **`telonex_depth_feature_grid`** (chunked `pq.iter_batches`→last-book-per-second→the *same* `short_horizon._frame_depth_features`/`build_depth_feature_grid` the live model + parity guard use; a full day ~851k×100-string-col book would be multi-GB read whole → chunked keeps peak ~1.6GB/asset-day), `telonex_top_mid` (fine 100ms top-mid for the momentum ring), `reconstruct_window_snapshots` (1s p_up/σ from the grid + strike, price-only z), `journal_resolved_outcomes` (segment-**filename-date-filtered** so the overlap check never decompresses the whole 16GB journal). **Part 2 (`historical_labels`):** primary=quote convergence, cross-check=Binance (aggTrades) on Telonex-only days / **journal `Resolved` exact-match on overlap days (any mismatch = hard fail)**; knife-edge guard = FAIL if ≥10 disagreements and <60% are knife-edge (`strike_dist_vol < 0.25`); coverage per series per month; `out/historical_labels/{labels.parquet,metrics.json,report.html}`; **real-data validated** (BTC-5m 2026-05-01..03: 46% coverage, 4.9% disagreement, **77% knife-edge**, disagree strike-dist-vol p50 0.065 vs agree 0.869 — disagreements cluster tightly at knife-edge exactly as required). **Part 1 (`historical_dataset`):** **day-by-day resumable** — one partition parquet per `(series,day)` under `out/historical_dataset/parts/` + `manifest.json` (re-run skips `ok` days, atomic tmp→replace), `--combine` streams to `out/historical_dataset.parquet`. **Day-ownership dispatch:** Telonex-owned days reconstructed here (`label_source=telonex`, `depth_source=telonex`, Binance strike, Telonex depth+PM microstructure via `sh._build_samples`, quote-convergence outcome); journal-owned days built by **one bounded `sh.short_horizon(include_history=False)` run** (reuses the existing path verbatim) then split into per-day partitions (`label_source=chainlink`, `depth_source=recorder`); journal days enumerated from segment filenames (not just Telonex coverage, so post-07-07 journal-only days build too). **Schema = `sh.COLUMN_SPEC` + `depth_source` meta** (kept OFF the live builder to avoid perturbing its tested schema — the plan's open-note alternative). **Strike/basis:** historical `K`=Binance-anchored, Chainlink-definition cols (`chainlink`/`basis_bps`/`basis_ewma`/corrected-`z`) journal-era-only (NaN on Telonex rows, `z`=price-only) — documented in SCHEMA.md + metadata. **`--overlap-parity` (C5 / standing contract):** rebuilds the Telonex version on overlap days and compares source-agnostic features (mid, σ_1s, depth_imb_20, microprice_gap, slopes) vs the journal-owner rows using `telonex_binance_validate` guard tolerances (GUARD_MAG 0.98–1.02, |bias|≤0.01, corr≥0.99); **matching-book fixture → PASS with corr=1.0/ratio=1.0/bias=0 on all 6**. `sh._assert_no_lookahead` runs on every Telonex batch (sh._build_samples skips it). **Real-data validated** (BTC-5m 2026-05-01, 1 day: 9,960 samples/166 resolved windows, depth_imb_20 & pm_mid populated, chainlink all-NaN, z 100% finite, peak RSS 1.9GB). **Part 3 (`backtest_momentum`):** replays the engine trigger via **money_judge's exact ports** (`_run_momentum`/`_confirmed_direction`/`_plan_take_single_level`/`_book_asof`/`_leaned_quote`/`_mark_to_resolution`/`lm.taker_fee`, identity-asserted in tests) over reconstructed 1s model snapshots + a 100ms Telonex-top-mid ring + Telonex-quote PM books; outcome=quote convergence. **Controls:** never-trade $0; **matched-frequency shuffled control** (per-(series,day) local `fire_prob = confirmed/eligible`, fire at random eligible snapshots, side=model lean, identical cooldown/budget/edge-gate/fill, N seeds; `zlib.crc32` salt for cross-process determinism — NOT `hash()`); majority baseline; **real must beat shuffled (net > mean AND permutation p ≤ 0.1) else VOID.** Reports PnL-after-fees/win-rate/trades/**drawdown per series per month** + **by time-of-day** + equity/control charts; `out/backtests/momentum/{report.html,metrics.json,trades.csv,per_series_month.csv,per_hour.csv}`. **Real-data validated** (BTC-5m 2026-05-01..03, 8 seeds: **398 trades, net +$256.80 after $86.28 fees, 67% win, max DD $83.42, BEATS the random control decisively p=0.00** — the §8 lead-lag edge: the fast Telonex/Binance mid leads the Polymarket book, and the momentum take buys the stale-cheap side before the book reprices; caveats = money_judge's, single-level fill, 100ms ring, reconstruction≠journaled-model). **No-lookahead** enforced three ways (AST scan +2 kernels, runtime `_assert_no_lookahead`, **differential** test: corrupt every feature grid >T → the T-sample's `FEATURE_COLS` bit-identical). **Verify** offline (fixtures, no network): labels(8 resolved/2 ambiguous/clean cross-check) → dataset(telonex depth_source, chainlink NaN, combine) → backtest(controls+outputs) → overlap parity PASS; `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS**, historical tests **32 passed** (17 new + 15 telonex), core-suite regression **43 passed** (dataset/short_horizon/stream/smoke/basis). **Env:** the big journal read (overlap check, 07-04+) touches ~670–895 real segments (minutes) — bounded to overlap-day ranges; validated via a synthetic overlap fixture instead. **Deferred:** consuming `historical_dataset.parquet` in a learner (short-horizon challenger trained on the full history with the `depth_source` split-metrics gate — the standing contract's training-side half); the real-journal overlap exact-match validation as a bounded operator run; extending aggTrades to 18mo (`hist --days 540`) to lengthen the 15m pre-Telonex-book tail. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-09 — **Label coverage fixed 46%→99.5% via OFFICIAL Polymarket resolutions + a stratified validation; production runs launched.** The historical pipeline's quote-convergence label only covered ~46–58% (diagnosis: **49.8% of unlabeled windows are `not_converged_leaning`** — the market decisively leaned but its *final recorded* Telonex quote capped ~0.97 because quotes stop ~at close before settlement to $1; only ~1.3% sparse-recording; 0 conflicting). **Finding (operator-approved via AskUserQuestion):** the **Telonex markets catalog** (`data/telonex/raw/polymarket/markets.parquet`, 35 cols) already carries the on-chain resolution as **`result_id`** (`"0"`→outcome_0=Up won, `"1"`→outcome_1=Down) with `status=resolved`+`settled_at_us` — **156,324 of 157,101 our-series windows (99.5%) resolved, offline**; the 777 remainder are `active` (unsettled). This IS the official resolution: validated **0-mismatch** against the Polymarket **CLOB API** (`clob.polymarket.com/markets/{cid}` → `tokens[].winner`; the API 404s on ~12% of old markets, so a full 156k-call fetch would have *lower* coverage). **New `io/polymarket.py`** (read-only CLOB client, `PolymarketNotFound`/`PolymarketHTTPError`, `clob_market`/`resolution_winner`, `fetch` seam) + **`historical_resolutions.py`** (Part A.2): reads the 1 GB catalog in row-group batches → compact caches `data/resolutions/{catalog_resolutions.parquet, catalog_windows.parquet, clob_cache.jsonl}` (`clob_cache` resumable/rate-limited). **Operator-mandated stratified API validation (hard-fail on ANY mismatch):** `stratified_sample` covers **every (series, month) + knife-edge windows specifically** (per-stratum: knife-edge kept in full + ≤80 uniform, `binance_outcome` vol-distance <0.25 = knife-edge, aggTrades day-loaded once/stratum) → real run **sample=3,773 (893 knife-edge, all 4 series, 10 months) → 3,260 API-cross-checked, 0 MISMATCH, 513 api-absent (old-market 404s)**; journal-overlap exact-match (bounded by `max_segments=30` ≈ 1 h/segment on the 16 GB journal — the full 4-day overlap read is impractical, ~30 min+) **97 checked, 0 mismatch**. **Official is now PRIMARY everywhere:** `historical_labels` rewritten — window universe = **catalog** (`catalog_windows`, fast; NOT a 261k-file glob which hung the first run), label = official dict-lookup (~100% instantly, no per-window quote reads), unlabeled = counted-not-guessed; the two proxies are **graded** (Part A.3) over a stratified sample vs official truth: **quote-convergence 0.00% error** (perfectly reliable *where* it converges, ~58% coverage), **Binance-anchored 7.81% error / 20.29% at knife-edge** (the ~13 bps basis flips near-strike outcomes) — so quote-convergence is the proxy to trust where the API lacks a market, Binance must be down-weighted near strike. `historical_dataset` + `backtest_momentum` swapped from quote-convergence to the official `res_map` (injectable for tests) → dataset per-day coverage **58%→100%** (288/288 windows on 2026-05-01, 17,280 samples vs 9,960). Reused `journal_resolved_outcomes` gained `max_segments`; `hc.journal_resolved_outcomes` widening dropped the leading buffer (capped read spends budget on overlap days). **backtest made overnight-resumable** — per-`(series,day)` JSON checkpoints (trades + per-seed control net/trades, atomic, seed-count-guarded) under `out/backtests/momentum/checkpoints/`; a killed run resumes (subsumes the operator's "month-by-month"). **Fixtures/tests/verify:** `make_historical_fixture`/`make_overlap_parity_fixture` now write a markets catalog + return a `res_map` (unique per-(asset,open) condition ids); new `tests/test_historical_resolutions.py` (6: catalog decode, offline-`fetch`-seam API validation match + hard-fail-on-mismatch + cache-resumable, journal overlap clean/mismatch); `test_historical_labels` reworked (official coverage 12/12, no-official counted-not-guessed, proxy grading); `verify.check_historical_offline` adds the offline API validation. `verify` **PASS**; historical tests **green** (resolutions 6, labels 4, dataset 6, backtest 4). **Part B production (launched detached, overnight-safe, resumable — this box kills multi-min jobs):** `historical_labels` full-history **DONE** (156,324/157,101 = 99.5%, `out/historical_labels/`); `historical_dataset` full-history + `backtest_momentum` launched detached (per-day partitions/checkpoints, resumable via `--combine` / re-run). **No Rust; no new pip deps** (urllib/pyarrow/numpy/pandas). **Deferred:** completing the full-history dataset/backtest overnight (resumable) + `--combine`; a shared depth-grid cache to halve the dataset's redundant per-asset-day Telonex depth reads (btc-5m + btc-15m re-read the same book); wiring the graded proxy trust into any gap-filling. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-07 venue regime change: GTD min expiry 1→3min, phantom-liquidity reduction — all Telonex history is pre-change; treat book-microstructure comparisons across this date accordingly.

- 2026-07-09 — **Venue 250ms-taker-delay + fee-regime audit (read-only, no code changes)** → `out/audits/venue_regime_timeline.md`. **(1) Live market info (all 4 series):** the taker-delay flag is **`itode` on `GET clob.polymarket.com/clob-markets/{cid}`** (abbreviated keys `ao/aot/c/fd/itode/mbf/mos/mts/r/t/tbf`) — **`itode:true` on BTC/ETH-5m/15m right now**; `fd={r:0.07,e:1,to:true}` (the exponent-1 `shares·0.07·p·(1−p)` fee our bot already uses), `mts=0.01`, `mos=5`, `mbf/tbf=1000` (legacy bps, overridden by `fd`). NOTE the legacy `seconds_delay` on `/markets/{cid}` is an **integer** = `0` and **cannot represent 250ms — a red herring**; use `itode`. No field literally named `feeRateBps`. **(2) Stack delay-awareness:** venue-live = **NO** (maps venue status `"delayed"`→`OrderState::PendingNew` and treats it as working — `venue.rs:298`, `store.rs:617` — but **no no-cancel-window / await / balance-reservation logic**); paper venue = **YES** (`place()`→`PendingNew{activate_at=now+placement_latency}`, marketable walks the **activation-time** book; latency uniform **150–250ms** default one-sided-slower — `engine.rs`, `params.rs:27`); money_judge = **YES** but **fixed 200ms** (`_book_asof(sample_ts+latency_ms)`, `DEFAULT_LATENCY_MS=200`); backtest_momentum = **YES** but **fixed 200ms** (`mj._book_asof(now+latency_ms)`). **None models the exact 250ms** — the Python sims use 200ms (~50ms short → fills a **staler** book → momentum edge **overstated**), and no sim models the Jun-5 pending no-cancel lock. **(3) Verified regime timeline (2026):** Jan 7 dynamic taker fees (15m crypto); **Feb 18** original **500ms delay removed** (takers trade current prices — momentum-favorable); **Mar 30** fee categories expanded to 10 categories (docs changelog); **250ms taker delay introduced** post-Feb-18 on crypto/finance up-down markets — **exact date NOT pinned** in docs/changelog/news (mechanism-only, `itode`); **Jun 2 announce / Jun 5 effect** — 250ms delay behavior **LOCKED** (taker order in the delay window is pending & **non-cancellable**, balance reserved; CLOB restart 12:10–12:54 UTC + 2min post-only); **Jul 7 14:00 UTC** GTD min-expiry 1→3min. Jun 5 taken as the verified boundary of the CURRENT (locked) 250ms regime. **(4) Fraction of backtest history in the current regime (post-Jun-5, over Telonex coverage):** BTC-5m **22.6%** (9,498/42,030), ETH-5m **23.7%** (9,500/40,073), BTC-15m **12.2%** (3,167/25,867), ETH-15m **12.2%** (3,167/25,863) — and **40–76%** of history sits in the Feb18→Jun5 **no-delay** window (most momentum-favorable), so the full-history momentum headline is **inflated** and the verdict should rest **only on the post-Jun-5 slice** (re-run `backtest_momentum --since 2026-06-05 --latency-ms 250`). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-10 — **Momentum verdict under the current 250ms taker-delay regime — implemented the 2026-07-09 audit's follow-ups.** 8 workstreams; touches `config`/`venue-paper`/`bot` (Rust) + `money_judge`/`backtest_momentum` + 3 new model-lab modules; **no new deps.** **(WS1) Rust paper venue — additive, marketable-only venue taker delay.** New `venue_taker_delay_ms` (default **250**) on `config::PaperConfig` + `venue_paper::PaperParams` + `config/default.toml` (scalar key BEFORE the `[paper.*]` sub-tables) + `bot::paper::paper_params`; `venue-paper/src/engine.rs::place()` adds the hold to `activate_at` **only** for `order.tif.is_marketable()` (resting post-only/GTC makers never match on entry ⇒ no hold): `activate_at = now + sample(placement) + (marketable ? venue_taker_delay_ms : 0)`. Validation `>= 0` both sides (new `PaperParamsError::VenueTakerDelayNegative`); `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` holds. New venue-paper unit test (marketable activates at `now+network+250`, maker at `now+network`). **`itode`-gating + per-series override deferred** — `MarketInfo`/discovery carry no taker-delay field and `itode` lives on a separate `/clob-markets/{cid}` endpoint discovery doesn't hit; all four series `itode:true` today so the global config value is faithful ("where available = not plumbed ⇒ config default"). **Test-timing fix:** `PaperParams::default()` now carries 250ms which shifts marketable-fill timing, so the 6 bot integration tests + the venue-paper `engine()` helper that build params via `..PaperParams::default()` set `venue_taker_delay_ms = ZERO` (they verify engine logic, not the venue delay — separately covered). **(WS2/WS3) Python sims.** `--latency-ms` reinterpreted as NETWORK; new `--venue-delay-ms` (default `DEFAULT_VENUE_DELAY_MS=250`) added on top for taker fills (all fill paths are FAK takers), `effective = network + venue` flows into the existing `_book_asof` calls, decomposition recorded in `params.{network_ms,venue_delay_ms,effective_latency_ms}` + caveats. `backtest_momentum` gained `--out-name` (→ `out/backtests/<name>`, isolates checkpoints) so verdict runs never collide with the full run. **(bug) parallel Telonex readers raced on a shared scratch temp** — `telonex_pm._read_tel_columns` + `historical_common.telonex_top_mid` decompress `.zst` to a **deterministic** temp; two concurrent stages reading the same file clobbered each other (0-byte read → `ArrowInvalid` crash, hit immediately when the two verdict runs launched in parallel). Fixed process-unique via `os.getpid()`; reader tests still green. **(WS5) `detect_delay.py` — pin the 250ms introduction date from the tape.** The delay floors how fast an aggressive PM print can follow a Binance move; per (ISO-week, series) over Feb→Jun, lag from a Binance move (|1s-ret|>3σ, 2s refractory) to the first same-direction aggressive PM print (`read_pm_trades`, aggressor `side` **auto-calibrated** from data), scan the weekly **p10 floor** for a clean low→high step to ~250ms. 9 unit tests. **Real-data result: NO clean step → `detected_date=null` → Jun 5 stays the boundary, no secondary run.** Side calibration excellent (buy→up 0.93 / sell→down 0.94 over 82k samples; 10k+ lags/week) but the **p10 floor stays ~13–55ms every week incl. post-June** — the low quantile is dominated by **coincidental** same-direction prints (base rate on an active book), not reactions, so it cannot see the delay; the **median (p50) ramps gradually ~130→~280ms** crossing ~250 around late-April (gradual rollout, not a clean step — matches the audit's "date unverified / possibly gradual"). Honest negative result. **(WS6) full-history report annotated with regime boundaries** — `REGIME_BOUNDARIES` const (Jan-7 / Feb-18 500ms-off / Mar-30 / Jun-5 250ms-lock / Jul-7 GTD) + `regime_boundaries(paths)` folding in `detect_delay`'s `detected_date` when present; per-month chart overlays dated `axvline`s + a boundary table + honest `params` decomposition. Regenerated from the existing 826 checkpoints (no re-sim, 384MB, ran `--venue-delay-ms 0 --latency-ms 200` for honest flat-200 params); null detected_date ⇒ 5 verified markers, no fabricated "introduced" marker. **(WS7) `momentum_verdict.py`** (`out/backtests/momentum_verdict.{md,json}`) — per slice & series net PnL / PnL-day / win / trades-day / sample sizes, effective latency labelled, shuffled p-value/VOID carried. **THE VERDICT (real data):** full-history **+$58,344** (152,224 trades / 130,040 windows, 63% win) is **optimistic** (flat 200ms, blends the Feb18→Jun5 no-delay window). Honest current-regime (post-2026-06-05): **VPS 255ms +$5,057** (28,411 trades / 24,697 windows, 64% win), **home 450ms +$2,006** (26,917 trades, 64% win) — **both still beat the matched-frequency random control p=0.00**. Per-series the edge is **concentrated in BTC-5m** (robustly positive: VPS +$3,988 / home +$2,564) while **ETH-5m/15m go slightly NEGATIVE at home-450ms** (−$364 / −$532). ⇒ momentum survives the current regime but is **~10–30× smaller than the headline and latency-sensitive** — a VPS-colocated deployment (low network) + BTC-5m focus are materially better than home/ETH. Small sample (~5 weeks) — directional; the 24/7 paper soak grows it. **(WS8) `out/audits/pre_live_checklist.md`** (docs only): the two live-arming blockers — (1) Jun-5 **no-cancel lock** (a mid-delay taker is pending, non-cancellable, collateral reserved; cancel-all does NOT evacuate it — every "cancel-all ⇒ flat" safety assumption is violated for in-flight takers), (2) `venue-live` recognizes `"delayed"` (`venue.rs:296`→`PendingNew`) but has **no** no-cancel-window/await/reservation logic — with file refs + §11 arming implications + the `itode`-plumbing follow-up. **Verification:** `python -m model_lab.verify` PASS; **pytest 225 passed**; Rust config 80 / venue-paper 60 (incl. new venue-delay test) / bot unit 85 + integration (momentum-taker 2, late-window 2, quote-manager 5, risk-manager 6, venue-parity 13) + chaos 9 green; `cargo clippy -p bot --all-targets` 0 warnings; rustfmt clean on all touched files. **Env:** a stale 2-day-old `bot.exe` (PID 27192) from a prior session held a build lock (removed the stale exe to unblock; the orphan process is harmless — operator may want to kill it); this box kills bash jobs at ~10min so the verdict/detector runs + full pytest were launched detached via `Start-Process`. **Deferred:** `itode` per-market plumbing (discovery→`MarketInfo.taker_delay_ms`→live adapter) + the two live no-cancel-lock gaps (pre-live blockers); a reaction-isolated delay detector (filter coincidental prints, e.g. those repricing the PM mid) if a cleaner date is wanted; re-running the verdict as more post-Jun-5 data accrues. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-10 — **Forensic audit of the current-regime (VPS 255ms) momentum run — adversarial validation + capital accounting.** Ran the previously-specced forensic battery against `out/backtests/momentum_current_regime/vps255` (NOT the rosy full-history run) + completed the latency-decay curve + a new capital-accounting section. **New:** `backtest_momentum` flags `--invert-trigger` / `--shuffle-labels` / `--fee-rate` (+ `money_judge._run_momentum(invert=…)` kwarg and a per-day `_shuffle_outcomes` permutation); new `forensic_audit.py` + `tests/test_forensic_audit.py`. **No new deps.** **The 5 checks — ALL PASS.** (1) **Accounting** — recomputed fee/cost/payoff/net/won identities across all **28,411** trades → **CLEAN** (won-mismatches 0; payoff/cost/net identities exact; Σnet/Σfee reconcile to reported within $0.001). Finding: my independent `_fee_formula` is byte-identical to `lm.taker_fee`, but **33/28,411 fees differ from the recorded value by exactly one 5-dp unit (1e-5)** when recomputed from the **CSV-stored** shares/price vs the runtime full-precision value — a storage-precision rounding boundary (Σ=$0.0003), NOT a defect; the check uses the *recorded* fee for the cost/net identities (exact) and tolerates the ≤1-quantum fee-formula delta. (2) **Hand-verification** — **24/24** sampled trades (stratified/seeded) re-derived exactly from the **raw Telonex book** as-of `fire+255ms`: fill price (book ask / Down-mirror `1−bid`), budget-tracked shares (`min(avail, budget_left/price)`, budget_left reconstructed from prior in-window fills), edge gate, independent fee, official-resolution match — every number tied to ground truth. (3) **Inverted trigger** (bet against the confirmed direction) → **−$7,513** (20,701 trades, 49% win, beats-random FALSE p=1.00): edge COLLAPSES → **directional**. (4) **Shuffled labels** (per-day-permuted outcomes) → **−$8,490** (28,411 trades, **50% win**): collapses to a coinflip fee-bleed → edge is the **signal→outcome** relationship, not a fill artifact. (5) **Doubled fees (0.14)** → **+$1,873** (65% win, beats-random p=0.00): **survives 2× costs** (down from +$5,057 as fees ~doubled to $11.4k). **Latency decay** (255/450 from the verdict runs + new 500/1000/2000ms, `--venue-delay-ms 250 --since 2026-06-05`): 255→**+$5,057** ($0.178/tr), 450→+$2,006, 500→+$1,342, **1000→−$1,999**, 2000→−$5,686 — roughly linear, **zero-crossing ≈ 700ms** ⇒ the edge is **latency-critical**, profitable only at low effective latency, so a **colocated VPS is essential** (the take buys the stale-cheap side before the book reprices; too slow ⇒ book already repriced ⇒ pay fair+fees). **Capital accounting (new):** **peak concurrent open exposure = $76** — the strategy is **capital-trivial** (each fire capped at the **$10/window** taker budget; 5–15 min windows recycle capital fast; ~4–8 windows open at once). Bankroll caps **$1k/$5k/$20k all take 100% of trades → identical +$5,057** (506%/101%/25% return on the cap — the % merely dilutes with idle capital). **Bankroll is NOT the binding constraint** — the ceiling on absolute PnL is the per-window budget + displayed book depth, not capital; the "$153/day" runs on a ~$76 base, so the headline % return is on a trivially small base and is not the meaningful scaling axis. Concurrent exposure via opens-before-closes cumsum; capped sim = chronological bankroll with a close-time min-heap, skip-if-unaffordable. `forensic_audit.py` reads the baseline + 6 variation `metrics.json` + accounting/hand-verify/capital → `out/backtests/forensic/forensic_report.{md,html,json}`; standalone (not in verify/run_all, like `hist`/`detect_delay`). **OVERALL: the edge is real, correctly accounted, directional, cost-robust — and latency-critical.** **Env:** 6 re-runs launched detached in 2 parallel batches of 3 (the earlier scratch-collision fix made parallel Telonex reads safe); ~1.8GB/≤25min each. **Verify:** `test_forensic_audit.py` (fee formula, accounting clean+corruption-detect, capital peak+caps, dur/slug) + full pytest green. **Deferred:** a depth-limited "how much can you deploy per window before exhausting book depth" scaling analysis (the real absolute-PnL scaling question, since capital isn't binding); annualized/compounded return framing. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-10 — **model-lab `learn_walkforward` — the definitive walk-forward challenger (money is the bar).** New stage `model_lab/learn_walkforward.py` + `tests/test_learn_walkforward.py` (8) trains a deterministic LightGBM over the full `historical_dataset.parquet` (13.79M rows, 2025-10→2026-07 = the Telonex-proxy + journal-recorder history, rebuilt through 07-09 earlier this session) by **strict expanding-window walk-forward** and scores each block **two ways**: `eval_harness` calibration (Brier/log-loss/dir-acc vs the formula `p_up_model` and market `pm_mid`, supplied directly — no journal) as *diagnostics*, and **money** (threshold-swept taker on the recorded book, marked to the official resolution, at the current 255ms regime) as the **verdict metric**. **Python-only; opt-in `[gbt]` (LightGBM); no new deps; research-only, nothing wired to the Rust bot.** Heavy reuse: `lib/gbt` (determinism), `learn_common` (`_fold_masks`/`_fold_metrics`/`_label_info_ms`), `money_judge` fill primitives (`_book_asof`/`_leaned_quote`/`_fill_shares`/`_mark_to_resolution`/`_side_of`/`_run_taker`/`_run_momentum`/`_metrics_for_trades`), `backtest_momentum` Telonex reconstruction (`_day_grid_and_ring`/`_window_model_rows`/`_grid_bars`), `eval_harness.run`+`_finalize`. **Operator decisions (this is the definitive run, not another single split):** expanding train / 14-day test blocks slid through full history (per-block + aggregate); the bar to clear is MONEY vs the hand-coded momentum trigger replayed on the *same* current-regime windows (+cited `momentum_current_regime/vps255`); shuffled-label control must collapse or VOID; every block annotated by venue regime, headline never pooled across boundaries; two targets **raced** — `dir10` (10s mid-direction `fwd_up_10s`) vs `taker_ev` (buy-Up-now-at-`pm_mid+pm_spread/2`, exact fee, held-to-resolution, net>0), report both through money_judge, recommend one; the **depth_source split gate** (telonex vs recorder) fails loudly on a material gap; chunked/bounded/resumable. **Design decisions (left to me):** (1) forked `_fold_schedule`→`_expanding_folds` (train anchored at `lo`, not the sliding trailing window learn_short_gbt uses); (2) **base vs full** feature ablation (base=10 price/vol/formula; full=+8 Binance-depth +6 PM-book=24) — one training scope, depth_source is a *reporting* split (halves compute); no flow features (they live only in `feature_set.parquet`, not historical_dataset — deferred); (3) `taker_ev` held-to-resolution **≡ outcome_up** (buying the winner profits for any ask<~1) — an honest "predict-the-window-winner" target distinct from dir10; label vectorized (13.79M-row Python loop → numpy replica of `lm.taker_fee`); (4) money-scorer reconstructs the current-regime (≥2026-06-05) Telonex windows exactly as `backtest_momentum` does, θ-swept `_run_taker` per target + formula-as-taker (`p_up_model`) + `_run_momentum` on identical windows, resumable per-(series,day) checkpoint; (5) **tractability** — GBT budget cut (2000→600 rounds, lr 0.03→0.05) + **per-fold training-row cap** (2M, uniform seeded subsample of the expanding window; keeps the full temporal span, bounds each fit's time+memory to a constant) after an initial 19-min/fold blowup → ~40s/fit; `del mat`+gc before the money phase. **THE VERDICT (real full run: 13.79M rows, 16 expanding folds, peak RSS 8.07GB on the 16GB box, ~40min train + ~50min money):** over the **24,697** current-regime windows (255ms effective), the momentum trigger replayed here nets **$5,056.69** — **bit-identical to the cited vps255 reference** (28,411 trades, 64% win; per-series BTC-5m $3,988 / ETH-5m $427 / BTC-15m $625 / ETH-15m $16 all exact) → the money-scorer's methodology is **validated**. Challenger **`dir10` = +$2,284.01** (θ=0.03, 32,050 trades, 53% win, $0.0713/trade), **`taker_ev` = +$1,211.72** (θ=0.25, 28,057 trades, 79% win), **formula-as-taker = +$929.76**, never-trade $0. **⇒ Both challengers MAKE money and BEAT the formula-taker, but NEITHER beats the momentum trigger** ($5,057). `dir10` (microstructure-driven) is the best challenger (~45% of momentum's money). **Signal is real, result NOT void:** shuffled-label control **COLLAPSED** (pooled Brier 0.2462 == chance 0.2462); the `dir10` feature importances are **microstructure-dominated** (microprice_gap, depth_imb_1, sigma_1s) with own-label dir-acc 0.655 (vs 0.504 transferred to the window outcome — a genuine 10s-direction signal that does not predict the window). **depth_source gate PASSES over all history** — telonex Brier 0.2692 (41,457 windows) vs recorder 0.2697 (1,632 windows), gap **0.00059** ≪ 0.03 tol ⇒ **Telonex historical depth is trustworthy training data** (the standing contract holds). **Per-series nuance (a real finding):** the challenger money is **more balanced across BTC-5m+ETH-5m** than momentum's BTC-5m-concentrated edge — on **ETH-5m `dir10` (+$1,331) actually BEATS momentum (+$427)**; momentum wins overall only because BTC-5m is its stronghold ($3,988 vs dir10's $1,168), and both lose on ETH-15m — hinting a per-series ensemble (momentum on BTC-5m + dir10 on ETH-5m) could beat either alone. **Artifacts** `out/learn_walkforward/`: `metrics.json`, `report.html`, `money_comparison.csv`, `feature_importance.csv`, `per_block_{dir10,taker_ev}.csv`, `oos_{target}_{base,full}.parquet` (resumable), `harness_{target}/` (5-file eval each), `money_checkpoints/`. **8 tests** (expanding-folds anchored+block-sized+fallbacks, taker_ev branches, walk-forward beats-chance+deterministic, shuffled collapse, depth gate pass/fail, no-money smoke on a synthetic multi-day dataset, money-scorer smoke on the Telonex fixture at a current-regime day). **Env:** the box's cp1252 console chokes on `θ` from a bare `python -c` (the module's `config._enable_utf8_console()` only fires on package import) — use `sys.stdout.reconfigure('utf-8')`; the ~90-min run was launched detached and monitored (box kills bash jobs at ~10min). **Deferred:** add aggregated-flow features (join `feature_set.parquet` like learn_short_gbt) + the depth-only ablation variant; per-series models / a momentum+dir10 per-series ensemble (the ETH-5m finding); a residual (formula-init-score) variant; wiring a validated microstructure signal into the engine's fair value only if a challenger ever earns it (none did — momentum stays the best strategy). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-11 — **Auto-settlement at resolution made observable + honest across venue-paper and the dashboard.** Five-part task; touches `venue-paper` (engine/driver), `dashboard` (state/dto/ui), `analytics` (attribution test), `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md`. **No new `Event`/`VenueEvent`/journal surface** (the codebase deliberately avoids growing the exhaustive-`match Event` set) — the durable per-window settlement record stays `Event::Settlement(SettlementSummary)` (journaled, drives attribution + the two-bucket panel), which already carries outcome + per-side shares/cost, so per-position `(window, side, shares, proceeds)` is derivable. **(1) venue-paper per-position settlement audit** (`engine.rs::on_window`): the wallet already auto-settles on `Resolved` ($1/winning share, $0 losers, positions zeroed, cash returned, idempotent) at `now` (the resolution EVENT time, never `Closed`/window-close); added a per-position audit that snapshots each side's shares *before* `settle_window` zeroes them and `tracing::info!`s `window/side/winner/shares/proceeds` per non-zero side (`proceeds = $1×shares` for the winning side else `$0`, signed so a short winner shows negative). New tests: `resolved_pays_winner_and_zeroes_loser_two_sided` (engine) + `settlement_returns_cash_equity_equals_starting_plus_realized` (driver, full `PaperVenue`, `start_paused`) — the paper settlement smoke: equity converges to `starting + realized` with positions closed. `Outcome` added to engine.rs top import. **(2) Dashboard three-card identity `Cash + Open positions = Equity`** (`dto::AccountDto{cash,open_positions,equity,awaiting_resolution}` on `SummaryDto`; new UI card row + CSS). **Equity redefined = cash (`wallet.collateral_total`) + open-position COST BASIS** (not mark-to-market — the conservative §9 choice: a buy just moves cash into position cost, so equity is recognized only at resolution; the curve is now stable between settlements and steps at each resolution, fewer/cleaner points). `set_wallet` now pushes this true equity; headline `equity` uses it too. **Open positions exclude settled by construction:** `open_positions_cost(mode)` ranges only over inventory whose window is present-and-unresolved in the shared lifecycle view (`view.outcome.is_none()`), so a resolved window never counts even while it lingers in grace (its inventory is still shown in the Live-window view). **Fixed a pre-existing unbounded leak:** the per-mode `inventory` map was never pruned; now pruned to `shared.windows` membership on each window prune (disjoint field borrows). `awaiting_resolution()` = count of `WindowLifecycle::Closed` (closed-but-unresolved) shared windows → a UI indicator. **(3) Settlement display honesty:** `settlements` ring now stores `SettledWindow{summary, resolved_at}` stamped with the resolution-event `now` (the `SettlementSummary.ts` is window *close*); the resolved-windows feed uses `resolved_at` for `ts_ms` and adds per-position `up_shares`/`down_shares`/`proceeds`. **Two-bucket labels + reconciliation** (§3 of the task): honest units — "Pairs completed" (pairs), "Shares stranded" (shares), "Avg cost to complete a pair" ($/pair), "Avg P/L per stranded share" ($/share); `ExplainerDto.comprehensive_pnl = total_pnl + rebate_estimate` with the explainer note showing the full chain (`completed + stuck = realized (X); + rebate (Y) → total (Z)`). Reconciliation tests: `completed_pair_pnl + stuck_leg_pnl + estimated_rebate == comprehensive_pnl` (analytics `attribution.rs` + dashboard `summary_reconciles_*`), and `equity == starting + realized` for a fully-settled history (dashboard `account_three_card_identity_through_settlement`: through open→closed→resolved, `Cash + Open == Equity == starting` while open, then Open drops to `$0` and `Equity == Cash == starting + realized`). Controls "Current equity" relabeled "Cash balance" (it is `collateral_total`); the equity chart is now true equity. **(4) `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md` Step 2b — settlement/redemption parity note** ⚠️ NOT WIRED: paper settles instantly at resolution; the live adapter MUST auto-redeem resolved CTF positions on-chain (gas + relayer + retry, merge matched pairs) on the same `market_resolved` trigger, or the §9 paper/live parity is violated (live cash/equity would lag the paper model and capital would not recycle); tracked in the closing "Open gaps" list (`/trading/ctf/redeem`, `/trading/ctf/merge`, `/trading/gasless`). **Verified (this box):** `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` clean; clippy `-D warnings` clean on venue-paper/dashboard/analytics; touched files rustfmt-clean; tests green — venue-paper 62, analytics 58, dashboard 32 lib + 12 endpoints (incl. previously-`assert!(!inventory.is_null())` `windows_list_*` which passes since a resolved-in-grace window keeps its Live-view inventory while being excluded from open positions) + 8 + 4 + 2, and bot integration venue_parity 13 / risk_manager_paper 6 / analytics_replay 1 / inventory_replay 1. A live network paper smoke (`bot paper-sim`/`bot run`) is the operator's to run (needs Polymarket data + an NTP-synced clock); the deterministic driver-level equivalent passes. **Deferred:** a live on-chain auto-redeem/merge pipeline (the Step 2b gap); marking open positions to the live book instead of cost (deliberately not done — cost basis is the conservative, exactly-reconciling choice). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-11 — **model-lab `backtest_pair_lean` — the operator's pair-lean strategy over the full current-regime history (VERDICT: loses money at every setting; the pair structure is self-defeating).** New self-contained stage `model_lab/backtest_pair_lean.py` + `tests/test_backtest_pair_lean.py` (14) + a LightGBM-free offline check wired into `verify.py` (Part 4 of `check_historical_offline`) + README. **Research only; no Rust; no new pip deps.** Per window, when the walk-forward **dir10** model (`out/learn_walkforward/oos_dir10_full.parquet`, **no retraining**; `p_up`=P(10s-fwd mid Up)) predicts direction with confidence `|p_up−0.5|≥T`, **take the predicted side at the ask** (255ms effective, exact taker fee, displayed depth, sized to a fraction of displayed depth), then **rest a maker quote on the other side** at limit `L=C−price_s` so combined pair cost ≤ C; a completed pair collects $1 (locked profit `1−C` minus the leaned taker fee), an uncompleted leg **rides to resolution**. Distinct from `money_judge._run_pairleg` (taker cross): the completion is a **resting maker** (zero fee, fills only when the recorded opposite ask reaches ≤ L). **Operator params (AskUserQuestion):** T=`|p−0.5|≥θ` over `mj.SWEEP_THRESHOLDS`; C∈{0.90,0.94,0.96,0.98} (pair-cost ceilings); lean size = fraction of displayed ask depth {0.25,0.5,1.0}; **multi-fire** within the $10/window budget. **Architecture:** reuses `money_judge`'s fill primitives (`_book_asof`/`_leaned_quote`/`_side_of`/`lm.taker_fee`) + `backtest_momentum`'s reconstruction (`_day_grid_and_ring`/`_grid_bars`/`_window_model_rows`) + `historical_resolutions.load_resolution_map` + `io/telonex_pm`; regime floor `2026-06-05` / fires/regime helpers reimplemented locally (importing `learn_walkforward` would pull in lightgbm via `lib/gbt`). Vectorized first-crossing maker-completion; budget charged on the leaned leg only so the fire set depends only on (θ,depth_frac) (completions swept over C in one book pass). **Memory-bounded (24/7-safe):** per-(series,day) AGGREGATES only (no per-trade retention), resumable atomic checkpoints; OOS load via `series` predicate-pushdown so per-series parallel invocations sharing one `--out-name` each hold a slice. **Baselines on IDENTICAL windows** (reproduced EXACTLY, the acid test that the reconstruction matches `learn_walkforward`): replayed momentum **$5,056.69** == vps255 reference, model-taker (dir10, best θ=0.03) **$2,284.01** == reference, over **24,697** windows == reference; never-trade $0. **Fidelity fix:** momentum must run on EVERY reconstructed window (not just OOS windows) — `_process_day` adds each reconstructed window to `win_by_key`/`model_rows` and only conditionally builds the OOS fires (matching `_money_score`). **Controls:** matched-frequency random-trigger (crc32-seeded, 10 seeds, net-only, p=fraction of seeds ≥ real) + post-hoc shuffled-labels (per-(series,day) outcome permutation recomputed from the day's rows — a locked pair pays $1 regardless, so shuffling only re-marks the stranded legs). **Run:** 4 series × 33 days = 132 (series,day), 120-config sweep, run **4-way parallel** (one worker per series, ~2.7GB total; I/O-bound on cold Telonex `.zst` decompression, ~75min wall) then a 5s all-series finalize over the checkpoints. **VERDICT (real, over 24,697 windows):** **0/120 configs are net-positive.** Best overall = T≥0.25 / C=0.90 / lean=0.5 → net **−$5,571.86** (20,189 trades, 68% win, 76% pair-completion) vs momentum **+$5,057**, model-taker **+$2,284**, never-trade **$0**. **Root cause (the locked/stranded split):** locked pairs make **+$14,258** but the stranded legs lose **−$19,830** — the maker completion is **anti-hedging / adversely selected**: it completes (locks a thin $0.10 margin) exactly when the leaned side is WINNING (opposite ask falls to L), and STRANDS the leaned leg to full loss exactly when the leaned side is LOSING (opposite stays expensive). So the strategy caps its winners at +$0.10 while keeping losers fully exposed, on a weak (53%-win) dir10 lean. Per-series ALL negative (BTC-5m −$2,510, ETH-5m −$820, BTC-15m −$902, ETH-15m −$1,030), every best config converging on C=0.90 (max locked margin) + high θ (most selective) = least-bad. **Controls confirm the diagnosis, not a fluke:** pair-lean BEATS the matched-frequency random-trigger control (−$5,572 vs −$12,620, p=0.00, 3/4 series) — the dir10 signal genuinely helps *timing* — but sits at the BOTTOM of the shuffled-labels distribution (real −$5,572 vs shuffled **+$61,468**, p=1.00): with RANDOM outcomes the strategy would print money, proving the losses come precisely from the real signal→outcome→completion correlation (the completion mechanism is structurally anti-correlated with outcomes), not from fees. **Honest takeaway: a resting-maker pair-lean on a weak directional signal is structurally a loser — the completion converts winning bets into thin locked margins and leaves losing bets fully stranded; momentum ($5,057) remains the only current-regime strategy that makes money.** Outputs `out/backtests/pair_lean/{metrics.json, sweep.csv, per_series_best.csv, report.html}` (aggregates only). **Verification:** 14 unit tests (lock/strand/`price_s≥C`/budget/determinism/controls/aggregation/end-to-end) + `verify` PASS + full suite **260 passed** + two real-data smokes + the momentum/model-taker/window-count acid test. **Caveats (in `CAVEATS`):** maker fill at limit L when the recorded opposite ask first reaches ≤ L (Telonex has no PM trade tape; mildly optimistic vs §9 queue-behind-displayed); maker rebate not credited; single top-of-book level; float book space; flat 255ms venue delay valid only within the current regime. **Deferred:** a hedged variant that completes the LOSING side (true insurance) rather than the winning side; per-market fee rate; a bounded/`--since` journal window if the OOS grows. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-12 — **Shadow mode implemented — live champion-model observation (BUILD_PLAN 12–13), influences NOTHING.** New `shadow` crate + `[shadow]` config + `bot run` wiring + dashboard tile + 3 model-lab modules; the champion `dir10_full` walk-forward LightGBM now predicts live in Rust every 5 s per active window (BTC/ETH × 5m/15m), journals to a side channel, and is provably isolated from order flow. **(A) Champion export (`model_lab/export_champion.py`, new):** the walk-forward discards every fold's booster (only OOS parquets survive), so this reproduces `learn_walkforward._final_model` deterministically (full history, 2M-row cap, `num_boost_round=590` + `seed=20260710` from `out/learn_walkforward/metrics.json`) → `models/model_dir10_full.txt` (1.08 MB, 590 trees) + `.meta.json` (identity: sha256 / `trained_through_ms`=2026-07-09 / seed, drives the staleness alert) + the Rust export-parity fixtures (`crates/shadow/tests/fixtures/{model_dir10_full.txt, parity_cases.json}`, 51 cases incl. NaN- and exactly-0-bearing rows). Training uses ALL finite-`fwd_up_10s` rows (no coverage filter — matches `_prep_target`), float32 with NaN kept. **(B) `shadow` crate (§4, deps `core-types`/`model`/`feed-util` only — NOT `bot`/`engine`/`venue-*`/`dashboard`):** sans-IO core + async driver. **Inference = a pure-Rust `model.txt` tree-walker (`lgbm/`), the allowlist-clean "ONNX equivalent" — NO new dep** (ONNX/tract are forbidden §3 heavyweights). Reproduces LightGBM `NumericalDecision` exactly: `<=` (not `<`) split, per-node `decision_type` bits (default-left + missing-type None/Zero/NaN: a NaN at a non-NaN node → 0.0 then compare; missing → default dir), leaf = `~child`, `raw = Σ leaf_values` (shrinkage/boost-from-average baked in), `p_up = sigmoid(sigmoid_coeff·raw)`; f32 features widened to f64. **Note the model.txt `feature_names` are `Column_0..23`** (numpy training) so feature ORDER is the positional contract pinned by the Rust `FeatureVector` assembly (`Column_i`=`FULL_FEATURES[i]`), not name-matching; parser asserts `max_feature_idx==23` + `num_cat==0` (loud `CategoricalSplit` error otherwise). Export-parity test: **1e-6 vs `booster.predict` on all 51 fixture cases — GREEN against the real 590-tree champion.** **Features (24, in `FULL_FEATURES` order):** `sigma_1s` REUSES `model::VolEstimator` + `z`/`p_up_model`/`log_s_k` reuse `model::norm_cdf` (parity by construction); only `realized_vol` (pandas `ewm(hl=60).std()`, adjust=True/bias=False/ignore_na=False) and `basis_ewma` (pandas `ewm(hl=120,min30,ignore_na=True).mean()`) are reimplemented (`features/ewma.rs`, golden-tested to 1e-9 vs pandas — the #1 parity hazard, isolated). Per-asset `PriceState` maintains the per-second Binance-mid bar chain + the 3 continuous EWMAs (advanced on bar completion); `z = clip((log(mid/K)+basis_ewma)/(σ√τ), ±39)` basis-corrected exactly per `dataset._derive_asof_features`; strike from a reused `model::StrikeCapture` (Chainlink-at-open, frozen). Depth (8, `features/depth.rs` = `short_horizon._frame_depth_features`) from shadow's OWN Binance `depth20@100ms` feed (`depth_feed.rs`, mirrors `depth_capture` transport; `depth_capture` holds nothing in-process). PM (6, `features/pm.rs` = `build_pm_grid`) from `Event::TopOfBook` (the Up token). **Design note:** features are read **as of the last completed bar**, which lags the offline reconstruction (whose as-of bar is the current second's close) by up to ~1 s — an inherent streaming-vs-batch gap the LIVE guard measures/tolerates. Sampling is window-aligned (`ts = floor(now/5000)·5000`, matching the lab's `open + k·5 s`); filtered to M5/M15 (the model's `tau_secs` range is [5, 900] — 1h is OOD). **(C) Journaling = side-channel writer (`record.rs`, `depth_capture` precedent) — NO new `Event`/`JournalRecord` variant** (no exhaustive-`match Event` ripple): `ShadowPrediction{ts, series, window_open_ms, p_up, 24 features (NaN→JSON null), features_hash, model identity, strike + as-of diagnostics}` → own `data/shadow/shadow-{YYYYMMDD}.jsonl.gz` gzip series (dedicated OS-thread writer, bounded drop-on-full channel). **(D) Isolation & wiring (`crates/bot/src/run.rs`):** new `Task::Shadow`, **non-critical** (`critical()` excludes it — death never cancels-all, backoff-restart with current-window re-seed like clob), gated on `config.shadow.enable`, fed a **drop-on-full `mpsc::Sender<Event>` clone** the orchestrator `try_send`s in the `bus_rx` arm (Arc-cheap; never back-pressures). **`order_path_open` is NOT modified — shadow holds no `bus_tx`/`VenuePort`, so it is structurally incapable of reaching a venue or stalling the engine.** Model+identity loaded at boot (fail-fast) only when enabled; dashboard updates ride a dedicated `ShadowUpdate` channel drained to `handle.set_shadow`. **Order-flow-identical proof (`crates/bot/tests/shadow_order_flow.rs`):** drives `RiskManager`→`PaperVenue` through one scripted flow (converge + model-jump repricing) TWICE — shadow ON (fed a clone, fake depth transport) vs OFF — and asserts **byte-identical** normalized `VenueEvent` streams. **GREEN.** **(E) Config `[shadow]` (off by default):** `enable`/`model_path`/`model_meta_path`/`sample_secs 5`/`depth_ws_url`(empty→reuse feeds)/`shadow_dir`/`staleness_alert_days 30`/`bus|depth|record_channel_cap`/`max_staleness_secs 120`;
