validate + `default.toml` + anti-drift pin (config 82 tests green). **(F) Dashboard "Shadow" tile** (`dashboard/src/shadow.rs` + `set_shadow` setter + `ShadowDto` + `GET /api/shadow` + `WsUpdate::Shadow` + Summary-view tile): per-series predictions/min + last p_up + feature coverage + the **"model stale, refit due"** flag (`now − trained_through > staleness_alert_days`). Dashboard has NO `shadow` dep — the orchestrator threads a dashboard-owned `ShadowTick`. **(G) Feature-parity guards — the mandatory basis-bug tripwire, two tiers:** (a) EXPORT parity = the 1e-6 Rust walker test (`just shadow-parity`); (b) LIVE parity (`model_lab/shadow_parity.py`, new, `just shadow-parity-live`) joins shadow's journaled features to the offline `short_horizon.parquet` on `(series, window_open_ms, sample_ts_ms)` and grades per-feature (reuses `telonex_binance_validate._feature_stats`): tight (corr>=0.99, abs_ratio in [0.95,1.05]) for the 10 price features, looser (corr>=0.95, [0.8,1.25]) for depth/PM; a scale/basis bug FAILs loudly (proven: a 3× `sigma_1s` bug → abs_ratio 3.0 out of band). `report_shadow.py` (new) = the smoke-report generator (predictions/series/day + coverage + model identity). Both wired into `verify` (a synthetic in-memory parity check — pass on clean, fail on a scale bug); `verify` PASS. **Verification (this box):** `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` + `cargo clippy -p shadow -p config -p dashboard -p bot --all-targets -D warnings` clean; touched files rustfmt-clean; shadow 27 lib + 1 export-parity(1e-6) + bot `shadow_order_flow`(identical) + dashboard 3 shadow + config 82 all GREEN; Python `verify` PASS + `pytest tests/test_shadow_parity.py` 5 passed. **Env constraint:** the operator's 24/7 `bot run` (a live process holding `target/debug/bot.exe`, unkillable this session) blocks relinking the bot binary — `cargo check`/`clippy` are unaffected (rmeta only); the `shadow_order_flow` run + full `cargo test -p bot` were done in a separate `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`. **Operator to run:** (1) `export_champion` is already run (model committed); to deploy, stop the running `bot run`, rebuild, set `[shadow] enable=true`, restart — shadow rides the existing 24/7 paper soak. (2) The **24 h smoke report is operational** — enable shadow, let a day accrue (~17 280 predictions/series/day), then `python -m model_lab.report_shadow` + `python -m model_lab.shadow_parity` (needs `short_horizon` built over the same period). **Deferred:** tuning the LIVE-guard tolerances once real shadow data exists; a standalone `bot shadow` smoke; per-series shadow models; and (out of scope by design) any path from shadow into fair value — the observation phase's invariant is that it influences nothing. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-13 — **Taker-rebate modeling + self-match audit.** Five deliverables; touches `model-lab` (Python) + `venue-paper`/`config`/`dashboard`/`bot` (Rust) + `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md`. No new deps; no new `Event`/`VenueEvent`/journal surface. **(1) Rebate mechanics VERIFIED (docs "Taker Rebates" + news coverage):** the rebate is a **REFUND of taker FEES** (`tier% × taker fees paid`), NOT a % of notional — the docs page leaves the base textually undefined and "applies going forward" is purely *non-retroactive*, but 3–50% of notional is economically impossible and multiple sources say "Taker Fee Rebate Program with up to 50% **refund** on taker fees" (parallel to the maker program's "% of taker fees"). **Tier = 30-day *rolling* weighted volume** `wV = size·(1−entry)·category_weight·bonus` (crypto weight **2.3**, no crypto bonus): None `<$2k`·Bronze `$2k`→3%·Silver `$20k`→8%·Gold `$200k`→18%·Platinum `$1M`→32%·Diamond `$4M`→44%·Obsidian `$10M`→50% (+ one-time level-up bonuses $10→$25k). Recalc daily, tier effective next update ("going forward"), **paid daily 00:00 UTC in pUSD**, $1 min accrual. **Corrects the prior "immaterial → 0%" finding** — see the real result in (2). **(2) model-lab `rebate_sim` stage** (`model_lab/rebate_sim.py`, generic over any taker-fill `trades.csv`): per exec-day `wv_day`/`fees_day`; `tier_D` from the trailing 30 completed days `[D-30…D-1]` (non-retroactive); `rebate_day = tier_D% × fees_day`; $1 daily-accrual carry; reports tier timeline, total rebate, **corrected net PnL** (`net + rebate`), and marginal-wV-to-next-tier + a wV/tier chart. Shared math added to `lib/math.py` (`TAKER_TIERS`, `CRYPTO_WV_WEIGHT=2.3`, `weighted_volume`, `taker_rebate_tier`, `taker_rebate_next_tier` — mirror the Rust consts). `money_judge._write_outputs` now also dumps the winning-threshold model-taker fills to `out/money_judge/trades.csv` (same header as `backtest_momentum`) so rebate_sim can be run on the model-taker; the model-taker run is `python -m model_lab.rebate_sim --trades out/money_judge/trades.csv --out-name model_taker` after a `money_judge` run (its OOS+journal pipeline was NOT regenerated in this task). Wired into `verify.py` (crafted None→Bronze→Silver frame + the money_judge trades.csv) + `tests/test_rebate_sim.py` (13) + README. **Real headline (current-regime momentum tape `out/backtests/momentum_current_regime/vps255/trades.csv`, 28,411 trades / 33 days):** rebate **+$737.43 = 13% of the $5,659.61 in taker fees**, lifting net **$5,056.69 → $5,794.12**, reaching **Gold (18%)** at $411,267 30-day wV — the momentum strategy generates enough taker volume that the rebate is materially non-zero. **(3) Rust report-only taker-rebate line** (`venue-paper`) — **operator decision: REPORT-ONLY**, never added to `collateral`/paper equity (the more conservative choice; never inflates paper equity with a projected rebate). `PaperWallet` gained `taker_rebate_accrued`/`taker_rebate_credited` (report-only lines), a rolling 30-bucket daily-wV `VecDeque` ring + `today_taker_wv`/`today_taker_fees`/`last_day_taker_fees`, and `accrue_taker(wv,fee)` (does NOT touch cash) + `credit_taker_rebate(tier_pct,$1)` (rebate = tier% × today's taker fees, moved accrued→credited on the $1 floor without touching cash, then today's wV rolls into the ring — tier from the ring *before* today, matching "going forward"). Tier table + `2.3` weight are code consts in `wallet.rs` (mirror `lib/math.py`). `engine.rs`: taker fills in `activate_marketable` call `accrue_taker` + arm the daily cycle; `run_rebate_cycle` credits the taker rebate alongside the maker one; gated on new `PaperParams.taker_rebate_enabled` (default true). **The conservation identity + shadow-oracle property test are UNCHANGED** (report-only ⇒ no `+ taker_rebate_credited` cash term). `PaperLedgerSnapshot` extended with the taker fields + tier/pct/next-tier/wv-to-next. Config `paper.taker_rebate_enabled` (default true) → `default.toml` + validate + `paper_params` + `dashboard.rs` ParamsView; anti-drift `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` holds. **Simplification note:** the daily cycle reuses the existing 24h-from-first-accrual timer (not strict 00:00 UTC) for parity with the maker cycle; the "30-day window" is 30 rolling 24h buckets; a multi-period catch-up attributes to one bucket (rare — the driver on_clocks constantly). New venue-paper tests: 5 wallet unit (tier boundaries, wV formula, report-only-cash, trailing-tier + $1 carry mirroring the Python crafted case → $27.20, ring-caps-at-30) + 2 engine (accrual through a real marketable fill + daily cycle; disabled-flag records nothing). **(4) Dashboard tile** — `TakerRebateDto` (`From<&PaperLedgerSnapshot>`, `projected_per_day = tier% × last_day_taker_fees`) on `LedgerDto` (→ `/api/overview`) and `SummaryDto` (→ `/api/summary`, the calm landing); a Summary-view "Taker rebate — simulated, not in equity" tile (30-day wV, tier pill, projected/day, credited, marginal-to-next-tier) in `ui/{index.html,app.js}` reusing the shadow-tile pattern. **(5) Self-match audit — VERDICT + hard blocker (doc only, NOT implemented):** **paper venue = NO, structurally impossible** (a marketable FAK walks only the feed-fed `SimBook.asks`; our own resting paper orders are in a disjoint `orders` map; resting fills only from external `Event::LastTrade`) ⇒ paper PnL is free of self-trade artifacts. **Live engine = NO client-side safeguard exists** — takers hold no `RestingView`, the risk guard checks only halt/loss/notional, no self-trade logic anywhere; the **momentum** taker is *incidentally* protected by its edge gate (`fair ≤ ask ⇒ BookAlreadyRepriced`, our quotes rest on the far side of fair) but the **late-window certainty taker has NO edge gate** (buys any ask ≤ 0.99) → it is the one path that could lift our own §7-mirror quote; whether a self-trade then trades is left to Polymarket's server-side STP, which our code neither invokes nor documents. ⇒ effectively YES for the live path → recorded a **hard pre-live blocker in `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md` (new Step 5b + Open-gaps #3)**: blocks enabling the taker modules (`taker_budget_per_window > 0`) at scale until prevention lands (give takers `RestingView` access + skip / cancel-own-quote-first + a risk-gateway self-cross veto; late-window taker is the priority). At the recommended first-live config (takers OFF) it cannot occur — paper can never reproduce it, so it will not surface in the paper evaluation. **Operator decisions:** rebate credited = REPORT-ONLY; blocker → committed `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md` (the named `out/audits/pre_live_checklist.md` doesn't exist / `out/` is gitignored); model-taker via a `money_judge` trades.csv export + documented command. **Verification:** Python `model_lab.verify` PASS (chain now includes `→ rebate_sim →`), `pytest tests/test_rebate_sim.py` 13 + `test_money_judge`/`test_math` 25 green; Rust `venue-paper` 69 (5 wallet + 2 engine new), `config` 82 (incl. anti-drift), `dashboard` 35 lib + integration green; `cargo clippy -p venue-paper -p config -p dashboard --all-targets -D warnings` clean; `cargo check -p bot --all-targets` clean. **Env constraint:** the operator's live 24/7 `bot run` (PID holds `target/debug/bot.exe`) blocks relinking any `-p bot` target, so the bot integration tests (venue_parity/momentum/quote_manager/late_window/risk_manager_paper) were run in a separate `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`; the taker-rebate change is provably inert to the VenueEvent stream (report-only, emits no event, touches no cash/fill/order), and the venue-paper suite already covers marketable taker fills + conservation. **Deferred:** strict-00:00-UTC daily cycle if intraday tier changes ever matter; per-market `fee.rate` in rebate_sim (currently 0.07); the self-match prevention implementation (blocker only); crediting the taker rebate to paper cash if the operator later wants it in equity. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-13 — **Model integration behind a config switch — the champion model becomes a live PAPER taker.** The `shadow` crate's per-window `p_up` stream now (optionally) drives a real taker in paper; also closes the §7 self-match blocker for ALL taker paths. Touches `engine` (new modules + wiring) + `shadow` (1 additive field) + `config` (new section) + `bot` (mapper/drain/recorder) + `dashboard` (tile + PnL-by-driver) + `LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md`. **No new deps; no new `core_types::Event`/`VenueEvent`/`JournalRecord` variant.** **Operator decisions (AskUserQuestion):** (1) model-taker is **per-window** (a `HashMap<WindowId, ModelWindowState>`, trades all four M5/M15 series concurrently — because shadow feeds a prediction per active window; momentum/late stay single-`active`, structurally unchanged); (2) arbitration = **conflict-window ledger** (both takers active on all series; precedence suppresses the loser only while the winner actually fired that same window recently); (3) budgets **independent** per taker (the RiskManager per-window worst-case-loss breaker bounds combined exposure; "contention" reported as cap-vetoed fires). **The tested recipe replicated EXACTLY** (`model-lab/money_judge.py::_run_taker`, the dir10 @ θ=0.03 that scored +$2,284): fire iff `|p_up−0.5| ≥ θ` (0.03), buy the predicted side (`p_up≥0.5+θ`→Up token, `≤0.5−θ`→Down), FAK sized `min(displayed depth, $10 window budget / price)`, $1 min notional, **NO edge/fee gate, NO price cap, NO cooldown** (the ~5 s prediction cadence throttles), holds to resolution. `p_up` = P(10 s-forward mid Up), NOT the analytic window fair — the model taker never reads `ModelSnapshot`. **Engine (new, all `pub(crate)`):** (a) `arbitration.rs` — `FireLedger{arbitration_window_ms, precedence: HashMap<Asset,TakerId>, last_fire: HashMap<(WindowId,TakerId),i64>}` + `TakerId{Momentum,Model}`; `check(who,window,now)` = winner (per `window.series.asset`) always Ok, loser blocked while the winner fired within the window; `record` on every fire, `drop_window` on close; **`FireLedger::default()` (empty precedence, 0 window) suppresses nobody** so momentum is unchanged when the model is off. (b) `self_match.rs` — pure `filter_asks(outcome, asks, window, resting: Option<&RestingView>) -> Vec<BookLevel>`: for each ask at `p`, subtract the remaining size of our live resting BUY on `outcome.opposite()` at `p.complement()` (§7 mirror) in the same window; skip a fully-covered level, shrink a partial one. Uses `Outcome::opposite()` + `Price::complement()`. **Applied in ALL THREE takers' `decide`** (momentum/late/model) before the book walk; naturally scoped (the quoter's `RestingView` holds only its single active window, so it is a no-op unless a taker fires the quoter's window). (c) `model_taker/{mod,edge,driver}.rs` — `ModelTaker` mirrors momentum (committed-in-flight budget, `our_orders`, risk veto) but **per-window** (`windows: HashMap<WindowId,_>`, `order_window: HashMap<OrderId,WindowId>` routes fills); fires ONLY on `on_prediction(pred, port, now, arbiter, resting) -> ModelTakeOutcome` (bus `Window`/`Book`/`Risk` only refresh state); `plan_model_take` walks asks with no gate + optional cap; `ModelPrediction{series,window_open_ms,ts,p_up,finite_count,model_stale}` (engine-local, built from `ShadowUpdate`); client id `md:{open_ms}:{seq}`; `NoModelTakeReason` (15 variants incl. `ArbitrationSuppressed`/`PlaceRejected`). **Engine (wiring):** `RiskParams` gained `model_enabled`(**default false**), `model: ModelTakerParams`, `arbitration_window_ms`(3000), `series_precedence`(**fortress map default {Btc→Momentum, Eth→Model}** via `default_series_precedence()`); `RiskManager` gained `model`+`arbiter` fields, `on_model_prediction` (guard-interposed; returns None if disabled — does NOT run the fold cycle, the guard + the taker's `standing_down` are the §11 protection), `model_*` inspectors, and threads `&mut self.arbiter` + `self.quoter.resting_view()` (disjoint field borrows — the borrow-checker hot spot, validated) into momentum + model, `resting` into late, in `forward_bus_event` (also `arbiter.drop_window` on close). Momentum's `decide` gained one arbitration gate (`TakerId::Momentum`) so it defers on ETH per the fortress map. **`FillDriver{MakerCore,Momentum,Late,Model}` + `RiskManager::driver_of(order_id)`** (each strategy gained `owns()`) — queried at fill time (order still owned) for per-driver PnL attribution, since `Fill` carries no client_id (adding one = 41-site churn, rejected). **Shadow:** `ShadowUpdate` gained `window_open_ms` (additive, populated at the emit site). **Config `[model_taker]`** (off by default): `enable`/`kill_switch`/`theta`(0.03)/`budget_per_window`($10)/`min_finite_count`(24)/`max_prediction_age_ms`(15000, the >15 s stale/missing stand-down)/`max_book_staleness_ms`(2000)/`arbitration_window_ms`(3000)/`series: BTreeSet<Series>`(the 4 M5/M15)/`precedence_btc`+`precedence_eth: Driver{momentum,model}` (per-asset, avoids serde map-key issues). **Cross-section validation: `model_taker.enable` requires `shadow.enable`** (its prediction source). `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` + `risk_params(&AppConfig::default())==RiskParams::default()` both hold (model off, fortress-map precedence default on both sides). **Bot (`run.rs`):** `model_taker_params`/`series_precedence` mappers; the shadow-drain arm now also builds a `ModelPrediction` and calls `risk.on_model_prediction` **only when** `config.model_taker.is_active(series)` (enable+kill+allowlist) AND `order_path_open` (armed+not-halted+critical-deps-alive) AND the prediction is fresh (`now−ts ≤ max_prediction_age_ms`) — stale/missing/non-allowlisted are silently not-forwarded (never touches quoter/momentum); the outcome is journaled + pushed to the dashboard tile. `model_taker_record.rs` — a side-channel gzip recorder (shadow/depth_capture precedent) writing `ModelTakerDecision{ts,series,window,p_up,finite_count,model_stale,fired,reason,outcome/shares/notional/worst_price}` (fired AND suppressed+why) to `data/model-taker/model-taker-{YYYYMMDD}.jsonl.gz`; spawned when the model can fire, `finish()`ed at shutdown; fills tagged via `driver_of` in `handle_venue_event`. **Dashboard:** `model_taker.rs` — `ModelTakerState` (per-series fires/min + fires/suppressed totals + last p_up, fed by `set_model_taker`) + `DriverPnlState` (buffers driver-tagged fills per window via `record_driver_fill`, marks them to the resolved outcome at `Event::Settlement`, bounded pending-cap; `Σ over drivers ≈ realized_pnl`); `GET /api/model-taker` → `ModelTakerDto{active, series[], pnl_by_driver[]}`; a Summary-view "Model taker" tile (fires/day per series + a PnL-by-driver table) polled like the shadow tile. **`LIVE_PREFLIGHT.md` Step 5b + Open-gaps #3 flipped from "NOT WIRED / HARD BLOCKER" → "✅ IMPLEMENTED for all taker paths via `engine::self_match::filter_asks`; needs live-mode verification"** (paper can't reproduce a self-cross, so it's unit-proven only — skip-not-cancel-first; a risk-gateway self-cross veto is deferred). **Verification:** `cargo clippy -p engine -p config -p dashboard -p shadow --all-targets -D warnings` clean; new files rustfmt-clean (pre-existing CRLF files left untouched, §2.2). Tests green: **engine 223** (incl. new `arbitration`/`self_match` + `model_taker` decide/edge/per-window unit tests + migrated momentum/late `decide` call sites with `&FireLedger::default()`/`None`), **config 85** (incl. anti-drift + new `[model_taker]` tests), **dashboard 37** (incl. `driver_pnl_marks_at_settlement`), **bot `model_taker_paper` 3** (prediction fires+fills 12.5 @ 0.80 with exact `taker_fee` off journal record→replay; a forwarded breaker stands it down; the open-notional gate vetoes → `PlaceRejected`) + `model_taker_record` unit. **Env constraint (unchanged):** the operator's live 24/7 `bot run` holds `target/debug/bot.exe`, so all `-p bot` builds/tests ran in a separate `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`; the full bot suite (momentum/late/quote_manager/risk_manager_paper/venue_parity + `shadow_order_flow`, which stays byte-identical since the model is off by default) was run there. **Operator next step (req 7):** enable `[shadow]`+`[model_taker]` on all four series (fortress precedence), NTP-sync the clock, run 48 h in paper, then report per series — fires/day + PnL-by-driver (maker-core vs momentum vs model), conflict count + arbitration resolution, budget/cap contention, breaker interactions — from the decision journal (`data/model-taker/`) + the dashboard tile. **Deferred:** a risk-gateway self-cross veto (defense-in-depth beyond the per-taker filter); shared cross-taker budget reconciliation (independent budgets chosen); config exposure of the decision-dir/channel-cap; a runtime control-plane model-taker toggle (config `kill_switch` + the global Manual-kill breaker cover on/off today); fixing the single-instance limitation for momentum/late/quoter (only the model taker is per-window); live-execution pipeline replacing paper under the same wiring. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-14 — **model-lab `accuracy_push` + `backtest_burst_pair` — the operator's high-confidence burst-pair-building strategy test. VERDICT: does NOT beat what we have (≈ breakeven, 36× below momentum).** Two new Python stages (no Rust; opt-in LightGBM; research only). **(A) `accuracy_push.py`** — the per-market **15-second** model producer the strategy needed (nothing existing fit: `horizon_sweep` is per-market/15s but persists no predictions; `learn_short_gbt` fwd15 is pooled + covers only BTC/ETH-5m in the current regime; `learn_walkforward` is dir10/10s pooled). Trains the champion GBT (`lib/gbt`) on `historical_dataset.parquet` **per market** (all 4 series covered, 2025-10→2026-07), screens **price** (10 price/vol/formula/basis) vs **flow** (+14 depth/PM order-flow microstructure = champion 24; the 4-series matrix has NO signed-aggressor-flow columns, so "flow" = the microstructure block — documented), picks the winner on a held-out pre-regime tail ("flow if it wins; else best available"), refits the winner on ALL pre-regime (purged at the regime boundary), predicts the current-regime windows **OOS** (leakage-safe — one deployable model/market, not a walk-forward ensemble). `θ_85` = smallest gate `|p−0.5|≥θ` where held-out accuracy hits 85% on the regime OOS. **Flow won every market**; **θ_85 ≈ 0.325–0.340 at 0.15–1.5% coverage ⇒ 0.18–1.20 qualifying signals/window** — an 85%-accurate 15s gate is brutally selective. Peak RSS 5.1GB (loads a market's full history; over the 3GB advisory but fine on the 16GB box — bound with `--series`/`--days` on smaller boxes). `out/accuracy_push/{oos_<market>.parquet, model_<market>_<variant>.txt, metrics.json, frontier.csv, report.html}`. **(B) `backtest_burst_pair.py`** — the strategy (built on the `backtest_conf_pair` template + `money_judge` fills + `backtest_momentum` reconstruction). Per qualifying signal: a **burst** (3×20-share FAK consuming ONE displayed Telonex level in turn, no replenishment ⇒ conservative; depth shortfall reported) on the leaned side, then a **completion maker** at `limit = C − price_s` (pair cost ≤ C, locked edge **1−C** — vs conf_pair's break-even 1.00 hedge), inventory capped at **U** unhedged (completions free the cap, event-driven; no force-hedge; no entry in the final 30s; unpaired legs ride to the official resolution). Sweep **C∈{0.94,0.96,0.98} × U∈{20,40,60}** (9 cells). P&L splits EXACTLY into locked-pairs + stranded-legs (reconciles < $0.01). **Benchmarks on identical windows** (all in the same per-day Telonex reconstruction): never-trade $0, engine **momentum** + champion **dir10 model-taker** (θ=0.03), both at the engine's $10/window budget; + a base-rate-preserving **shuffled-outcome control**. Resumable per-(series,day); run per-series in parallel sharing `--out-name` then a final all-series pass aggregates. **Design decisions (operator deferred all to me):** burst = single-snapshot sequential consume (conservative, no replenishment); one completion attempt/lot (conservative); shuffled control = per-(series,day) outcome permutation (a locked pair pays $1 regardless ⇒ shuffle only re-marks stranded legs — for a pair strategy, real>shuffled means the *stranded* component is signal-driven, but the locked arb is outcome-independent and appears in shuffled too). **RESULTS (all 24,697 current-regime windows, 255ms, real fees):** benchmarks reproduce the known refs **exactly** — momentum **$5,057** (=vps255), dir10 **$2,284** (=learn_walkforward) — validating the reconstruction. Best cell **C0.94/U20 net +$141** (8/9 cells LOSE): = locked-pairs **+$3,523** (the 1−C completion arb, biggest at C=0.94) almost exactly canceled by stranded-legs **−$3,382** (35% completion ⇒ 65% of shares stranded; the 15s θ_85 signal only weakly predicts the *window* outcome). Shuffled −$288 < real +$141 (the ~$429 the signal adds to stranded legs is real but trivial; the +$3,523 locked arb is present in shuffled too and is partly an artifact of the optimistic maker-fill proxy). **500–1000 sh/window is physically impossible** — inventory maxes at 240 sh (typical 60 = one burst), blocked by **signal scarcity** (only 4,165/24,697 = 17% of windows trade; 0.60 signals/window), then the U cap in rare signal-rich windows (up to 43 signals), then depth (25% burst shortfall). Per-series (fixed best cell): 15m marginally positive (BTC-15m +$364, ETH-15m +$83 — the θ_85-selective *directional* stranded component works slightly there), 5m negative (BTC-5m −$247, ETH-5m −$59). **Bottom line: momentum ($5,057) remains the only clearly-profitable current-regime strategy; burst-pair is ≈ breakeven under optimistic completion assumptions and loses under realistic ones — consistent with the prior pair_lean/conf_pair losses.** Unit tests: `tests/test_accuracy_push.py` (5: abstention/θ_85 finder + a lightgbm `_fit_predict` clean-signal recovery) + `tests/test_backtest_burst_pair.py` (11: burst shortfall, locked 1−C edge, stranded settlement, cap-U block, final-30s, reconciliation, shuffle-only-moves-stranded, key round-trip) — 16/16 pass. README entries added. **Deferred:** walk-forward-within-regime accuracy_push (currently one static pre-regime model/market); a realistic (queue-behind-displayed) completion-fill model to stress the locked arb; a fixture-based `verify` hook (kept standalone like backtest_momentum/pair_lean, needs real Telonex + accuracy_push OOS); wiring any of this into the engine (nothing earned it — burst-pair does not beat momentum). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-14 — **model-lab challenger bake-off — MLP / XGBoost / logistic-regression vs the LightGBM champion (VERDICT: nothing beats GBT by enough to matter; the champion stays).** Was [[burst-pair-strategy]]'s breakeven the *model's* fault or the *strategy's*? This swaps ONLY the model and holds everything else fixed — same `accuracy_push` static pre-regime→current-regime split, same θ_85 gate, same burst money over the identical **24,697** current-regime windows — across four model classes. Python-only; no Rust; no config/`Event` surface. **User decisions (AskUserQuestion):** (1) **static split** (`accuracy_push`'s methodology, NOT a rolling walk-forward — "same" = the setup that produced the best-2 cells); (2) **MLP = hand-rolled numpy** (not PyTorch/sklearn — sklearn's MLPClassifier has no dropout, which the spec requires). **Anti-drift (load-bearing):** extracted the leakage/purge-critical helpers `accuracy_push.{build_split_masks, capped_train_idx, inner_val_split}` as a single source of truth (`run_market` refactored to call them, byte-unchanged, regression-tested) so the split can never drift; the **GBT-backend-byte-identical-to-`run_market` test is the tripwire**. **Architecture:** new `lib/backends.py` = a uniform `Backend`/`Fitted` interface (`fit(x_tr,y_tr,x_val,y_val,*,seed,threads)->Fitted`; `predict_proba` hides best_iteration/standardization; `feature_importance` dict for gbt/xgb else None) over **unchanged** `lib/gbt.py` + `lib/logreg.py` and two new wrappers — `lib/xgb.py` (deterministic XGBoost: `tree_method="hist"`/`nthread=1`/fixed seed/no subsampling, native NaN, opt-in `[xgb]`/`[bakeoff]` extras + `requirements-xgb.txt`, `xgboost==2.1.4` numpy-2-compatible) and `lib/mlp.py` (pure-numpy MLP: input→NaN-aware-standardize (reused from logreg)→Dense(64)→ReLU→inverted-dropout(0.1)→Dense(32)→ReLU→dropout(0.1)→Dense(1)→sigmoid; He init; **AdamW** (decoupled L2 1e-4, lr 1e-3); mini-batch 8192; ≤200 epochs; **early stopping on val logloss, patience 15, restore-best**; float64; byte-deterministic same-seed at a fixed BLAS thread count). Lazy imports keep the numpy-only MLP/LogReg backends importable without the tree extras. **New stage `challenger_bakeoff.py`** (two subcommands): `train --backend {gbt,xgb,mlp,logreg}` runs the methodology per market (screen price/flow → refit winner on all pre-regime → predict regime OOS via the backend seam), resumable, writing the **8-column** `out/bakeoff/<b>/{oos_<market>.parquet, metrics.json (per-market `theta_gate` + accuracy/lift/Brier/logloss/ECE/reliability), frontier.csv, report.html}` + a **model-level shuffled-label control** (train on permuted labels → OOS collapses to chance, per `learn.py`); `aggregate` reads every model's model-level + money metrics and emits `out/bakeoff/{verdict.html, verdict.md, summary.csv}` with a two-part gate (Δ OOS diracc ≥ +1 pp AND Δ Brier < 0, AND money clears ±|shuffled_net| on both best-2 cells). **`backtest_burst_pair` additive, default-preserving flags:** `--oos-dir <dir>` (threads into `_load_theta_gates`/`_load_burst_fires` so any model's OOS + its OWN θ_85 gates drive the fires) and `--no-benchmarks` (skip the momentum+dir10 reconstruction — the real compute cost — and reuse the champion's reference numbers). All 9 C×U cells kept (restricting saves ~0 — the per-window book reconstruction dominates, `_simulate_window` is cheap); the aggregator headlines the champion's best-2 (`C0.94_U20`, `C0.98_U20`). **Run (real, full 13.79M-row `historical_dataset`, per-market 1.2M-cap fits, threads=1):** all 4 backends trained (mlp restarted mid-run with 8 BLAS threads — it is per-step-Python-overhead-bound so threading barely helped; its BTC-5m fit finished single-thread before the restart, others 8-thread → bit-level only, verdict unaffected). Money-scored logreg/xgb/mlp via `--oos-dir bakeoff/<b> --no-benchmarks` (4-series parallel, ~2.7 GB, resumable); gbt money = the existing `burst_pair` run (byte-identical OOS ⇒ identical money). All four reconcile over 24,697 windows. **RESULTS — pooled OOS diracc / Brier / ECE / θ85-reached:** gbt **0.6000 / 0.2341 / 0.0060 / 4-4**; xgb **0.6002 (+0.02 pp) / 0.2339 / 0.0059 / 4-4** (best calibration — a dead heat); mlp **0.5978 (−0.22 pp) / 0.2353 / 0.0184 / 2-4** (worst-calibrated, ~3× ECE); logreg **0.5997 (tie) / 0.2365 / 0.0107 / 0-4** (**never produces an 85%-accurate confident subset**). All four shuffled-label controls **collapse** (no leakage). **Money (best-2 cells net):** gbt +$141/−$178 (only non-negative cell); xgb −$833/−$871; logreg **−$8,023/−$8,115** (its non-selective fallback gate fires **1.76 sig/win = 3× the champion** of lower quality → far more stranded legs); mlp +$729/+$24 but +$729 sits **below** its own shuffled +$1250 (the outcome-independent locked-pair arb, not signal). **VERDICT: nothing beats GBT by enough to matter** — all four land within **0.24 pp** OOS diracc and **0.0024** Brier of each other (statistical noise over ~15k gated signals). Classic tabular-microstructure result: gradient boosting ≥ every alternative, XGBoost mirrors it, the linear floor is close but under-calibrated, the net is worst. **The burst-pair strategy was the ceiling, not the model.** **Tests: 378 pass full suite** (+38: `test_lib_xgb` 5, `test_lib_mlp` 6, `test_backends` 13, `test_challenger_bakeoff` 8 incl. the GBT byte-identity tripwire + per-backend recover-signal/shuffle-collapse, `test_accuracy_push` +2 split-helper regression, `test_backtest_burst_pair` +4 `--oos-dir`/`--no-benchmarks`); `verify` PASS. **Deferred:** a BLAS-thread-consistent mlp re-run of BTC-5m (verdict unaffected); per-series model comparison; wiring any challenger into the engine (none earned it — GBT stays the pick). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

(2) I build **clones + study only** — the parent owns the roster orchestrator, so each clone emits the shared `money_judge._TRADE_COLS` trades.csv + carries controls/capital/tier/bracket so it slots in; (3) **all money numbers over the post-2026-06-05 current-regime slice**, plus a per-clone **clone-vs-owner calibration ladder** — the owner's own official user-pnl delta over that slice (FACT) next to the clone net, gap printed not smoothed. **New files:** `competitors/manuals.py` (A+nagi+F compute), `competitors/manuals_report.py` (one HTML/account), `backtest_clones.py` (B/C/D/E). **Edits (additive):** `rebate_sim.py` `force_tier` override (credit every day at a fixed tier — C tier honesty; new `_TIER_PCT_BY_NAME`/`_forced_pct`, `--force-tier`); `money_judge.py` sell primitives `_leaned_sell_quote`/`_sell_at_bid`/`_mark_with_momentum_exit` (buys untouched; `DEFAULT_CONV_TOL` 0.005); `fixtures.py` `make_competitor_fills_fixture` (aligned to `make_historical_fixture` slugs/books/res_map); `competitors/resolve.py` (nagi777 added + `main` now MERGES handles.json, never overwrites); `competitors/__main__.py` (`manuals`/`manuals_report` stages); `verify.py` (`check_manuals_offline` + `check_clones_offline` + a `force_tier` case). **(A) Manuals** = distributions (percentiles + sparkline histograms), NOT averages: entry timing (maker/taker), price-vs-book at fill (at-touch/inside/crossing, half-tick tol via `price_class`, **tape-required** — gates on real Telonex book presence), sequencing (simultaneous vs alternating), inventory trajectory, size ramping, merge timing, per-window capital. **nagi merge-velocity** (`merge_velocity`): frequency, inventory-state-at-merge (matched ≤0.1 skew vs skewed), timing, capital recycled/merge, **turns/day** (gross BUY notional ÷ peak concurrent open capital, MERGE frees matched cost early) vs a hold baseline (`capital_velocity_hold`, on EVERY manual, no early frees). **(F) Provenance:** manual BODY = FACT+CALC only (lint: no `est`/`unv` span in `manuals_report.manual_body`); EST/UNV → Inference section; **anchor** reconciles the OUR-4-series reconstruction vs the ACCOUNT-WIDE official P/L (never assert equality — only a contradiction, i.e. reconstructed-positive-net exceeding a non-positive/smaller official slice, flags red "OUR RECONSTRUCTION SUSPECT"); 10-window hand-trace appendix (fills next to the as-of book, re-derivable by eye; unestablishable → "unknown"). **(B) Clones:** taker-accumulation (takerner/bonereaper — two-sided toward equal inventory, per-fill mark-to-resolution) + maker-quote (0xb27b/wolf9478/nagi777 — resting two-sided BUY limits). **(E) Maker-fill bracket** = both models on the SAME sell-aggressor-print stream so pessimistic (behind all displayed size) ≤ optimistic (front-of-queue), "truth is between; only live measures it"; taker clones cross (no bracket). **(C) Tier honesty:** each clone's trades.csv run through `rebate_sim` at Bronze AND owner tier (Platinum/Obsidian/Silver from `handles.json`) side by side — makers pay ~$0 fee so tier is immaterial for them (stated, not a bug). **(D) Momentum-exit:** enter on the momentum trigger, exit by selling at bid on PM-mid-converges-to-fair or after `T∈{15,30,60}`s, fees both ways, vs hold-to-resolution. Controls: shuffled-outcome (crc32-salted) + matched-frequency random (random side/limit) + `forensic_audit.capital_accounting`. **Design decisions (mine):** half-tick tol (0.005/0.0005); momentum-exit a pluggable clone; caps `[1000,5000,20000]`; our tier = Bronze; merge = $1 immediate (== hold PnL, so clone net marks matched pairs to resolution — the recycle advantage lives in the manual's velocity); manual distributions use the account's full traded range (fills-only), only tape-required metrics gate on coverage; clone money = post-Jun-5. **Load-fills filter relaxed (real-data fix):** `load_our_activity` now keeps ALL OUR-series fills in `[since,until)` (was hard-gated to Telonex coverage, which zeroed nagi) — fills-only behaviors see everything, `price_vs_book`/hand-trace report `book_missing` where no tape exists; `telonex_only=True` restores the memory bound for ultra-active accounts. **nagi777 FETCHED + resolved** (`0xbf33…6bf7`, high conf, merged into handles.json): a bounded `--max-records 60000` pull got only its most-recent 3 days (2026-07-12→14, all **btc-updown-5m** — a pure BTC-5m merge-heavy trader), which is **PAST Telonex book coverage (ends 2026-07-07)** → the real manual (relaxed filter) reconstructs **54,502 fills / 721 windows / 7,724 merges (10.7/window) / ~70 capital turns/day / $340k recycled / matched-at-merge 31%**, with `price_vs_book` correctly 0-with-book/721-missing (honestly flagged). **The tape-required + clone-backtest deliverables for nagi need a DEEPER fetch reaching ≤2026-07-07** (`fetch --only nagi777 --refresh-pnl` without/with a large `--max-records`; nagi does ~21k records/day so ~a week back = ~150k records) — an overnight operator step; the bounded fetch proved the whole resolve→fetch→manual path on real data. **Verification (this box):** `python -m model_lab.verify` **PASS** (banner now `… + manuals + clones + shadow-parity`); new tests green — `test_manuals` 13 (classifier/distribution units + fixture integration: counts, provenance lint, anchor consistent-vs-red, hand-trace), `test_backtest_clones` 8 (bracket ordering, `_TRADE_COLS`+fee/cost/net identities, tier honesty, momentum-exit sweep, owner ladder, determinism), `test_rebate_sim` +4 force_tier, `test_money_judge` +5 sell/exit; `test_dataset`/`test_short_horizon`/`test_stream_journal`/`test_maker_core`/`test_feature_set` 73 + `test_backtest_momentum`/`pair_lean`/`forensic` 24 + `test_smoke` 3 all pass (no regression from the additive `fixtures`/`money_judge`/`rebate_sim` changes). Full-suite-to-completion blocked only by the box terminating detached runs mid-way (0 failures in what ran). **Deferred:** the deep nagi fetch + its real manual/clone numbers; the real heavy runs for the 4 cached accounts (`competitors manuals` + `manuals_report` + `backtest_clones --clone all`) are operator/overnight (Telonex tape is multi-GB; b27b activity 2 GB — use `--telonex-only` there); the parent's `final_bakeoff.py` roster assembly consuming the clone trades.csv. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-14 — **Competitor manuals + clones RAN on real data (operator ran the pipeline).** Added two resumability/tractability fixes discovered live and RE-verified: (1) `manuals` `--max-book-windows N` — the tape-required `price_vs_book` bounds its per-window Telonex `.zst` reads to an evenly-strided N-window sample (reported `windows_sampled/windows_total`), else b27b's ~11k windows blow past any time budget (fills-only metrics still use all); (2) `backtest_clones` now **checkpoints per-(series,day)** (mirrors `backtest_pair_lean`: `_process_clone_day` + `ckpt_dir` + atomic resume) — one clone over the full current regime is ~90 s/(series,day) ≈ far more than any single run, so per-day checkpoints make `--clone all` resumable across kills. Also fixed b27b's handle everywhere to its **full address** `0xb27bc932bf8110d8f78e55da7d5f0497a18b5b82` (its handle in `handles.json`, not the short `0xb27b` roster/clone key) — `--only 0xb27b` had silently matched nothing. Tests still green (`test_backtest_clones` 8, `test_manuals` 13, `test_rebate_sim` 17); `verify` PASS. **Real run:** nagi777 resolved `0xbf33…6bf7` + deep-fetched to 618k records / cursor 2026-06-06 (`activity.jsonl` 655 MB — note a `--max-records` run marks `activity.state.json` `complete=true` which short-circuits a later resume; reset `complete=false` to go deeper); 5 manuals built `--telonex-only --max-book-windows 1500`; 5 clones over **BTC-5m 2026-07-01→07-07 (1,780 windows)** — a bounded slice (full 4-series/full-regime clone run = ~16 h in this env, an overnight/VPS job). **Manual findings:** every tape-covered account rests ~54–62% at-touch / ~37–45% crossing (more passive than their taker labels imply); nagi777 is a massive merge-recycler (31,112 merges/11 days = 2,828/day, 11/window, $1.31M recycled, ~65 capital turns/day vs takerner's 29.5 hold-velocity); **wolf9478's anchor flagged SUSPECT (red)** — OUR-series reconstruction $107k vs account-wide official only $14k (the anchor rule catching an implausible reconstruction); takerner/bonereaper show OUR-series losses vs positive account-wide official (profit is elsewhere/rebate — consistent). **Clone verdict — all LOSE at our tier over the window:** taker clones small-negative + control-VOID (takerner −$535, bonereaper −$880); maker clones lose big to adverse selection even at the optimistic bracket bound (b27b/nagi −$7,310 [bracket −$7,310..−$4,128], wolf −$4,648 [−$4,648..−$2,670]); tier honesty helps the takers marginally (Bronze $16 vs owner Platinum/Obsidian $176/$276 rebate) and is $0 for makers (no taker fee); the **only positive strategy on the window is the engine's momentum trigger — hold-to-resolution +$1,248, early-exit T15/T30/T60 +$1,033/$1,027/$886 (hold BEATS early exit)**. Consistent with every prior finding: momentum is the only edge, makers get adversely selected. Outputs: `out/competitors/manuals/*.{json,html}` (5), `out/backtests/clone_*/{trades.csv,metrics.json,report.html}` (5). — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-15 — **Anchor-rule asymmetry bug fixed (user-caught) + consolidated `final_report`.** The F anchor rule flagged wolf9478 (recon $107k ≫ official $14k) but RATIONALIZED the opposite-direction contradictions (takerner −$37k vs +$38k official; bonereaper −$230k vs +$242k) as "profit is elsewhere" — invalid, since both accounts are ~100% OUR-series (only TRADE+REDEEM records, all OUR slugs) so other-markets can't explain a sign-flipped gap. **Diagnosis (hand-trace + method compare):** the reconstruction is a CORRECT event-cash-flow ledger (`_reconstructed_net`): redeems ARE credited (redeemed windows reconcile to $0 gap), and a mark-held-to-resolution alternative (method B) is WORSE (−$258k for takerner, because `res_map` has 727 unresolved windows whose buys stay but settlement drops) — so method A is the more complete recon, and takerner/bonereaper genuinely show ~break-even-minus-fees OUR-series cash-flow that CANNOT be reconciled with their positive official P/L. **Fix:** `manuals.recon_contradicts_official(recon, official)` — symmetric (gap > `max($2k, 0.5·max|·|)` OR opposite-sign both-material, `SUSPECT_ABS_TOL`/`SUSPECT_FRAC_TOL`/`SUSPECT_SIGN_MATERIAL`), factual message, NO narrative — used in `anchor_reconcile` (full-window) + `_slice_ladder` (adds `slice_ladder.suspect`); `manuals.assert_anchor_consistent()` raises `AnchorInconsistency` if any contradiction reaches a report unflagged, wired into `final_report._load` (fails loud). Existing 5 manual JSONs patched post-hoc (rule is a pure fn of the already-correct recon/official values — no re-stream) + re-rendered. **Corrected ladder:** takerner/bonereaper/wolf9478/nagi777 slice-cells SUSPECT (red; gaps shown "unreliable"); **0xb27b is the ONE reliable recon** — its full-window reconciles ($79,638 ≈ official $79,595). **Behavioral distributions confirmed UNAFFECTED** (computed from raw fills, independent of the anchor). New `competitors/final_report.py` stage consolidates the 5 manuals + 5 clones → `out/competitors/final_report.{html,md}` (registered in `__main__`). Full 4-series clone run also completed (operator ran it): all 5 clones over 24,697 current-regime windows LOSE — takerner −$8,942 / bonereaper −$10,257 (takers), wolf9478 −$83,686 [−$83,686..−$49,218] / 0xb27b & nagi777 −$140,787 [−$140,787..−$85,746] (makers, adverse selection even at the optimistic bracket bound); the momentum trigger held-to-resolution +$5,057 (== the vps255 reference, validating the reconstruction) beats every early exit (T15 −$237, T30 +$1,649, T60 +$1,826). Tests: `recon_contradicts_official` symmetric + `assert_anchor_consistent` fail-loud (test_manuals 15); fixture official curves redesigned (consistent ≈ recon, inconsistent = large gap); `verify` PASS; `backtest_clones` now per-day-checkpointed (resumable) + `--max-book-windows` bounds b27b's tape reads. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-15 — **Per-window engine refactor + six-series maker gate (Phase 0 of the 2-week paper-eval launch).** Fixed the documented single-active-slot bug (`out/audits/btc_quoting_gap.md`): the maker `QuoteManager`, momentum `taker`, and `late_window` taker each ran ONE global `active: Option<…>` slot, so at every shared boundary the last-opened series (`Series::ALL` = BTC-before-ETH) clobbered the slot and 5 of 6 series placed ~0 resting quotes. All three are now `HashMap<WindowId, State>` mirroring the already-per-window `model_taker` (per-window `market`/`view`/`books`/budget/cooldown/attribution; `on_window` inserts on Open, removes+prunes on Closed/Resolved; events routed by window; `on_requote_tick` iterates all windows collect-ids-then-`get`; per-window `order_window: HashMap<OrderId,WindowId>` for fill routing; momentum's `signals: HashMap<Asset,SignalWindow>` stays shared). **Strategy math byte-identical (plumbing only)** — `quoting.rs`/`taker/edge.rs`/`signal.rs`/`late_window/edge.rs`/`model_taker/edge.rs`/`plan.rs` untouched (verified by `git diff --name-only`). **Self-match window-aware:** new `RestingLookup` trait (`quote_manager/view.rs`, exported from `mod.rs`) with `resting_view_for(window)`; each taker's `resting: Option<&RestingView>` → `Option<&dyn RestingLookup>` and resolves the maker's view FOR the fired window before the unchanged `filter_asks`; `RiskManager::forward_bus_event`/`on_model_prediction` bind `let quoter: &dyn RestingLookup = &self.quoter;` as a local (disjoint from `&mut momentum/late/model/arbiter`). **cancel semantics (the #1 correctness risk):** quoter `Closing` now issues `port.cancel_market(condition_id)` for THAT window only (was global `cancel_all`), while the risk-veto `cancel_all` + `CancelAction::All` stay global — proven by `closing_one_window_leaves_siblings_resting` (close BTC-5m → BTC-5m withdrawn, ETH-5m still resting). **FairVsMid per-window** (`risk/core.rs`): replaced the singleton `last_p_up`/`last_mid`/`sanity_breach_since` with `sanity: HashMap<WindowId, WindowSanity>` folded from `Event::Model` (by `snap.window`) + the Up-token mid (token→window); `recompute_sanity` trips the GLOBAL `FairVsMid` iff ANY window is over the dwell and clears only when NONE is (a healthy sibling never clears a diverged one — semantics unchanged, still a global OPERATIONAL halt, never shadowed); `prune_window_sanity` on close. `RiskStateSnapshot`/`resting_view()`→`resting_view_for(window)`+`total_resting_len()` updated. **Tests:** new `crates/bot/tests/per_window_maker_paper.rs` — `all_coincident_series_quote_concurrently` (arms all six in `Series::ALL` order, asserts every series has resting orders AND `btc_opens == eth_opens`) + `closing_one_window_leaves_siblings_resting`; per-window independence + cross-window self-match (`a_resting_quote_on_another_window_does_not_shield_a_take`) unit tests in `taker`/`late_window`; `second_window_divergence_trips_and_healthy_sibling_does_not_clear` in `risk/core`. **Verified (this Windows box):** `cargo test -p engine` **228 passed**; `cargo clippy -p engine -p bot --all-targets -D warnings` clean; bot integration green — `per_window_maker_paper` 2, `quote_manager_paper` 5, `risk_manager_paper` 6, `momentum_taker_paper` 2, `late_window_taker_paper` 2, `venue_parity` 13, `shadow_order_flow` 1, `inventory_replay` 1, `chaos --features chaos` 9. `model_taker_paper` stays blocked by Windows App Control (`os error 4551`) on this box (its logic is covered by the engine unit tests; the VPS Linux build is the definitive full-suite gate at cutover). Implemented by a subagent to spec + independently reviewed (a fabricated audit-doc edit it slipped in was reverted). Branch `per-window-refactor-20260715` (base `origin/engine-quote-manager-20260715`); push to both remotes + VPS cutover pending Phase 1 (rustls harness fix) + Phase 2.0 (shadow loss-stops, 60-share clip cap, cumulative maker deployment budget) landing on the same branch. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)

- 2026-07-15 — **Phase 2.0 sizing mechanics + shadow loss-stops (eval launch, same branch as the per-window refactor).** Four ADDITIVE features, all OFF/no-op by default so every pre-existing test passes byte-for-byte. **(A) Shadow loss-stops** (`risk.shadow_loss_stops`, default `false`): when true, the two P&L-based stops (`DailyStop`, per-window `WindowLoss`) are computed exactly as today but, instead of halting/cancelling, emit `RiskOutput::ShadowStop(ShadowStop{kind, window, threshold, value, ts})` and let trading continue — `check_window_loss` records once per window via `shadow_window_latched` (no `halted_windows` insert, no `CancelMarket`), `apply_settlement` records once per UTC day via `daily_stop_latched` (no `trip_global`), the day-roll re-arms both. Operational breakers (FeedStale/WsDisconnect/ClockSkew/FairVsMid/EngineRestart/ErrorRate/Manual) go through `trip_global` directly and are UNTOUCHED (still hard, still cancel-all mesh) — proven by `operational_breakers_still_hard_under_shadow`. `RiskCore.shadow_tripped: Vec<ShadowStop>` (pruned on close) → `RiskStateSnapshot.shadow_tripped` for the STATUS strip; `RiskManager` warn-logs (`target:"risk"`) + buffers into `take_shadow_stops()`; `bot run` records them to a `data/shadow-stops/shadow-stops-{YYYYMMDD}.jsonl.gz` side-channel (`crates/bot/src/shadow_stops_record.rs`, mirrors `model_taker_record.rs`). **(B) 60-share clip cap** (`NormalizerParams.clip_size_shares: Option<Decimal>`, default `None`=no clip): `normalize` clips any `OrderQty::Shares > cap` down + flags `Adjustments.size_clipped`; config `engine.defaults.clip_size_shares` (default `0`=disabled), validated so `touch_size`/`ladder_size_per_level ≤ clip` when set. **(C) Taker clip-splitting**: momentum/late/model `fire` split the planned FAK into SEQUENTIAL `≤ clip` clips (the burst 3×20 pattern) via a shared `split_take_clips` helper; `None`/single-clip ⇒ byte-identical single FAK; strategy math (`edge.rs`/`signal.rs`/`plan.rs`/`quoting.rs`) UNCHANGED (verified). **(D) Maker per-window cumulative deployment budget** (`QuoteManagerParams.maker_deployment_budget_per_window: Dollars`, default `ZERO`=unbounded; config `engine.defaults.maker_deployment_budget_per_window`): `ActiveWindow.deployed_notional` accrues **maker-only** gross-buy notional — attributed from the quoter's OWN order-update fill deltas (`RestingView.filled_of(id)` is `Some` only for our orders; the risk manager forwards every strategy's fills to every strategy, so a taker BUY on the window never consumes the maker budget — the initial agent draft counted every `Side::Buy` fill; I corrected it to the order-delta/own-orders attribution and rewrote `maker_deployment_budget_gates_new_placements` to fill real placed orders); `on_requote_tick` clears `plan.places` (cancels still flow) once `deployed_notional ≥ budget`. **`clip_size_shares`/`maker_deployment_budget_per_window` are `STRUCTURAL_PARAM_KEYS`** (restart-only), set per-phase by the eval config. **Verified (this box):** `cargo test -p engine` **236** (+8: shadow ×3, clip ×5-ish, budget, +existing); `cargo test -p config --lib` **87** (anti-drift `committed_default_toml_matches_code_defaults` + new validation); bot paper green — `per_window_maker_paper` 3, `quote_manager_paper` 5, `risk_manager_paper` 6, `momentum_taker_paper` 3, `late_window_taker_paper` 2, `venue_parity` 13, `shadow_order_flow` 1, `chaos --features chaos` 9; `cargo clippy -p engine -p config -p bot --all-targets -D warnings` clean. `model_taker_paper` WDAC-blocked on this box (Linux/VPS confirms). Implemented by a subagent to spec + independently reviewed — reverted two out-of-scope edits it slipped in (`out/audits/vps_runbook.md`, `model-lab/.../final_report.py`) and fixed the maker-budget attribution bug. Config VALUES (clip 60, budget $150→$1000, wcl/caps, shadow on) go in the VPS `bot.local.toml` per the §2.7 sizing table after operator sign-off. — Claude Code (Opus 4.8)
